# GHOST WIRE PR 02a Auth API Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use subagent-driven-development (recommended) or executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Close the PR 02 entry gates, then ship wallet-signature sign-in with revocable server-side sessions and multi-wallet management over ten `/api/v1` routes.

**Architecture:** Three modules gain behaviour: `wallet` (base58, Ed25519, the sign-in message, the `wallets` repository), `security` (tokens, Origin check, client IP, body reader, MongoDB rate limiting, `security_events`) and a new `auth` module (challenges, sign-in, link and unlink, sessions, route wrappers). `auth` depends only on `wallet`, `security`, `database`, `config` and `shared`. Route files under `src/app/api/v1/` are thin closures built by `withOrigin`/`withSession`. Every variable filter value goes through `safeEquals`, every unique index exists before the first write (`ensureAuthReady`), and every failure maps to the spec §7 error contract.

**Tech Stack:** Next.js 16.4.5 (App Router route handlers), TypeScript 6.0.3, Zod 4.6.7, mongodb driver 7.8.0, `node:crypto` Ed25519/SHA-256, `bs58` 6.0.0 (new), Vitest 5.1.0 with `mongodb-memory-server-core` 11.3.0 (MongoDB 7.0.12 replica set), ESLint 9.39.5, Prettier 3.9.10. Bun 1.4.4 runs scripts; Node 24 is the runtime.

**Source documents (read before starting any task):**
- Spec: `docs/specs/2026-11-13-auth-api-design.md` (scope, API, data model, flows, error contract, acceptance criteria).
- Architecture: `docs/specs/2026-11-13-auth-api-architecture.md` (signatures, data model, failure strategy, test harness). §7 records three user decisions: `safeEquals` is required for variable values only; the link routes get the same rate limits as sign-in; `bs58` 6.0.0 is approved.
- Every code block below was compiled, linted, formatted and run against this repository's PR 01 code before the plan was written. Copy it exactly.

## Global Constraints

**Toolchain and commands (lessons from executing PR 01)**
- First command in a fresh checkout: `bun install --frozen-lockfile` (the main checkout has no `node_modules`). Task 1 Step 1 does this.
- Run tests only with `bun run test` (Vitest). Never `bun test`, which starts Bun's own runner.
- Every test command is prefixed with `MONGOMS_SYSTEM_BINARY=/usr/bin/mongod` (the host's MongoDB 7.0.12). Integration tests start their own in-memory replica set.
- An unrelated developer `mongod` listens on `localhost:27017`. No test, helper or fixture may ever connect to it. Unit tests that need a syntactically valid URI use `mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0` (port 1: nothing listens). The unit project never reaches a database: `tests/unit/instrumentation.test.ts` mocks `ensureIndexesOnce`.
- `bun run format` and `bun run format:check` exit 126 on this host (bun installs prettier's bin with mode 0644). Use `node node_modules/prettier/bin/prettier.cjs --check <files>` (and `--write <files>` to fix). Every task leaves its own files Prettier-clean; the plan's code is already formatted. `docs/` is in `.prettierignore`; `README.md` is not.
- `bun run typecheck` runs `next typegen` first. It also type-checks the route exports against Next's generated route validator.
- Lint, typecheck and the full test suite pass at the end of every task.
- `git add` only the files a task lists. Never `git add -A` or `git add .`: `.agenkit/`, `.claude/`, `AGENTS.md` and `agenkit.lock.json` are untracked and are not this plan's to commit.
- Each task ends with exactly one commit. Its final `-m` is the Co-Authored-By trailer your harness attribution specifies, naming the model that actually authored the commit. Never copy a model name from this plan or from an older commit.
- Route tests print JSON `request failed` lines on stderr (handled errors are logged at `error`). That is expected; only the Vitest summary decides pass or fail.

**Language and library rules**
- Zod 4.6.7: never give a branded schema an explicit `z.ZodType<…>` annotation (it fails tsc). Use an inferred `.brand()` with `z.output`, or a TypeScript brand cast as `SolanaAddress` does. `objectIdSchema` is an inferred `z.custom<ObjectId>(…)`.
- Next 16.4.5 swallows errors thrown from `register()`. Boot failures log and call `process.exit(1)` inside `src/instrumentation-node.ts` (`failFastOnInvalidConfig`, `prepareDatabase`).
- ESLint prefixes `no-restricted-imports` messages with its own text. Boundary tests assert the exact set of `(line, ruleId)` pairs plus `message.endsWith(configuredMessage)`.
- When a task changes an existing `index.ts`, the plan shows the complete new file and keeps every existing export. Every other changed file is also shown complete.
- Exact pins only (`bunfig.toml` `exact = true`). The only new package is `bs58` `6.0.0` (transitive `base-x` `5.0.1` through `bun.lock`). Add it with `bun add bs58@6.0.0` (Task 6). No other dependency is added or changed.
- Only `src/modules/wallet/internal/base58.ts` imports `bs58` in `src/`; `tests/helpers/ed25519.ts` may import it to encode.

**Boundaries and conventions (PR 01, unchanged)**
- Outside a module, import only `@/modules/<name>`; within a module, `./x`; `internal/` is flat. `src/modules/**` never imports `src/app/**`. Only `database` imports `mongodb`/`bson`. Only `config` (plus `src/instrumentation.ts` and test setup/helpers) reads `process.env`. No `console.*` in `src/`.
- `auth` imports exactly `wallet`, `security`, `database`, `config` and `shared` (never `validation`). `wallet` and `security` never import `auth` or each other.
- Nothing runs `loadConfig()`, connects or registers indexes at module top level. Route files only build closures at import, so `next build` works on a fresh clone with no `.env`.
- Every variable value in a MongoDB filter goes through `safeEquals(field, value)`; server constants (`consumedAt: null`, `revokedAt: null`, `status: "active" as const`, `expiresAt: { $gt: at }`) are written literally next to it (architecture §7 item 1).
- Every database-touching auth service function awaits `ensureAuthReady()` before its first database operation, including the rate-limit upsert.
- `recordSecurityEvent` is awaited, never rejects, and is never called inside a `withTransaction` `fn`.
- Auth log fields use `uid`, `wid`, `sid`, `cid` for ObjectId hex. `session`, `nonce`, `token`, `signature`, `cookie`, `key` and `secret` are redacted key parts, so never use them as log field names for ids.

**Values copied from the spec (one line each)**
- Redaction key parts added: `passwd`, `pwd`, `passphrase`, `credential`, `bearer`, `jwt`, `session`, `csrf`, `nonce` (`auth` is deliberately excluded).
- Free-text scrubbing: `Bearer <token>` / `Basic <token>` → `Bearer [REDACTED]` / `Basic [REDACTED]`, case-insensitive; `token`, `access_token`, `api-key`, `api_key`, `apikey`, `secret`, `password`, `session`, `nonce`, `signature` `=value` → `name=[REDACTED]`, ending at `&`, whitespace, a quote or end of string.
- `safeEquals` accepts string, number, boolean, bigint, ObjectId and Date only; anything else throws `ValidationError("FILTER_INVALID")`.
- `typedCollection` reads, updates and counts pass `maxTimeMS: 5000`.
- `toErrorResponse` returns `{ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" }` for every status ≥ 500 except `DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE` (503 + `Retry-After: 5`); the original code is logged.
- `noStoreJson` serializes `bigint` as a decimal string.
- New errors: `UnauthenticatedError` 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`; `AuthFailedError` 401 `AUTH_FAILED`; `ForbiddenOriginError` 403 `ORIGIN_REJECTED`; `RateLimitedError` 429 `RATE_LIMITED` with `retryAfterSeconds` sent as `Retry-After`.
- Config: `APP_ORIGIN` required, `https://` or `http://` origin with no path; `SOLANA_NETWORKS` default `devnet,mainnet-beta`; `TRUST_PROXY` default `false`; `SESSION_IDLE_DAYS` 1–30 default `7`; `SESSION_MAX_DAYS` ≥ idle, ≤ 90, default `30`; `RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN` 1–1000 default `10`; `RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS` 1–1000 default `20`; `RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN` 1–1000 default `10`. Config error messages name keys only.
- Sign-in message: spec §5.1, exactly 11 lines, `\n` endings, no trailing newline; nonce 16 random bytes base64url; timestamps ISO-8601 UTC with milliseconds; expiry 5 minutes after issue.
- Session token: 32 random bytes base64url; only `sha256(token)` stored. Cookie: `__Host-gw_session=<token>; Path=/; Max-Age=<s>; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax` for https, `gw_session=…` without `Secure` for http. Sliding writes at most once per hour.
- Rate limits: challenge 10/min per IP and 20/hour per address; verify 10/min per IP; the link routes use the same limits under `link_*` keys.
- JSON bodies over 4096 bytes → 400 `VALIDATION_FAILED`.
- Never stored: private keys, seed phrases, raw session tokens, raw nonces (as a field), signatures, IP addresses, email. Never logged: token, token hash, nonce, signature, full message, IP, raw body.
- No field, code or message in this API asserts privacy capability; every wallet and session response states its `network`.

### Task 1: Entry gates 1–2: redaction key names and free-text credential scrubbing

Spec §3.1 items 1–2. Adds nine sensitive key parts and two new free-text scrubbing passes (`Bearer`/`Basic` values and credential query parameters) after the existing URI-userinfo pass, and extends the residual-risk comment.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/shared/redact.test.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/shared/internal/redact.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/shared/redact.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `redact(value: unknown): unknown` and `REDACTED` from `@/modules/shared` (unchanged signatures). `scrubString(value: string): string` stays internal to `shared` (the logger uses it for `msg`).
- Produces: the same signatures with new behaviour. `SENSITIVE_KEY_PARTS` gains `passwd`, `pwd`, `passphrase`, `credential`, `bearer`, `jwt`, `session`, `csrf`, `nonce`. `scrubString` runs three passes in order: URI userinfo; `/\b(Bearer|Basic)\s+[^\s"'<>,;]+/gi` → `$1 [REDACTED]`; `/\b(token|access_token|api-key|api_key|apikey|secret|password|session|nonce|signature)=[^&\s"']*/gi` → `$1=[REDACTED]`. Side effect relied on later: log fields named `sessionId` or `nonceHash` are redacted, so auth logs use `uid`/`wid`/`sid`/`cid`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { REDACTED, redact } from "@/modules/shared";

describe("redact: PR 02 sensitive key names (entry gate 1)", () => {
  it.each([
    "passwd",
    "dbPwd",
    "passphrase",
    "credentials",
    "bearerValue",
    "jwt",
    "sessionToken",
    "sessionId",
    "csrf",
    "nonce",
    "nonceHash",
  ])("redacts the value under %s", (key) => {
    expect(redact({ [key]: "SENTINEL-VALUE" })).toEqual({ [key]: REDACTED });
  });

  it("matches the new key parts case-insensitively", () => {
    expect(redact({ PassPhrase: "x", JWT: "y", CSRF_HEADER: "z" })).toEqual({
      PassPhrase: REDACTED,
      JWT: REDACTED,
      CSRF_HEADER: REDACTED,
    });
  });

  it("does not redact author (auth is deliberately not a key part)", () => {
    expect(redact({ author: "ana", authorName: "Ana B" })).toEqual({
      author: "ana",
      authorName: "Ana B",
    });
  });

  it("keeps the opaque id field names used by auth logs", () => {
    expect(redact({ uid: "u1", wid: "w1", sid: "s1", cid: "c1", reason: "r" })).toEqual({
      uid: "u1",
      wid: "w1",
      sid: "s1",
      cid: "c1",
      reason: "r",
    });
  });
});

describe("redact: Authorization-style values in free text (entry gate 2)", () => {
  it.each([
    ["Authorization: Bearer abc.def-ghi_jkl", "Authorization: Bearer [REDACTED]"],
    ["authorization: bearer abc", "authorization: bearer [REDACTED]"],
    ["BEARER xyz end", "BEARER [REDACTED] end"],
    ["Basic dXNlcjpwYXNz", "Basic [REDACTED]"],
    ["header basic dXNlcjpwYXNz; next", "header basic [REDACTED]; next"],
    ['{"h":"Bearer tok"}', '{"h":"Bearer [REDACTED]"}'],
  ])("scrubs %j", (input, expected) => {
    expect(redact(input)).toBe(expected);
  });
});

describe("redact: credential parameters in free text (entry gate 2)", () => {
  const NAMES = [
    "token",
    "access_token",
    "api-key",
    "api_key",
    "apikey",
    "secret",
    "password",
    "session",
    "nonce",
    "signature",
  ] as const;

  it.each(NAMES)("scrubs %s= terminated by &", (name) => {
    expect(redact(`https://h.example/cb?${name}=SENTINEL&x=1`)).toBe(
      `https://h.example/cb?${name}=[REDACTED]&x=1`,
    );
  });

  it.each(NAMES)("scrubs %s= terminated by whitespace", (name) => {
    expect(redact(`${name}=SENTINEL next`)).toBe(`${name}=[REDACTED] next`);
  });

  it.each(NAMES)("scrubs %s= terminated by a double quote", (name) => {
    expect(redact(`"${name}=SENTINEL"`)).toBe(`"${name}=[REDACTED]"`);
  });

  it.each(NAMES)("scrubs %s= terminated by a single quote", (name) => {
    expect(redact(`'${name}=SENTINEL'`)).toBe(`'${name}=[REDACTED]'`);
  });

  it.each(NAMES)("scrubs %s= at the end of the string", (name) => {
    expect(redact(`${name}=SENTINEL`)).toBe(`${name}=[REDACTED]`);
  });

  it("matches parameter names case-insensitively and keeps the original spelling", () => {
    expect(redact("API_KEY=abc&Nonce=def")).toBe("API_KEY=[REDACTED]&Nonce=[REDACTED]");
  });

  it("leaves other parameters alone", () => {
    expect(redact("page=2&sort=asc")).toBe("page=2&sort=asc");
  });

  it("still scrubs URI userinfo, and combines with parameter scrubbing", () => {
    expect(redact("mongodb://u:p@h/db?password=x")).toBe(
      "mongodb://[REDACTED]@h/db?password=[REDACTED]",
    );
  });

  it("scrubs error messages and stacks too", () => {
    const out = redact({ err: new Error("callback failed token=SENTINEL") }) as {
      err: { message: string; stack: string };
    };
    expect(out.err.message).toBe("callback failed token=[REDACTED]");
    expect(out.err.stack).not.toContain("SENTINEL");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the implementation**

Replace the whole of `src/modules/shared/internal/redact.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
export const REDACTED = "[REDACTED]" as const;

const SENSITIVE_KEY_PARTS = [
  "key",
  "seed",
  "secret",
  "token",
  "password",
  "mnemonic",
  "viewingkey",
  "privatekey",
  "signature",
  "authorization",
  "cookie",
  "passwd",
  "pwd",
  "passphrase",
  "credential",
  "bearer",
  "jwt",
  "session",
  "csrf",
  "nonce",
] as const;

/** A scheme plus the rest of its whitespace-delimited token. */
const URI_TOKEN = /([a-z][a-z0-9+.-]*:\/\/)(\S*)/gi;
/** `Bearer <token>` / `Basic <token>`, any case; the value ends at whitespace, a quote, <, >, a comma or a semicolon. */
const AUTH_SCHEME_VALUE = /\b(Bearer|Basic)\s+[^\s"'<>,;]+/gi;
/** `name=value` for credential-like parameter names; the value ends at &, whitespace or a quote. */
const SECRET_PARAM =
  /\b(token|access_token|api-key|api_key|apikey|secret|password|session|nonce|signature)=[^&\s"']*/gi;
const MAX_DEPTH = 8;
const CIRCULAR = "[Circular]";
const TRUNCATED = "[Truncated]";

function isSensitiveKey(key: string): boolean {
  const lower = key.toLowerCase();
  return SENSITIVE_KEY_PARTS.some((part) => lower.includes(part));
}

/**
 * Best-effort scrubbing of credentials embedded in free text. Three passes, in order:
 *
 * 1. URI userinfo: a credentialed URI becomes `scheme://[REDACTED]@host...`.
 *    Tokens are delimited by whitespace only, so quotes and angle brackets inside a password
 *    (`u:p'q@h`, `u:p"q@h`, `u:p<q>r@h`) cannot end the token early. Everything up to the LAST
 *    `@` in the same token counts as userinfo, so unencoded `@` or `/` in a password cannot leak.
 *    A token counts as credentialed when its first `@` comes before any `/` (`user@host`) or
 *    follows a `:` (`user:pass@host`). This keeps `https://example.com/a@b` intact.
 * 2. Authorization-style values: `Bearer <token>` and `Basic <token>` (any case) become
 *    `Bearer [REDACTED]` / `Basic [REDACTED]`.
 * 3. Credential parameters: `token`, `access_token`, `api-key`, `api_key`, `apikey`, `secret`,
 *    `password`, `session`, `nonce` and `signature` (any case) followed by `=` keep the name and
 *    lose the value up to `&`, whitespace, a quote or the end of the string.
 *
 * Accepted over-redaction (safe failure): a query-string `@`, comma-joined URIs, a
 * `host:port/path@x` token, everything before the last `@` in a token that also holds other
 * quoted JSON (for example `{"uri":"mongodb://u:p@h/x","o":"a@b"}`), and prose such as
 * "Basic plan" (the next word is redacted).
 * Accepted residual risks (not scrubbed):
 * - a password containing whitespace is not scrubbed past that space;
 * - a credential split across separate log fields or strings;
 * - a value containing whitespace after `Bearer`/`Basic` (only the first word is redacted);
 * - parameter names outside the list above (for example `sig=` or `x_api_key=`) and JSON-style
 *   `"nonce":"..."` pairs inside a string (sensitive KEYS are redacted by `redact`, not here).
 */
export function scrubString(value: string): string {
  return scrubUriUserinfo(value)
    .replace(AUTH_SCHEME_VALUE, `$1 ${REDACTED}`)
    .replace(SECRET_PARAM, `$1=${REDACTED}`);
}

function scrubUriUserinfo(value: string): string {
  return value.replace(URI_TOKEN, (match: string, scheme: string, rest: string) => {
    const lastAt = rest.lastIndexOf("@");
    if (lastAt === -1) return match;
    const firstAt = rest.indexOf("@");
    const firstSlash = rest.indexOf("/");
    const credentialed =
      rest.slice(0, firstAt).includes(":") || firstSlash === -1 || firstAt < firstSlash;
    if (!credentialed) return match;
    return `${scheme}${REDACTED}@${rest.slice(lastAt + 1)}`;
  });
}

/** Pure: returns a JSON-safe, redacted copy of `value`. Never mutates the input. */
export function redact(value: unknown): unknown {
  return walk(value, 0, new WeakSet<object>());
}

function walk(value: unknown, depth: number, ancestors: WeakSet<object>): unknown {
  if (typeof value === "string") return scrubString(value);
  if (typeof value === "bigint") return value.toString();
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return value;
  if (depth > MAX_DEPTH) return TRUNCATED;
  if (ancestors.has(value)) return CIRCULAR;

  ancestors.add(value);
  try {
    if (value instanceof Error) return walkError(value, depth, ancestors);
    if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.map((item) => walk(item, depth + 1, ancestors));
    if (value instanceof Map) {
      return walkEntries(
        Array.from(value.entries(), ([k, v]) => [String(k), v] as const),
        depth,
        ancestors,
      );
    }
    if (value instanceof Set) return Array.from(value, (item) => walk(item, depth + 1, ancestors));
    const toJSON = (value as { toJSON?: unknown }).toJSON;
    if (typeof toJSON === "function") return walk(toJSON.call(value), depth, ancestors);
    return walkEntries(Object.entries(value), depth, ancestors);
  } finally {
    ancestors.delete(value);
  }
}

function walkEntries(
  entries: readonly (readonly [string, unknown])[],
  depth: number,
  ancestors: WeakSet<object>,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  for (const [key, item] of entries) {
    out[key] = isSensitiveKey(key) ? REDACTED : walk(item, depth + 1, ancestors);
  }
  return out;
}

function walkError(
  error: Error,
  depth: number,
  ancestors: WeakSet<object>,
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const out: Record<string, unknown> = { name: error.name, message: scrubString(error.message) };
  const { code, errorLabels } = error as { code?: unknown; errorLabels?: unknown };
  if (code !== undefined) out.code = walk(code, depth + 1, ancestors);
  if (Array.isArray(errorLabels)) out.errorLabels = walk(errorLabels, depth + 1, ancestors);
  if (typeof error.stack === "string") out.stack = scrubString(error.stack);
  if (error.cause !== undefined) out.cause = walk(error.cause, depth + 1, ancestors);
  return out;
}
```

### Task 2: Entry gates 5–6: 5xx code collapsing and bigint JSON

Spec §3.1 items 5–6. `toErrorResponse` now logs `{ err, code }` and collapses every status ≥ 500 to `INTERNAL`, except `DependencyUnavailableError`. `noStoreJson` serializes `bigint` with a replacer. The existing `http.test.ts` is replaced: the `ConfigError` row now expects `INTERNAL`, and every row checks the logged original code.

**Files:**
- Modify: `tests/unit/shared/http.test.ts`, `src/modules/shared/internal/http.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/shared/http.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AppError`, `DependencyUnavailableError`, `isAppError` (`./errors`), `logger` (`./logger`).
- Produces (signatures unchanged, behaviour new):
  - `noStoreJson(body: unknown, init?: { status?: number; headers?: Record<string, string> }): Response`: `JSON.stringify(body, replacer)` where bigint → `value.toString()`; always `Cache-Control: no-store` and `Content-Type: application/json`.
  - `toErrorResponse(error: unknown): Response`: always `logger.error("request failed", { err: error, code })` with `code = isAppError(error) ? error.code : "INTERNAL"`; non-AppError → 500 INTERNAL; `DependencyUnavailableError` → its status and code + `Retry-After: 5`; any other `httpStatus >= 500` → 500 `{ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" }`; otherwise `{ code, message: safeMessage }` at `httpStatus`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  AppError,
  ConfigError,
  ConflictError,
  DependencyUnavailableError,
  InternalError,
  NotFoundError,
  ValidationError,

const SENTINEL = "SENTINEL-INTERNAL-DETAIL";

describe("noStoreJson", () => {
  it("serializes the body with no-store and JSON content type", async () => {
    const res = noStoreJson({ status: "ok" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(res.headers.get("content-type")).toBe("application/json");
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ status: "ok" });
  });

    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("5");
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
  });

  it("serializes bigint values as decimal strings, nested or top level (entry gate 6)", async () => {
    const res = noStoreJson({ amount: 18446744073709551616n, nested: { list: [1n, 2] } });
    expect(await res.text()).toBe('{"amount":"18446744073709551616","nested":{"list":["1",2]}}');
    expect(await noStoreJson(5n).text()).toBe('"5"');
  });
});

function silenceLoggerError() {
  return vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
}

describe("toErrorResponse", () => {
  it.each([
    {
      error: new ValidationError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 400,
      code: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
      message: "Invalid request",
      logged: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
    },
    {
      error: new NotFoundError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 404,
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
      message: "Not found",
      logged: "NOT_FOUND",
    },
    {
      error: new ConflictError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 409,
      code: "CONFLICT",
      message: "Conflict",
      logged: "CONFLICT",
    },
    {
      error: new DependencyUnavailableError("database", { message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 503,
      code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "Service temporarily unavailable",
      logged: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
    },
    {
      error: new InternalError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 500,
      code: "INTERNAL",
      message: "Internal error",
      logged: "INTERNAL",
    },
    {
      error: new ConfigError([{ key: SENTINEL, reason: "Required" }]),
      status: 500,
      code: "INTERNAL",
      message: "Internal error",
      logged: "CONFIG_INVALID",
    },
  ])(
    "maps $logged to $status $code with only the safe message",
    async ({ error, status, code, message, logged }) => {
      const logError = silenceLoggerError();

      expect(res.status).toBe(status);
      expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
      const text = await res.text();
      expect(JSON.parse(text)).toEqual({ code, message });
      expect(text).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
      expect(text).not.toContain("stack");
      expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request failed", { err: error, code: logged });
    },
  );

  it.each([
    { label: "DATA_INTEGRITY", error: new InternalError({ code: "DATA_INTEGRITY" }) },
    { label: "INDEX_CONFLICT", error: new InternalError({ code: "INDEX_CONFLICT" }) },
    {
      label: "a custom 502",
      error: new AppError({ code: "UPSTREAM_BROKEN", httpStatus: 502, safeMessage: "Bad gateway" }),
    },
  ])(
    "collapses $label to 500 INTERNAL and logs the original code (entry gate 5)",
    async ({ error }) => {
      const logError = silenceLoggerError();
      const res = toErrorResponse(error);
      expect(res.status).toBe(500);
      expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" });
      expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
      expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request failed", { err: error, code: error.code });
    },
  );

  it("keeps DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE as a 503 with Retry-After (not collapsed)", async () => {
    silenceLoggerError();
    const res = toErrorResponse(new DependencyUnavailableError("database"));
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("5");
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({
      code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "Service temporarily unavailable",
    });
  });

  it("adds Retry-After only for DependencyUnavailableError", () => {

    ).toBe("5");
    expect(toErrorResponse(new NotFoundError()).headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
    expect(toErrorResponse(new InternalError()).headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("maps unknown errors and non-Error throws to a generic 500", async () => {
    const logError = silenceLoggerError();
    for (const thrown of [new Error(SENTINEL), SENTINEL, { message: SENTINEL }]) {
      const res = toErrorResponse(thrown);
      expect(res.status).toBe(500);

      code: "INTERNAL",
    });
  });

  it("keeps cause out of the response", async () => {
    silenceLoggerError();
    const res = toErrorResponse(new InternalError({ cause: new Error(SENTINEL) }));
    expect(await res.text()).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Replace the whole of `src/modules/shared/internal/http.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { DependencyUnavailableError, isAppError } from "./errors";
import { logger } from "./logger";

const RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS = "5";
const INTERNAL_BODY = { code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" } as const;

/** JSON.stringify replacer: bigint becomes its decimal string (JSON has no bigint). */
function bigintAsString(_key: string, value: unknown): unknown {
  return typeof value === "bigint" ? value.toString() : value;
}

export function noStoreJson(
  body: unknown,
  init: { status?: number; headers?: Record<string, string> } = {},
): Response {
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
  headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body, bigintAsString), {
    status: init.status ?? 200,
    headers,
  });
}

/**
 * Logs the full error server-side (with its original code); the client only ever sees a stable
 * code and safe message. Every status >= 500 collapses to INTERNAL, except
 * DependencyUnavailableError, which keeps its 503 code and Retry-After.
 */
export function toErrorResponse(error: unknown): Response {
  const code = isAppError(error) ? error.code : "INTERNAL";
  logger.error("request failed", { err: error, code });
  if (!isAppError(error)) return noStoreJson(INTERNAL_BODY, { status: 500 });
  if (error instanceof DependencyUnavailableError) {
    return noStoreJson(
      { code: error.code, message: error.safeMessage },
      { status: error.httpStatus, headers: { "Retry-After": RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS } },
    );
  }
  if (error.httpStatus >= 500) return noStoreJson(INTERNAL_BODY, { status: 500 });
  return noStoreJson(
    { code: error.code, message: error.safeMessage },
    { status: error.httpStatus },
  );
}
```

### Task 3: Entry gates 3–4: safeEquals, ObjectId parsing, maxTimeMS and typed updates

Spec §3.1 items 3–4 and architecture §3.4. Adds the only permitted way to put a variable value into a filter, ObjectId helpers for the API boundary, `maxTimeMS: 5000` on every read, update and count, and the four typed operations the auth flows need (`findOneAndUpdate`, `updateOne`, `updateMany`, `countDocuments`). Update values are validated field by field against the collection schema.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/database/safe-equals.test.ts`, `tests/unit/database/parse-object-id.test.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/safe-filter.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/ids.ts`
- Modify: `tests/integration/database/typed-collection.test.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/typed-collection.ts`, `src/modules/database/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/database/safe-equals.test.ts`, `tests/unit/database/parse-object-id.test.ts`, `tests/integration/database/typed-collection.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `InternalError`, `ValidationError` (`@/modules/shared`); the existing `getDb`, `mapMongoError`, `assertCollectionName`.
- Produces from `@/modules/database` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3):
  - `type SafeScalar = string | number | boolean | bigint | Date | ObjectId`
  - `safeEquals<K extends string, V extends SafeScalar>(field: K, value: V): { readonly [P in K]: V }`. Throws `ValidationError({ code: "FILTER_INVALID" })` for a non-scalar (checked at runtime, so `unknown` input that bypassed typing is caught) and `InternalError({ code: "FILTER_FIELD_INVALID" })` for a field outside `/^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/`.
  - `parseObjectId(value: unknown): ObjectId | null` (24 hex, any case; never throws) and `objectIdSchema` (`z.custom<ObjectId>`, inferred type).
  - `QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS = 5000`.
  - `interface TypedUpdate<S extends z.ZodObject> { $set?: Partial<z.input<S>>; $setOnInsert?: Partial<z.input<S>>; $inc?: Partial<Record<keyof z.output<S> & string, number>> }` (readonly fields).
  - `TypedCollection<S>` gains `findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, options?: { session?; upsert?; returnDocument?: "before" | "after" })` → `Promise<WithId<z.output<S>> | null>` (default `"after"`); `updateOne(filter, update, options?: { session?; upsert? })` → `{ matchedCount, modifiedCount, upsertedId: ObjectId | null }`; `updateMany(filter, update, options?: { session? })` → `{ matchedCount, modifiedCount }`; `countDocuments(filter, options?: { session?; limit? })` → `number` (limit in [1, 1000], else `RangeError`). Unknown keys, failed field parses, non-integer `$inc` and an empty update throw `ValidationError("DOCUMENT_INVALID")` whose message names the key, never the value.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { parseObjectId, safeEquals } from "@/modules/database";
import { InternalError, ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";

function thrownBy(fn: () => unknown): unknown {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (error) {
    return error;
  }
  throw new Error("expected a throw");
}

const OID = parseObjectId("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718");

describe("safeEquals (entry gate 3)", () => {
  it("accepts the six scalar kinds and builds { [field]: value }", () => {
    if (OID === null) throw new Error("fixture ObjectId failed to parse");
    const at = new Date("2026-11-13T00:00:00.000Z");
    expect(safeEquals("a", "x")).toEqual({ a: "x" });
    expect(safeEquals("a", 0)).toEqual({ a: 0 });
    expect(safeEquals("a", false)).toEqual({ a: false });
    expect(safeEquals("a", 10n)).toEqual({ a: 10n });
    expect(safeEquals("a", at)).toEqual({ a: at });
    expect(safeEquals("_id", OID)).toEqual({ _id: OID });
    expect(safeEquals("nested.path_1", "x")).toEqual({ "nested.path_1": "x" });
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "{ $ne: 1 }", value: { $ne: 1 } },
    { label: '{ $gt: "" }', value: { $gt: "" } },
    { label: "[]", value: [] },
    { label: '["a"]', value: ["a"] },
    { label: "null", value: null },
    { label: "undefined", value: undefined },
    { label: "{}", value: {} },
    { label: "an invalid Date", value: new Date("not a date") },
    { label: "a function", value: () => "x" },
    { label: "a symbol", value: Symbol("x") },
    { label: "a RegExp", value: /x/ },
  ])("rejects $label with ValidationError FILTER_INVALID", ({ value }) => {
    const error = thrownBy(() => safeEquals("field", value as unknown as string));
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
    expect((error as ValidationError).code).toBe("FILTER_INVALID");
    expect((error as ValidationError).httpStatus).toBe(400);
  });

  it.each(["$where", "a.$gt", "", "a..b", ".a", "a b", "1a"])(
    "rejects the field name %j with InternalError FILTER_FIELD_INVALID",
    (field) => {
      const error = thrownBy(() => safeEquals(field, "x"));
      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(InternalError);
      expect((error as InternalError).code).toBe("FILTER_FIELD_INVALID");
    },
  );

  it("never echoes the rejected value in the error message", () => {
    const error = thrownBy(() =>
      safeEquals("tokenHash", { $ne: "SENTINEL" } as unknown as string),
    ) as ValidationError;
    expect(error.message).not.toContain("SENTINEL");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { objectIdSchema, parseObjectId } from "@/modules/database";

describe("parseObjectId", () => {
  it("parses 24 hex characters of either case to the same lowercase ObjectId", () => {
    const lower = parseObjectId("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718");
    const upper = parseObjectId("64B7F0C2A1B2C3D4E5F60718");
    expect(lower?.toHexString()).toBe("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718");
    expect(upper?.toHexString()).toBe("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718");
  });

  it.each([
    "64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6071",
    "64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f607180",
    "64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f6071g",
    " 64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718",
    "",
    123,
    null,
    undefined,
    { $ne: 1 },
    ["64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718"],
  ])("returns null for %j", (value) => {
    expect(parseObjectId(value)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("objectIdSchema", () => {
  it("accepts an ObjectId and rejects a hex string", () => {
    const id = parseObjectId("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718");
    expect(objectIdSchema.safeParse(id).success).toBe(true);
    expect(objectIdSchema.safeParse("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60718").success).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Replace the whole of `tests/integration/database/typed-collection.test.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { Collection } from "mongodb";
import { afterEach, beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, inject, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { z } from "zod";
import { getDb, safeEquals, typedCollection, withTransaction } from "@/modules/database";
import {
  ConflictError,
  createLogger,
  DependencyUnavailableError,
  InternalError,
  ValidationError,
} from "@/modules/shared";
import { startStandalone, useDatabaseUri } from "../../helpers/mongo";

const COLLECTION = "typed_probe";
const strictSchema = z.strictObject({ ref: z.string().min(1), amount: z.string() });
const counterSchema = z.strictObject({
  ref: z.string().min(1),
  count: z.number().int().min(1),
  note: z.string().max(8),
});

async function raw() {
  return (await getDb()).collection(COLLECTION);
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  await (await getDb()).createCollection(COLLECTION);
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  await (await raw()).deleteMany({});
});

afterEach(async () => {
  await useDatabaseUri(inject("mongoUri"));
});

describe("typedCollection", () => {
  it("rejects invalid documents with DOCUMENT_INVALID and writes nothing", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    const error: unknown = await probes
      .insertOne({ ref: "", amount: "1" })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
    expect((error as ValidationError).code).toBe("DOCUMENT_INVALID");
    expect((error as ValidationError).safeMessage).toBe("Invalid document");
    expect(await (await raw()).countDocuments({})).toBe(0);
  });

  it("names the collection and failing paths but not the values", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    const error = (await probes
      .insertOne({ ref: "SENTINEL-REF", amount: 5 as unknown as string })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e)) as ValidationError;
    expect(error.message).toContain(COLLECTION);
    expect(error.message).toContain("amount");
    expect(error.message).not.toContain("SENTINEL-REF");
  });

  it("rejects unknown keys with a strict schema", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    const doc = { ref: "a", amount: "1", extra: true };
    await expect(probes.insertOne(doc)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
  });

  it("inserts the parsed output and reads it back parsed", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    const inserted = await probes.insertOne({ ref: "a", amount: "100" });
    expect(inserted).toMatchObject({ ref: "a", amount: "100" });
    expect(inserted._id).toBeDefined();
    expect(await probes.findOne({ ref: "a" })).toEqual(inserted);
  });

  it("strips unknown keys with a non-strict schema", async () => {
    const loose = typedCollection(COLLECTION, z.object({ ref: z.string() }));
    const doc = { ref: "s", junk: "x" };
    await loose.insertOne(doc);
    const stored = await (await raw()).findOne({ ref: "s" });
    expect(stored).not.toHaveProperty("junk");
  });

  it("returns null from findOne when nothing matches", async () => {
    expect(await typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema).findOne({ ref: "missing" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("find is bounded, sorted and requires a limit in [1, 1000]", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    for (const ref of ["b", "a", "c"]) await probes.insertOne({ ref, amount: "1" });
    const found = await probes.find({}, { limit: 2, sort: { ref: 1 } });
    expect(found.map((d) => d.ref)).toEqual(["a", "b"]);
    for (const limit of [0, 1001, 1.5]) {
      await expect(probes.find({}, { limit })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(RangeError);
    }
  });

  it("reports stored documents that fail the schema as DATA_INTEGRITY", async () => {
    await (await raw()).insertOne({ ref: 5, amount: "1" });
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    for (const read of [() => probes.findOne({}), () => probes.find({}, { limit: 10 })]) {
      const error: unknown = await read().catch((e: unknown) => e);
      expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(InternalError);
      expect((error as InternalError).code).toBe("DATA_INTEGRITY");
    }
  });

  it("maps a duplicate key to ConflictError DUPLICATE_KEY", async () => {
    const db = await getDb();
    await db
      .collection("dup_probe")
      .createIndex({ ref: 1 }, { unique: true, name: "dup_ref_unique" });
    const dups = typedCollection("dup_probe", strictSchema);
    await dups.insertOne({ ref: "same", amount: "1" });
    const error: unknown = await dups
      .insertOne({ ref: "same", amount: "2" })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    expect((error as ConflictError).code).toBe("DUPLICATE_KEY");
    expect((error as ConflictError).safeMessage).toBe("Resource already exists");
  });

  it("never puts the duplicated value or driver text into safeMessage, code or message", async () => {
    const db = await getDb();
    await db
      .collection("dup_secret_probe")
      .createIndex({ ref: 1 }, { unique: true, name: "dup_secret_unique" });
    const dups = typedCollection("dup_secret_probe", strictSchema);
    const secret = "SENTINEL-DUP-VALUE-9f3a";
    await dups.insertOne({ ref: secret, amount: "1" });
    const error = (await dups
      .insertOne({ ref: secret, amount: "2" })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e)) as ConflictError;
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    for (const field of [error.safeMessage, error.code, error.message]) {
      expect(field).not.toContain(secret);
      expect(field).not.toContain("E11000");
      expect(field).not.toContain("dup_secret_unique");
    }
    const cause = error.cause as Error & { code?: number };
    expect(cause).toBeInstanceOf(Error);
    expect(cause.message).toBe("duplicate key");
    expect(cause.code).toBe(11000);
    for (const text of [cause.message, cause.stack ?? ""]) {
      expect(text).not.toContain(secret);
      expect(text).not.toContain("E11000");
    }
  });

  it("keeps the duplicated value out of a serialized log line", async () => {
    const db = await getDb();
    await db
      .collection("dup_log_probe")
      .createIndex({ ref: 1 }, { unique: true, name: "dup_log_unique" });
    const dups = typedCollection("dup_log_probe", strictSchema);
    const secret = "SENTINEL-LOG-VALUE-7c1d";
    await dups.insertOne({ ref: secret, amount: "1" });
    const err = await dups.insertOne({ ref: secret, amount: "2" }).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    const lines: string[] = [];
    const captured = createLogger({
      level: "debug",
      sink: { write: (_level, line) => void lines.push(line) },
    });
    captured.error("x", { err });
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(1);
    for (const needle of [secret, "E11000", "dup_log_unique", "dup key"]) {
      expect(lines[0]).not.toContain(needle);
    }
  });

  it("honours the session so a failed transaction persists nothing", async () => {
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
    await expect(
      withTransaction(async (session) => {
        await probes.insertOne({ ref: "t", amount: "1" }, { session });
        throw new Error("rollback");
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow("rollback");
    expect(await probes.findOne({ ref: "t" })).toBeNull();
  });

  it("rejects an invalid collection name synchronously", () => {
    expect(() => typedCollection("BadName", strictSchema)).toThrow(InternalError);
  });

  it("maps an unreachable server to DependencyUnavailableError", async () => {
    const standalone = await startStandalone();
    let stopped = false;
    try {
      await useDatabaseUri(standalone.getUri());
      const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, strictSchema);
      await probes.insertOne({ ref: "x", amount: "1" });
      await standalone.stop({ doCleanup: true });
      stopped = true;
      await expect(probes.findOne({ ref: "x" })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(DependencyUnavailableError);
    } finally {
      if (!stopped) await standalone.stop({ doCleanup: true });
    }
  });
});

describe("typedCollection: maxTimeMS on every read, update and count (entry gate 4)", () => {
  it.each([
    "findOne",
    "find",
    "findOneAndUpdate",
    "updateOne",
    "updateMany",
    "countDocuments",
  ] as const)("%s passes maxTimeMS: 5000 to the driver", async (method) => {
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Collection.prototype, method);
    const probes = typedCollection(COLLECTION, counterSchema);
    const filter = safeEquals("ref", "m");
    if (method === "findOne") await probes.findOne(filter);
    if (method === "find") await probes.find(filter, { limit: 1 });
    if (method === "findOneAndUpdate")
      await probes.findOneAndUpdate(filter, { $set: { note: "x" } });
    if (method === "updateOne") await probes.updateOne(filter, { $set: { note: "x" } });
    if (method === "updateMany") await probes.updateMany(filter, { $set: { note: "x" } });
    if (method === "countDocuments") await probes.countDocuments(filter);
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalled();
    const options = spy.mock.calls[0]?.at(-1);
    expect(options).toMatchObject({ maxTimeMS: 5000 });
  });
});

describe("typedCollection: update operations", () => {
  const counters = () => typedCollection(COLLECTION, counterSchema);

  it("findOneAndUpdate upserts with $inc and $setOnInsert and returns the document after", async () => {
    const filter = safeEquals("ref", "c1");
    const update = { $inc: { count: 1 }, $setOnInsert: { note: "new" } };
    const first = await counters().findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, { upsert: true });
    const second = await counters().findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, { upsert: true });
    expect(first).toMatchObject({ ref: "c1", count: 1, note: "new" });
    expect(second).toMatchObject({ ref: "c1", count: 2, note: "new" });
    expect(second?._id.equals(first?._id ?? "")).toBe(true);
  });

  it("findOneAndUpdate returns null when nothing matches and upsert is off", async () => {
    expect(
      await counters().findOneAndUpdate(safeEquals("ref", "missing"), { $set: { note: "x" } }),
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  it("findOneAndUpdate can return the document before the update", async () => {
    await counters().insertOne({ ref: "b1", count: 1, note: "old" });
    const before = await counters().findOneAndUpdate(
      safeEquals("ref", "b1"),
      { $set: { note: "new" } },
      { returnDocument: "before" },
    );
    expect(before?.note).toBe("old");
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "an unknown $set key", update: { $set: { other: "x" } } },
    {
      label: "a $set value that fails the field schema",
      update: { $set: { note: "too-long-note" } },
    },
    { label: "an unknown $setOnInsert key", update: { $setOnInsert: { other: 1 } } },
    { label: "a non-integer $inc", update: { $inc: { count: 1.5 } } },
    { label: "an unknown $inc key", update: { $inc: { other: 1 } } },
    { label: "an empty update", update: {} },
  ])("rejects $label with DOCUMENT_INVALID and writes nothing", async ({ update }) => {
    await counters().insertOne({ ref: "v1", count: 1, note: "n" });
    const error: unknown = await counters()
      .updateOne(safeEquals("ref", "v1"), update as never)
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
    expect((error as ValidationError).code).toBe("DOCUMENT_INVALID");
    expect(await counters().findOne(safeEquals("ref", "v1"))).toMatchObject({
      count: 1,
      note: "n",
    });
  });

  it("names the key but not the value in a DOCUMENT_INVALID message", async () => {
    const error = (await counters()
      .updateOne(safeEquals("ref", "x"), { $set: { note: "SENTINEL-TOO-LONG" } })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e)) as ValidationError;
    expect(error.message).toContain("$set.note");
    expect(error.message).not.toContain("SENTINEL");
  });

  it("updateOne reports matched, modified and upserted counts", async () => {
    await counters().insertOne({ ref: "u1", count: 1, note: "a" });
    expect(await counters().updateOne(safeEquals("ref", "u1"), { $set: { note: "b" } })).toEqual({
      matchedCount: 1,
      modifiedCount: 1,
      upsertedId: null,
    });
    const upserted = await counters().updateOne(
      safeEquals("ref", "u2"),
      { $setOnInsert: { count: 1, note: "c" } },
      { upsert: true },
    );
    expect(upserted.matchedCount).toBe(0);
    expect(upserted.upsertedId).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("updateMany updates every match and countDocuments counts with an optional limit", async () => {
    for (const ref of ["m1", "m2", "m3"]) await counters().insertOne({ ref, count: 1, note: "a" });
    const result = await counters().updateMany({}, { $set: { note: "z" } });
    expect(result).toEqual({ matchedCount: 3, modifiedCount: 3 });
    expect(await counters().countDocuments({ note: "z" })).toBe(3);
    expect(await counters().countDocuments({}, { limit: 2 })).toBe(2);
    for (const limit of [0, 1001, 1.5]) {
      await expect(counters().countDocuments({}, { limit })).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(RangeError);
    }
  });

  it("maps a duplicate key on update to ConflictError DUPLICATE_KEY", async () => {
    const db = await getDb();
    await db
      .collection("dup_update_probe")
      .createIndex({ ref: 1 }, { unique: true, name: "dup_update_unique" });
    const dups = typedCollection("dup_update_probe", counterSchema);
    await dups.insertOne({ ref: "a", count: 1, note: "n" });
    await dups.insertOne({ ref: "b", count: 1, note: "n" });
    const error: unknown = await dups
      .updateOne(safeEquals("ref", "b"), { $set: { ref: "a" } })
      .catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    expect((error as ConflictError).code).toBe("DUPLICATE_KEY");
  });

  it("honours the session for updates inside a transaction", async () => {
    await counters().insertOne({ ref: "t1", count: 1, note: "a" });
    await expect(
      withTransaction(async (session) => {
        await counters().updateOne(safeEquals("ref", "t1"), { $inc: { count: 1 } }, { session });
        expect(await counters().countDocuments(safeEquals("count", 2), { session })).toBe(1);
        throw new Error("rollback");
      }),
    ).rejects.toThrow("rollback");
    expect(await counters().findOne(safeEquals("ref", "t1"))).toMatchObject({ count: 1 });
  });
});
```

Create `src/modules/database/internal/safe-filter.ts`:

```ts
import { ObjectId } from "mongodb";
import { InternalError, ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";

export type SafeScalar = string | number | boolean | bigint | Date | ObjectId;

/** Dotted field path; no "$", no empty segment. */
const FIELD_PATH = /^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*(\.[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)*$/;

function isSafeScalar(value: unknown): value is SafeScalar {
  switch (typeof value) {
    case "string":
    case "number":
    case "boolean":
    case "bigint":
      return true;
    case "object":
      if (value instanceof Date) return !Number.isNaN(value.getTime());
      return value instanceof ObjectId;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}

/**
 * Builds `{ [field]: value }` only for a scalar value. The ONLY permitted way to put a
 * request-derived or otherwise variable value into a filter (see typedCollection). Server
 * constants (`revokedAt: null`, `status: "active"`, `expiresAt: { $gt: now }`) are written
 * literally next to it. Accepts values that bypassed static typing and checks them at runtime.
 * Compose several fields with `Object.assign({}, safeEquals("a", x), safeEquals("b", y))`.
 * @throws ValidationError({ code: "FILTER_INVALID" }) when value is not a SafeScalar (object,
 *         array, null, undefined, function, symbol, an invalid Date, an operator object).
 * @throws InternalError({ code: "FILTER_FIELD_INVALID" }) when field is not a plain dotted path.
 */
export function safeEquals<K extends string, V extends SafeScalar>(
  field: K,
  value: V,
): { readonly [P in K]: V } {
  if (!FIELD_PATH.test(field)) {
    throw new InternalError({
      code: "FILTER_FIELD_INVALID",
      message: `invalid filter field ${JSON.stringify(field)}`,
    });
  }
  if (!isSafeScalar(value as unknown)) {
    throw new ValidationError({
      code: "FILTER_INVALID",
      message: `filter value for ${field} is not a scalar`,
    });
  }
  return { [field]: value } as { readonly [P in K]: V };
}
```

Create `src/modules/database/internal/ids.ts`:

```ts
import { ObjectId } from "mongodb";
import { z } from "zod";

const OBJECT_ID_HEX = /^[0-9a-fA-F]{24}$/;

/** 24-hex (any case) -> ObjectId; anything else -> null. Never throws. */
export function parseObjectId(value: unknown): ObjectId | null {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !OBJECT_ID_HEX.test(value)) return null;
  return ObjectId.createFromHexString(value.toLowerCase());
}

/** Zod schema for ObjectId-typed document fields. */
export const objectIdSchema = z.custom<ObjectId>(
  (value) => value instanceof ObjectId,
  "Must be an ObjectId",
);
```

Replace the whole of `src/modules/database/internal/typed-collection.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import type {
  ClientSession,
  Document,
  Filter,
  ObjectId,
  Sort,
  UpdateFilter,
  WithId,
} from "mongodb";
import { z } from "zod";
import { InternalError, ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { getDb } from "./client";
import { mapMongoError } from "./errors";
import { assertCollectionName } from "./names";

const MAX_FIND_LIMIT = 1000;
/** Server-side time limit for every read, update and count (entry gate 4). Inserts take none. */
export const QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS = 5000;

/** The update operators typedCollection supports. Values are validated against the schema. */
export interface TypedUpdate<S extends z.ZodObject> {
  /** Each value is parsed by schema.shape[key]; an unknown key or a failed parse -> DOCUMENT_INVALID. */
  readonly $set?: Partial<z.input<S>>;
  readonly $setOnInsert?: Partial<z.input<S>>;
  /** Keys must exist in schema.shape; values must be safe integers. */
  readonly $inc?: Partial<Record<keyof z.output<S> & string, number>>;
}

/**
 * Filters must never be built from request data except through `safeEquals`. Constant clauses
 * written in code (`revokedAt: null`, `status: "active"`, `expiresAt: { $gt: now }`) are allowed.
 */
export interface TypedCollection<S extends z.ZodObject> {
  readonly name: string;
  /** @throws ValidationError("DOCUMENT_INVALID"), ConflictError("DUPLICATE_KEY"), DependencyUnavailableError */
  insertOne(doc: z.input<S>, options?: { session?: ClientSession }): Promise<WithId<z.output<S>>>;
  /** @throws InternalError("DATA_INTEGRITY") if a stored doc fails the schema; DependencyUnavailableError */
  findOne(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    options?: { session?: ClientSession },
  ): Promise<WithId<z.output<S>> | null>;
  /** limit is required, integer in [1, 1000] (no unbounded reads). Same throws as findOne, plus RangeError. */
  find(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    options: { limit: number; sort?: Sort; session?: ClientSession },
  ): Promise<WithId<z.output<S>>[]>;
  /** returnDocument defaults to "after". @throws as findOne, plus ValidationError("DOCUMENT_INVALID"), ConflictError("DUPLICATE_KEY") */
  findOneAndUpdate(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    update: TypedUpdate<S>,
    options?: { session?: ClientSession; upsert?: boolean; returnDocument?: "before" | "after" },
  ): Promise<WithId<z.output<S>> | null>;
  /** @throws ValidationError("DOCUMENT_INVALID"), ConflictError("DUPLICATE_KEY"), DependencyUnavailableError */
  updateOne(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    update: TypedUpdate<S>,
    options?: { session?: ClientSession; upsert?: boolean },
  ): Promise<{ matchedCount: number; modifiedCount: number; upsertedId: ObjectId | null }>;
  /** @throws ValidationError("DOCUMENT_INVALID"), DependencyUnavailableError */
  updateMany(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    update: TypedUpdate<S>,
    options?: { session?: ClientSession },
  ): Promise<{ matchedCount: number; modifiedCount: number }>;
  /** limit, when given, is an integer in [1, 1000]; otherwise RangeError. @throws DependencyUnavailableError */
  countDocuments(
    filter: Filter<z.output<S>>,
    options?: { session?: ClientSession; limit?: number },
  ): Promise<number>;
}

function asRecord(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> {
  return value as Record<string, unknown>;
}

function asDocumentFilter(filter: unknown): Filter<Document> {
  return filter as Filter<Document>;
}

function issuePaths(error: z.ZodError): string {
  return error.issues
    .map((issue) => (issue.path.length === 0 ? "(root)" : issue.path.map(String).join(".")))
    .join(", ");
}

function assertLimit(limit: number): void {
  if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1 || limit > MAX_FIND_LIMIT) {
    throw new RangeError(`limit must be an integer in [1, ${MAX_FIND_LIMIT}]`);
  }
}

/**
 * The Zod schema is the single source of truth for the document shape, excluding `_id` (schemas
 * must not declare `_id`). Writes store the parsed output; reads are parsed. Sync, no I/O.
 * Filters must never be built from request data except through `safeEquals`.
 * @throws InternalError("COLLECTION_NAME_INVALID")
 */
export function typedCollection<S extends z.ZodObject>(
  name: string,
  schema: S,
): TypedCollection<S> {
  assertCollectionName(name);
  type Stored = WithId<z.output<S>>;
  const shape: Readonly<Record<string, z.ZodType | undefined>> = schema.shape;

  async function collection() {
    return (await getDb()).collection<Document>(name);
  }

  function documentInvalid(detail: string, cause?: unknown): ValidationError {
    return new ValidationError({
      code: "DOCUMENT_INVALID",
      safeMessage: "Invalid document",
      message: `${name}: ${detail}`,
      cause,
    });
  }

  function parseStored(doc: WithId<Document>): Stored {
    const { _id, ...rest } = doc;
    const parsed = schema.safeParse(rest);
    if (!parsed.success) {
      throw new InternalError({
        code: "DATA_INTEGRITY",
        message: `${name}: stored document failed schema validation at ${issuePaths(parsed.error)}`,
        cause: parsed.error,
      });
    }
    return { _id, ...asRecord(parsed.data) } as unknown as Stored;
  }

  /** Validates every field of a typed update against the schema. Error messages name keys only. */
  function toDriverUpdate(update: TypedUpdate<S>): UpdateFilter<Document> {
    const out: Record<string, Record<string, unknown>> = {};
    for (const operator of ["$set", "$setOnInsert"] as const) {
      const fields: Record<string, unknown> | undefined = update[operator];
      if (fields === undefined) continue;
      const parsed: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(fields)) {
        const fieldSchema = shape[key];
        if (fieldSchema === undefined)
          throw documentInvalid(`${operator}.${key} is not in the schema`);
        const result = z.safeParse(fieldSchema, value);
        if (!result.success) throw documentInvalid(`${operator}.${key}`, result.error);
        parsed[key] = result.data;
      }
      out[operator] = parsed;
    }
    if (update.$inc !== undefined) {
      const increments: Record<string, unknown> = {};
      for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(update.$inc)) {
        if (shape[key] === undefined || !Number.isSafeInteger(value)) {
          throw documentInvalid(`$inc.${key}`);
        }
        increments[key] = value;
      }
      out.$inc = increments;
    }
    if (Object.keys(out).length === 0) throw documentInvalid("empty update");
    return out as UpdateFilter<Document>;
  }

  return {
    name,

    async insertOne(doc, options = {}) {
      const parsed = schema.safeParse(doc);
      if (!parsed.success) throw documentInvalid(issuePaths(parsed.error), parsed.error);
      const data = asRecord(parsed.data);
      try {
        // Copy: the driver mutates the inserted object to add _id.
        const { insertedId } = await (
          await collection()
        ).insertOne({ ...data }, { session: options.session });
        return { _id: insertedId, ...data } as unknown as Stored;
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async findOne(filter, options = {}) {
      try {
        const doc = await (
          await collection()
        ).findOne(asDocumentFilter(filter), {
          session: options.session,
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
        });
        return doc === null ? null : parseStored(doc);
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async find(filter, options) {
      const { limit, sort, session } = options;
      assertLimit(limit);
      try {
        const cursor = (await collection()).find(asDocumentFilter(filter), {
          limit,
          session,
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
        });
        const docs = await (sort === undefined ? cursor : cursor.sort(sort)).toArray();
        return docs.map((doc) => parseStored(doc));
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, options = {}) {
      const driverUpdate = toDriverUpdate(update);
      try {
        const doc = await (
          await collection()
        ).findOneAndUpdate(asDocumentFilter(filter), driverUpdate, {
          session: options.session,
          upsert: options.upsert ?? false,
          returnDocument: options.returnDocument ?? "after",
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
        });
        return doc === null ? null : parseStored(doc);
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async updateOne(filter, update, options = {}) {
      const driverUpdate = toDriverUpdate(update);
      try {
        const result = await (
          await collection()
        ).updateOne(asDocumentFilter(filter), driverUpdate, {
          session: options.session,
          upsert: options.upsert ?? false,
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
        });
        return {
          matchedCount: result.matchedCount,
          modifiedCount: result.modifiedCount,
          upsertedId: (result.upsertedId as ObjectId | null | undefined) ?? null,
        };
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async updateMany(filter, update, options = {}) {
      const driverUpdate = toDriverUpdate(update);
      try {
        const result = await (
          await collection()
        ).updateMany(asDocumentFilter(filter), driverUpdate, {
          session: options.session,
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
        });
        return { matchedCount: result.matchedCount, modifiedCount: result.modifiedCount };
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },

    async countDocuments(filter, options = {}) {
      if (options.limit !== undefined) assertLimit(options.limit);
      try {
        return await (
          await collection()
        ).countDocuments(asDocumentFilter(filter), {
          session: options.session,
          maxTimeMS: QUERY_MAX_TIME_MS,
          ...(options.limit === undefined ? {} : { limit: options.limit }),
        });
      } catch (error) {
        throw mapMongoError(error);
      }
    },
  };
}
```

### Task 4: Shared additions: auth errors, Retry-After for 429, noContent and the clock

Architecture §3.1. Adds the four auth error classes, `Retry-After` for `RateLimitedError`, the 204 helper and one injectable clock that every auth flow reads through `now()`. Tests drive time with `setClockForTests` instead of fake timers, so the MongoDB driver's own timers keep real time.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/shared/clock.test.ts`, `src/modules/shared/internal/clock.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/shared/errors.test.ts`, `tests/unit/shared/http.test.ts`, `src/modules/shared/internal/errors.ts`, `src/modules/shared/internal/http.ts`, `src/modules/shared/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/shared/errors.test.ts`, `tests/unit/shared/http.test.ts`, `tests/unit/shared/clock.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `AppError`, `AppErrorOptions`, `initFrom` (inside `errors.ts`), `logger`.
- Produces from `@/modules/shared` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3):
  - `class UnauthenticatedError extends AppError { constructor(options?: AppErrorOptions) }`: 401 `UNAUTHENTICATED`, "Authentication required".
  - `class AuthFailedError extends AppError { readonly reason: string; constructor(reason: string, options?: AppErrorOptions) }`: 401 `AUTH_FAILED`, "Authentication failed", `message` `auth failed: <reason>`.
  - `class ForbiddenOriginError extends AppError { constructor(options?: AppErrorOptions) }`: 403 `ORIGIN_REJECTED`, "Origin not allowed".
  - `class RateLimitedError extends AppError { readonly retryAfterSeconds: number; constructor(retryAfterSeconds: number, options?: AppErrorOptions) }`: 429 `RATE_LIMITED`, "Too many requests"; seconds = `ceil`, clamped to [1, 86400], non-finite → 1.
  - `type Clock = () => Date`; `now(): Date` (a fresh Date each call); `setClockForTests(clock: Clock): void`; `resetClockForTests(): void`.
  - `noContent(init?: { headers?: Record<string, string> }): Response`: 204, no body, `Cache-Control: no-store`.
  - `toErrorResponse` additionally sets `Retry-After: String(error.retryAfterSeconds)` for `RateLimitedError`.

```ts
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  AppError,
  ConfigError,
  ConflictError,
  DependencyUnavailableError,
  InternalError,
  NotFoundError,
  RateLimitedError,
  ValidationError,
  logger,
  noContent,
  noStoreJson,
  toErrorResponse,
} from "@/modules/shared";

const SENTINEL = "SENTINEL-INTERNAL-DETAIL";

describe("noStoreJson", () => {
  it("serializes the body with no-store and JSON content type", async () => {
    const res = noStoreJson({ status: "ok" });
    expect(res.status).toBe(200);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(res.headers.get("content-type")).toBe("application/json");
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ status: "ok" });
  });

  it("applies status and extra headers but never drops no-store", () => {
    const res = noStoreJson(
      { a: 1 },
      { status: 503, headers: { "Retry-After": "5", "Cache-Control": "max-age=60" } },
    );
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("5");
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
  });

  it("serializes bigint values as decimal strings, nested or top level (entry gate 6)", async () => {
    const res = noStoreJson({ amount: 18446744073709551616n, nested: { list: [1n, 2] } });
    expect(await res.text()).toBe('{"amount":"18446744073709551616","nested":{"list":["1",2]}}');
    expect(await noStoreJson(5n).text()).toBe('"5"');
  });
});

describe("noContent", () => {
  it("returns 204 with no body and no-store", async () => {
    const res = noContent();
    expect(res.status).toBe(204);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(await res.text()).toBe("");
  });

  it("applies extra headers, including Set-Cookie, but never drops no-store", () => {
    const res = noContent({
      headers: { "Set-Cookie": "a=; Max-Age=0", "Cache-Control": "public" },
    });
    expect(res.headers.getSetCookie()).toEqual(["a=; Max-Age=0"]);
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
  });
});

function silenceLoggerError() {
  return vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
}

describe("toErrorResponse", () => {
  it.each([
    {
      error: new ValidationError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 400,
      code: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
      message: "Invalid request",
      logged: "VALIDATION_FAILED",
    },
    {
      error: new NotFoundError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 404,
      code: "NOT_FOUND",
      message: "Not found",
      logged: "NOT_FOUND",
    },
    {
      error: new ConflictError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 409,
      code: "CONFLICT",
      message: "Conflict",
      logged: "CONFLICT",
    },
    {
      error: new DependencyUnavailableError("database", { message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 503,
      code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "Service temporarily unavailable",
      logged: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
    },
    {
      error: new InternalError({ message: SENTINEL }),
      status: 500,
      code: "INTERNAL",
      message: "Internal error",
      logged: "INTERNAL",
    },
    {
      error: new ConfigError([{ key: SENTINEL, reason: "Required" }]),
      status: 500,
      code: "INTERNAL",
      message: "Internal error",
      logged: "CONFIG_INVALID",
    },
  ])(
    "maps $logged to $status $code with only the safe message",
    async ({ error, status, code, message, logged }) => {
      const logError = silenceLoggerError();
      const res = toErrorResponse(error);
      expect(res.status).toBe(status);
      expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
      const text = await res.text();
      expect(JSON.parse(text)).toEqual({ code, message });
      expect(text).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
      expect(text).not.toContain("stack");
      expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request failed", { err: error, code: logged });
    },
  );

  it.each([
    { label: "DATA_INTEGRITY", error: new InternalError({ code: "DATA_INTEGRITY" }) },
    { label: "INDEX_CONFLICT", error: new InternalError({ code: "INDEX_CONFLICT" }) },
    {
      label: "a custom 502",
      error: new AppError({ code: "UPSTREAM_BROKEN", httpStatus: 502, safeMessage: "Bad gateway" }),
    },
  ])(
    "collapses $label to 500 INTERNAL and logs the original code (entry gate 5)",
    async ({ error }) => {
      const logError = silenceLoggerError();
      const res = toErrorResponse(error);
      expect(res.status).toBe(500);
      expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" });
      expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
      expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request failed", { err: error, code: error.code });
    },
  );

  it("keeps DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE as a 503 with Retry-After (not collapsed)", async () => {
    silenceLoggerError();
    const res = toErrorResponse(new DependencyUnavailableError("database"));
    expect(res.status).toBe(503);
    expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("5");
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({
      code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "Service temporarily unavailable",
    });
  });

  it("adds Retry-After only for DependencyUnavailableError", () => {
    silenceLoggerError();
    expect(
      toErrorResponse(new DependencyUnavailableError("database")).headers.get("retry-after"),
    ).toBe("5");
    expect(toErrorResponse(new NotFoundError()).headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
    expect(toErrorResponse(new InternalError()).headers.get("retry-after")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("maps RateLimitedError to 429 with Retry-After set to its seconds", async () => {
    const logError = silenceLoggerError();
    const error = new RateLimitedError(42);
    const res = toErrorResponse(error);
    expect(res.status).toBe(429);
    expect(res.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("42");
    expect(res.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(await res.json()).toEqual({ code: "RATE_LIMITED", message: "Too many requests" });
    expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledWith("request failed", { err: error, code: "RATE_LIMITED" });
  });

  it("maps unknown errors and non-Error throws to a generic 500", async () => {
    const logError = silenceLoggerError();
    for (const thrown of [new Error(SENTINEL), SENTINEL, { message: SENTINEL }]) {
      const res = toErrorResponse(thrown);
      expect(res.status).toBe(500);
      const text = await res.text();
      expect(JSON.parse(text)).toEqual({ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" });
      expect(text).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
    }
    expect(logError).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(3);
    expect(logError).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith("request failed", {
      err: { message: SENTINEL },
      code: "INTERNAL",
    });
  });

  it("keeps cause out of the response", async () => {
    silenceLoggerError();
    const res = toErrorResponse(new InternalError({ cause: new Error(SENTINEL) }));
    expect(await res.text()).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/shared/clock.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { now, resetClockForTests, setClockForTests } from "@/modules/shared";

afterEach(() => {
  resetClockForTests();
});

describe("clock", () => {
  it("defaults to the system time", () => {
    const before = Date.now();
    const value = now().getTime();
    expect(value).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(before);
    expect(value).toBeLessThanOrEqual(Date.now());
  });

  it("uses the test clock until reset", () => {
    const fixed = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    setClockForTests(() => fixed);
    expect(now().toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    resetClockForTests();
    expect(now().getTime()).not.toBe(fixed.getTime());
  });

  it("returns a fresh Date on every call, so callers cannot mutate the clock", () => {
    const fixed = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    setClockForTests(() => fixed);
    const first = now();
    first.setTime(0);
    expect(now().toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    expect(now()).not.toBe(now());
  });
});
```
