Replace the whole of `src/modules/shared/internal/errors.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
export interface AppErrorInit {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly httpStatus: number;
  readonly safeMessage: string;
  /** Internal, may be detailed, never sent to clients. Defaults to safeMessage. */
  readonly message?: string;
  readonly cause?: unknown;
}

export interface AppErrorOptions {
  readonly code?: string;
  readonly safeMessage?: string;
  readonly message?: string;
  readonly cause?: unknown;
}

export class AppError extends Error {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly httpStatus: number;
  readonly safeMessage: string;

  constructor(init: AppErrorInit) {
    super(
      init.message ?? init.safeMessage,
      init.cause === undefined ? undefined : { cause: init.cause },
    );
    this.name = new.target.name;
    this.code = init.code;
    this.httpStatus = init.httpStatus;
    this.safeMessage = init.safeMessage;
  }
}

interface ErrorDefaults {
  readonly code: string;
  readonly httpStatus: number;
  readonly safeMessage: string;
}

function initFrom(defaults: ErrorDefaults, options: AppErrorOptions): AppErrorInit {
  return {
    code: options.code ?? defaults.code,
    httpStatus: defaults.httpStatus,
    safeMessage: options.safeMessage ?? defaults.safeMessage,
    message: options.message,
    cause: options.cause,
  };
}

export class ValidationError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        { code: "VALIDATION_FAILED", httpStatus: 400, safeMessage: "Invalid request" },
        options,
      ),
    );
  }
}

export class NotFoundError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(initFrom({ code: "NOT_FOUND", httpStatus: 404, safeMessage: "Not found" }, options));
  }
}

export class ConflictError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(initFrom({ code: "CONFLICT", httpStatus: 409, safeMessage: "Conflict" }, options));
  }
}

export type DependencyName = "database";

export class DependencyUnavailableError extends AppError {
  readonly dependency: DependencyName;

  constructor(dependency: DependencyName, options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        {
          code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
          httpStatus: 503,
          safeMessage: "Service temporarily unavailable",
        },
        options,
      ),
    );
    this.dependency = dependency;
  }
}

export class InternalError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(initFrom({ code: "INTERNAL", httpStatus: 500, safeMessage: "Internal error" }, options));
  }
}

export class UnauthenticatedError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        { code: "UNAUTHENTICATED", httpStatus: 401, safeMessage: "Authentication required" },
        options,
      ),
    );
  }
}

/** A failed sign-in or link proof. `reason` is a static string for logs and security events only. */
export class AuthFailedError extends AppError {
  readonly reason: string;

  constructor(reason: string, options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        { code: "AUTH_FAILED", httpStatus: 401, safeMessage: "Authentication failed" },
        { message: `auth failed: ${reason}`, ...options },
      ),
    );
    this.reason = reason;
  }
}

export class ForbiddenOriginError extends AppError {
  constructor(options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        { code: "ORIGIN_REJECTED", httpStatus: 403, safeMessage: "Origin not allowed" },
        options,
      ),
    );
  }
}

const MAX_RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS = 86_400;

export class RateLimitedError extends AppError {
  /** Integer in [1, 86400]: Math.ceil of the input, clamped. Sent as Retry-After. */
  readonly retryAfterSeconds: number;

  constructor(retryAfterSeconds: number, options: AppErrorOptions = {}) {
    super(
      initFrom(
        { code: "RATE_LIMITED", httpStatus: 429, safeMessage: "Too many requests" },
        options,
      ),
    );
    const seconds = Number.isFinite(retryAfterSeconds) ? Math.ceil(retryAfterSeconds) : 1;
    this.retryAfterSeconds = Math.min(MAX_RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS, Math.max(1, seconds));
  }
}

export interface ConfigIssue {
  readonly key: string;
  readonly reason: string;
}

/** Startup-only. The message lists key names and static reasons; it never contains values. */
export class ConfigError extends AppError {
  readonly issues: readonly ConfigIssue[];

  constructor(issues: readonly ConfigIssue[]) {
    const frozen = Object.freeze(issues.map((issue) => Object.freeze({ ...issue })));
    super({
      code: "CONFIG_INVALID",
      httpStatus: 500,
      safeMessage: "Internal error",
      message: `Invalid configuration: ${frozen.map((i) => `${i.key} (${i.reason})`).join("; ")}`,
    });
    this.issues = frozen;
  }
}

export function isAppError(value: unknown): value is AppError {
  return value instanceof AppError;
}
```

Create `src/modules/shared/internal/clock.ts`:

```ts
export type Clock = () => Date;

const systemClock: Clock = () => new Date();
let active: Clock = systemClock;

/** Current time from the active clock. Returns a fresh Date on every call. */
export function now(): Date {
  return new Date(active().getTime());
}

/** Test only: replaces the clock until resetClockForTests(). */
export function setClockForTests(clock: Clock): void {
  active = clock;
}

export function resetClockForTests(): void {
  active = systemClock;
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/modules/shared/internal/http.ts` (last written in Task 2) with:

```ts
import { DependencyUnavailableError, RateLimitedError, isAppError } from "./errors";
import { logger } from "./logger";

const RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS = "5";
const INTERNAL_BODY = { code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" } as const;

/** JSON.stringify replacer: bigint becomes its decimal string (JSON has no bigint). */
function bigintAsString(_key: string, value: unknown): unknown {
  return typeof value === "bigint" ? value.toString() : value;
}

export function noStoreJson(
  body: unknown,
  init: { status?: number; headers?: Record<string, string> } = {},
): Response {
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
  headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  headers.set("Content-Type", "application/json");
  return new Response(JSON.stringify(body, bigintAsString), {
    status: init.status ?? 200,
    headers,
  });
}

/** 204 with Cache-Control: no-store and no body (a 204 Response cannot carry a body). */
export function noContent(init: { headers?: Record<string, string> } = {}): Response {
  const headers = new Headers(init.headers);
  headers.set("Cache-Control", "no-store");
  return new Response(null, { status: 204, headers });
}

/**
 * Logs the full error server-side (with its original code); the client only ever sees a stable
 * code and safe message. Every status >= 500 collapses to INTERNAL, except
 * DependencyUnavailableError, which keeps its 503 code and Retry-After. RateLimitedError adds
 * Retry-After with its own seconds.
 */
export function toErrorResponse(error: unknown): Response {
  const code = isAppError(error) ? error.code : "INTERNAL";
  logger.error("request failed", { err: error, code });
  if (!isAppError(error)) return noStoreJson(INTERNAL_BODY, { status: 500 });
  if (error instanceof DependencyUnavailableError) {
    return noStoreJson(
      { code: error.code, message: error.safeMessage },
      { status: error.httpStatus, headers: { "Retry-After": RETRY_AFTER_SECONDS } },
    );
  }
  if (error.httpStatus >= 500) return noStoreJson(INTERNAL_BODY, { status: 500 });
  const headers: Record<string, string> =
    error instanceof RateLimitedError ? { "Retry-After": String(error.retryAfterSeconds) } : {};
  return noStoreJson(
    { code: error.code, message: error.safeMessage },
    { status: error.httpStatus, headers },
  );
}
```

### Task 5: Config additions, the network enum and .env.example

Spec §6 and architecture §2.4. Eight new keys with static, key-only error reasons. `APP_ORIGIN` is required, so the integration setup supplies it (and high rate limits, because every flow in one test file shares the `"unknown"` client-IP bucket), and the unit tests that build an env include it. The cross-field rule (`SESSION_MAX_DAYS ≥ SESSION_IDLE_DAYS`) runs only when every field parsed, so an out-of-range idle value is reported on its own key.

- Create: `tests/unit/validation/solana-network.test.ts`, `src/modules/validation/internal/solana.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/config/load-config.test.ts`, `tests/unit/instrumentation.test.ts`, `tests/setup/integration.setup.ts`, `src/modules/validation/index.ts`, `src/modules/config/internal/schema.ts`, `src/modules/config/internal/load.ts`, `.env.example`
- Test: `tests/unit/validation/solana-network.test.ts`, `tests/unit/config/load-config.test.ts`, `tests/unit/instrumentation.test.ts`

- Produces:

```ts
};

  } catch (error) {

    throw error;
  }
  throw new Error("expected loadConfig to throw ConfigError");
}

    { key: "TRUST_PROXY", value: SENTINEL, reason: "Must be true or false" },
    { key: "SESSION_IDLE_DAYS", value: "0", reason: "Integer 1-30" },
    { key: "SESSION_IDLE_DAYS", value: "31", reason: "Integer 1-30" },
    { key: "SESSION_IDLE_DAYS", value: SENTINEL, reason: "Integer 1-30" },

      key: "SESSION_MAX_DAYS",

    },
    { key: "RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN", value: "0", reason: "Integer 1-1000" },
    { key: "RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS", value: "1001", reason: "Integer 1-1000" },
    { key: "RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN", value: "1.5", reason: "Integer 1-1000" },
  ])("reports invalid $key ($value) without echoing its value", ({ key, value, reason }) => {
    const error = configErrorOf({ ...VALID, [key]: value });
    expect(error.issues).toEqual([{ key, reason }]);
    expect(error.message).toContain(key);
    expect(error.message).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
    expect(error.message.toLowerCase()).not.toContain(SENTINEL.toLowerCase());
    expect(JSON.stringify(error.issues)).not.toContain(SENTINEL);
  });

  it("reports SESSION_MAX_DAYS below SESSION_IDLE_DAYS on SESSION_MAX_DAYS", () => {
    expect(
      configErrorOf({ ...VALID, SESSION_IDLE_DAYS: "10", SESSION_MAX_DAYS: "9" }).issues,
    ).toEqual([{ key: "SESSION_MAX_DAYS", reason: "Integer 1-90 and at least SESSION_IDLE_DAYS" }]);
  });

  it("reports every missing required key in schema order", () => {
    const error = configErrorOf({});
    expect(error.issues).toEqual([
      { key: "NODE_ENV", reason: "Required" },
      { key: "MONGODB_URI", reason: "Required" },
      { key: "MONGODB_DB_NAME", reason: "Required" },
      { key: "APP_ORIGIN", reason: "Required" },
    ]);
  });

  it("rejects a database name longer than 63 characters", () => {
    expect(configErrorOf({ ...VALID, MONGODB_DB_NAME: "a".repeat(64) }).issues[0]?.key).toBe(
      "MONGODB_DB_NAME",
    );
  });

  it("formats the documented message", () => {
    const error = configErrorOf({ NODE_ENV: "test", MONGODB_DB_NAME: "x", LOG_LEVEL: "verbose" });
    expect(error.message).toBe(
      "Invalid configuration: MONGODB_URI (Required); LOG_LEVEL (Invalid option: expected one of debug|info|warn|error); APP_ORIGIN (Required)",
    );
  });
});

describe("loadConfig() from process.env", () => {
  function stubValidEnv(): void {
    for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(VALID)) vi.stubEnv(key, value);
  }

  afterEach(() => {
    resetConfigForTests();
    setLogLevel("info");
    vi.unstubAllEnvs();
  });

  it("parses once and returns the cached object until reset", () => {
    stubValidEnv();
    resetConfigForTests();
    const first = loadConfig();
    vi.stubEnv("MONGODB_DB_NAME", "other_db");
    expect(loadConfig()).toBe(first);
    resetConfigForTests();
    expect(loadConfig().MONGODB_DB_NAME).toBe("other_db");
  });

  it("applies LOG_LEVEL to the shared logger", () => {
    stubValidEnv();
    vi.stubEnv("LOG_LEVEL", "error");
    resetConfigForTests();
    loadConfig();
    const out = vi.spyOn(process.stdout, "write").mockImplementation(() => true);
    logger.info("dropped");
    expect(out).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("explicit env neither reads nor writes the cache", () => {
    stubValidEnv();
    resetConfigForTests();
    const cached = loadConfig();
    expect(loadConfig({ ...VALID, MONGODB_DB_NAME: "explicit_db" }).MONGODB_DB_NAME).toBe(
      "explicit_db",
    );
    expect(loadConfig()).toBe(cached);
  });

  it("throws ConfigError and caches nothing when process.env is invalid", () => {
    stubValidEnv();
    vi.stubEnv("MONGODB_URI", "");
    resetConfigForTests();
    expect(() => loadConfig()).toThrow(ConfigError);
    vi.stubEnv("MONGODB_URI", VALID.MONGODB_URI);
    expect(loadConfig().MONGODB_URI).toBe(VALID.MONGODB_URI);
  });

  it("requires APP_ORIGIN at runtime", () => {
    stubValidEnv();
    vi.stubEnv("APP_ORIGIN", "");
    resetConfigForTests();
    expect(() => loadConfig()).toThrow("APP_ORIGIN (Required)");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { register } from "@/instrumentation";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/modules/config";
import { ConfigError, logger, setLogLevel } from "@/modules/shared";

/** Port 1: nothing listens there, so the unit project can never reach a developer mongod. */
const VALID = {
  NODE_ENV: "test",
  MONGODB_URI: "mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0",
  MONGODB_DB_NAME: "ghostwire_test",
  LOG_LEVEL: "error",
  APP_ORIGIN: "https://ghostwire.test",
};

function stubEnv(env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): void {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(env)) vi.stubEnv(key, value);
  resetConfigForTests();
}

function stubExit() {
  return vi.spyOn(process, "exit").mockImplementation(((code?: number) => {
    throw new Error(`exit:${code}`);
  }) as never);
}

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  resetConfigForTests();
  setLogLevel("info");
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

describe("instrumentation register()", () => {
  it("skips config validation on the edge runtime", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv({ NEXT_RUNTIME: "edge", MONGODB_URI: "" });
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("skips config validation during the production build phase", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv({ NEXT_RUNTIME: "nodejs", NEXT_PHASE: "phase-production-build", MONGODB_URI: "" });
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("logs the invalid keys and exits with code 1 on the Node runtime when config is invalid", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    const error = vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    stubEnv({
      ...VALID,
      MONGODB_URI: "",
      NEXT_RUNTIME: "nodejs",
      NEXT_PHASE: "phase-production-server",
    });
    await expect(register()).rejects.toThrow("exit:1");
    expect(exit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const [message, fields] = error.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(message).toBe("invalid configuration, exiting");
    const logged = fields?.err;
    expect(logged).toBeInstanceOf(ConfigError);
    expect((logged as ConfigError).message).toContain("MONGODB_URI");
    expect((logged as ConfigError).message).not.toContain("ghostwire_test");
  });

  it("loads config on the Node runtime when config is valid", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv({ ...VALID, NEXT_RUNTIME: "nodejs", NEXT_PHASE: "phase-production-server" });
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import { afterAll, inject } from "vitest";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/modules/config";
import { closeDb, getDb } from "@/modules/database";

// @types/next types NODE_ENV as read-only, hence the cast.
(process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>).NODE_ENV = "test";
process.env.LOG_LEVEL = "error";
process.env.MONGODB_URI = inject("mongoUri");
// One database per test file, so files can run in parallel safely.
process.env.MONGODB_DB_NAME = `gw_test_${randomUUID().slice(0, 8)}`;
// Auth config (PR 02a). https gives the __Host- cookie path. Flows in one file share the
// "unknown" client-IP bucket, so the rate limits are raised; rate-limit tests lower them with useEnv.
process.env.APP_ORIGIN = "https://ghostwire.test";
process.env.SOLANA_NETWORKS = "devnet,mainnet-beta";
process.env.TRUST_PROXY = "false";
process.env.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN = "1000";
process.env.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS = "1000";
process.env.RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN = "1000";
resetConfigForTests();

afterAll(async () => {
  try {
    await (await getDb()).dropDatabase();
  } catch {
    // The server may have been stopped on purpose by the test file.
  }
  await closeDb();
  resetConfigForTests();
});
```

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { LOG_LEVELS } from "@/modules/shared";
import { solanaNetwork, type SolanaNetwork } from "@/modules/validation";

export const NODE_ENVS = ["development", "test", "production"] as const;
export type NodeEnv = (typeof NODE_ENVS)[number];

/** Schema key order; ConfigError issues are reported in this order. */
export const ENV_KEYS = [
  "NODE_ENV",
  "MONGODB_URI",
  "MONGODB_DB_NAME",
  "LOG_LEVEL",
  "APP_ORIGIN",
  "SOLANA_NETWORKS",
  "TRUST_PROXY",
  "SESSION_IDLE_DAYS",
  "SESSION_MAX_DAYS",
  "RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN",
  "RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS",
  "RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN",
] as const;
export type EnvKey = (typeof ENV_KEYS)[number];

/** Scheme check only; WHATWG URL rejects valid multi-host seed lists. The driver parses fully. */
const MONGODB_URI_PATTERN = /^mongodb(\+srv)?:\/\/\S+$/;
/** MongoDB forbids /\. "$*<>:|? in database names. */
const DB_NAME_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,63}$/;
const INTEGER_PATTERN = /^[0-9]{1,4}$/;

function integerIn(min: number, max: number) {
  return z
    .string()
    .regex(INTEGER_PATTERN)
    .transform(Number)
    .pipe(z.number().int().min(min).max(max));
}

/** scheme://host[:port] and nothing else: URL.origin must round-trip exactly. */
const appOrigin = z.string().refine((value) => {
  try {
    const url = new URL(value);
    return (url.protocol === "https:" || url.protocol === "http:") && url.origin === value;
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
});

const solanaNetworks = z
  .string()
  .transform((value) => value.split(",").map((part) => part.trim()))
  .pipe(z.array(solanaNetwork).min(1))
  .transform((list): readonly SolanaNetwork[] => Object.freeze(Array.from(new Set(list))));

const DEFAULT_SOLANA_NETWORKS: readonly SolanaNetwork[] = Object.freeze(["devnet", "mainnet-beta"]);

export const envSchema = z
  .object({
    NODE_ENV: z.enum(NODE_ENVS),
    MONGODB_URI: z.string().regex(MONGODB_URI_PATTERN),
    MONGODB_DB_NAME: z.string().regex(DB_NAME_PATTERN),
    LOG_LEVEL: z.enum(LOG_LEVELS).default("info"),
    APP_ORIGIN: appOrigin,
    SOLANA_NETWORKS: solanaNetworks.default(DEFAULT_SOLANA_NETWORKS),
    TRUST_PROXY: z
      .enum(["true", "false"])
      .default("false")
      .transform((value) => value === "true"),
    SESSION_IDLE_DAYS: integerIn(1, 30).default(7),
    SESSION_MAX_DAYS: integerIn(1, 90).default(30),
    RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: integerIn(1, 1000).default(10),
    RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS: integerIn(1, 1000).default(20),
    RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN: integerIn(1, 1000).default(10),
  })
  .superRefine(
    (config, ctx) => {
      if (config.SESSION_MAX_DAYS < config.SESSION_IDLE_DAYS) {
        ctx.addIssue({ code: "custom", path: ["SESSION_MAX_DAYS"], message: "max<idle" });
      }
    },
    // Compare the two lifetimes only once every field parsed, so an out-of-range
    // SESSION_IDLE_DAYS is reported on its own key and not also on SESSION_MAX_DAYS.
    { when: (payload) => payload.issues.length === 0 },
  );

/** Static reasons. They never contain the offending value. */
export const INVALID_REASONS: Readonly<Record<EnvKey, string>> = {
  NODE_ENV: "Invalid option: expected one of development|test|production",
  MONGODB_URI: "Must be a mongodb:// or mongodb+srv:// connection string",
  MONGODB_DB_NAME: "Must be 1-63 chars of A-Z a-z 0-9 _ -",
  LOG_LEVEL: "Invalid option: expected one of debug|info|warn|error",
  APP_ORIGIN: "Must be an http:// or https:// origin with no path, query or trailing slash",
  SOLANA_NETWORKS: "Comma list of devnet and/or mainnet-beta, non-empty",
  TRUST_PROXY: "Must be true or false",
  SESSION_IDLE_DAYS: "Integer 1-30",
  SESSION_MAX_DAYS: "Integer 1-90 and at least SESSION_IDLE_DAYS",
  RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: "Integer 1-1000",
  RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS: "Integer 1-1000",
  RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN: "Integer 1-1000",
};

export const REQUIRED_REASON = "Required";

export function isEnvKey(value: unknown): value is EnvKey {
  return typeof value === "string" && (ENV_KEYS as readonly string[]).includes(value);
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/modules/config/internal/load.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { ConfigError, setLogLevel, type ConfigIssue, type LogLevel } from "@/modules/shared";
import type { SolanaNetwork } from "@/modules/validation";
import {
  ENV_KEYS,
  INVALID_REASONS,
  REQUIRED_REASON,
  envSchema,
  isEnvKey,
  type EnvKey,
  type NodeEnv,
} from "./schema";

export interface AppConfig {
  readonly NODE_ENV: NodeEnv;
  readonly MONGODB_URI: string;
  readonly MONGODB_DB_NAME: string;
  readonly LOG_LEVEL: LogLevel;
  /** Public origin, scheme://host[:port]. https gives the __Host- Secure cookie. */
  readonly APP_ORIGIN: string;
  /** Frozen, de-duplicated, non-empty. */
  readonly SOLANA_NETWORKS: readonly SolanaNetwork[];
  readonly TRUST_PROXY: boolean;
  readonly SESSION_IDLE_DAYS: number;
  readonly SESSION_MAX_DAYS: number;
  readonly RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: number;
  readonly RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS: number;
  readonly RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN: number;
}

export type EnvSource = Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>;

let cached: AppConfig | undefined;

/**
 * With no argument: parses process.env once, memoizes, and applies LOG_LEVEL to the logger.
 * With an explicit env: pure, never reads or writes the cache.
 * @throws ConfigError listing failing key names and static reasons (never values).
 */
export function loadConfig(env?: EnvSource): AppConfig {
  if (env !== undefined) return parseConfig(env);
  if (cached === undefined) {
    const config = parseConfig(process.env);
    setLogLevel(config.LOG_LEVEL);
    cached = config;
  }
  return cached;
}

export function resetConfigForTests(): void {
  cached = undefined;
}

function parseConfig(env: EnvSource): AppConfig {
  const input: Partial<Record<EnvKey, string>> = {};
  for (const key of ENV_KEYS) {
    const raw = env[key];
    if (raw !== undefined && raw !== "") input[key] = raw;
  }
  const result = envSchema.safeParse(input);
  if (!result.success) {
    const failing = new Set(result.error.issues.map((issue) => issue.path[0]).filter(isEnvKey));
    const issues: ConfigIssue[] = ENV_KEYS.filter((key) => failing.has(key)).map((key) => ({
      key,
      reason: input[key] === undefined ? REQUIRED_REASON : INVALID_REASONS[key],
    }));
    throw new ConfigError(issues);
  }
  return Object.freeze({
    ...result.data,
    SOLANA_NETWORKS: Object.freeze([...result.data.SOLANA_NETWORKS]),
  });
}
```

```dotenv
MONGODB_DB_NAME=ghostwire_dev
# debug | info | warn | error (default: info)
```

### Task 6: Wallet crypto: base58 via bs58, Ed25519 verification and the sign-in message

Spec §3.2 (`wallet`), §5.1 and architecture §1.2–1.3, §3.5. Adds the one new dependency, strict base58 parsing (alphabet regex and length range before decoding, exact byte length after), `verifyEd25519` through a raw-key SPKI wrapper that never throws, and the exact sign-in message with a strict parser. Test helpers generate real Ed25519 keypairs and carry the RFC 8032 §7.1 vectors 1–3.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/helpers/ed25519.ts`, `tests/unit/wallet/base58.test.ts`, `tests/unit/wallet/ed25519.test.ts`, `tests/unit/wallet/sign-in-message.test.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/base58.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/address.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/ed25519.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/sign-in-message.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/wallet/index.ts`, `package.json`, `bun.lock`
- Test: `tests/unit/wallet/base58.test.ts`, `tests/unit/wallet/ed25519.test.ts`, `tests/unit/wallet/sign-in-message.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ValidationError` (`@/modules/shared`); `SOLANA_NETWORK_IDS`, `SolanaNetwork` (`@/modules/validation`); `bs58` (only in `internal/base58.ts`).

Create `tests/helpers/ed25519.ts`:

```ts
import { generateKeyPairSync, sign } from "node:crypto";
import bs58 from "bs58";

export interface TestWallet {
  /** bs58 of the raw 32-byte public key. */
  readonly address: string;
  readonly publicKey: Uint8Array;
  /** bs58 of the Ed25519 signature over the UTF-8 bytes of message. */
  sign(message: string): string;
  signBytes(message: Uint8Array): Uint8Array;
}

/** A fresh Ed25519 keypair shaped like a Solana wallet. Keys exist only in memory. */
export function createTestWallet(): TestWallet {
  const { publicKey, privateKey } = generateKeyPairSync("ed25519");
  const jwk = publicKey.export({ format: "jwk" });
  if (typeof jwk.x !== "string") throw new Error("ed25519 JWK export has no x");
  const raw = new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(jwk.x, "base64url"));
  const signBytes = (message: Uint8Array): Uint8Array =>
    new Uint8Array(sign(null, message, privateKey));
  return {
    address: bs58.encode(raw),
    publicKey: raw,
    signBytes,
    sign: (message) => bs58.encode(signBytes(new TextEncoder().encode(message))),
  };
}

export function base58(bytes: Uint8Array): string {
  return bs58.encode(bytes);
}

/** RFC 8032 §7.1 TEST 1-3 (hex). */
export const RFC8032_VECTORS: readonly {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly publicKey: string;
  readonly message: string;
  readonly signature: string;
}[] = [
  {
    name: "TEST 1 (empty message)",
    publicKey: "d75a980182b10ab7d54bfed3c964073a0ee172f3daa62325af021a68f707511a",
    message: "",
    signature:
      "e5564300c360ac729086e2cc806e828a84877f1eb8e5d974d873e065224901555fb8821590a33bacc61e39701cf9b46bd25bf5f0595bbe24655141438e7a100b",
  },
  {
    name: "TEST 2 (1 byte)",
    publicKey: "3d4017c3e843895a92b70aa74d1b7ebc9c982ccf2ec4968cc0cd55f12af4660c",
    message: "72",
    signature:
      "92a009a9f0d4cab8720e820b5f642540a2b27b5416503f8fb3762223ebdb69da085ac1e43e15996e458f3613d0f11d8c387b2eaeb4302aeeb00d291612bb0c00",
  },
  {
    name: "TEST 3 (2 bytes)",
    publicKey: "fc51cd8e6218a1a38da47ed00230f0580816ed13ba3303ac5deb911548908025",
    message: "af82",
    signature:
      "6291d657deec24024827e69c3abe01a30ce548a284743a445e3680d7db5ac3ac18ff9b538d16f290ae67f760984dc6594a7c15e9716ed28dc027beceea1ec40a",
  },
];
```

Create `tests/unit/wallet/base58.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { addressToPublicKey, parseSignature, parseSolanaAddress } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { base58, createTestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

function validationErrorOf(fn: () => unknown): ValidationError {
  try {
    fn();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ValidationError) return error;
    throw error;
  }
  throw new Error("expected a ValidationError");
}

describe("parseSolanaAddress", () => {
  it("accepts a real Ed25519 public key in base58 and round-trips to its bytes", () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const address = parseSolanaAddress(wallet.address);
    expect(address).toBe(wallet.address);
    expect(addressToPublicKey(address)).toEqual(wallet.publicKey);
  });

  it("handles leading zero bytes (leading 1s), including the all-zero key", () => {
    const zero = base58(new Uint8Array(32));
    expect(zero).toBe("11111111111111111111111111111111");
    expect(addressToPublicKey(parseSolanaAddress(zero))).toEqual(new Uint8Array(32));
    const leading = new Uint8Array(32).fill(7);
    leading[0] = 0;
    leading[1] = 0;
    const encoded = base58(leading);
    expect(encoded.startsWith("11")).toBe(true);
    expect(addressToPublicKey(parseSolanaAddress(encoded))).toEqual(leading);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "31 bytes", value: base58(new Uint8Array(31).fill(9)) },
    { label: "33 bytes", value: base58(new Uint8Array(33).fill(9)) },
    { label: "a 64-byte signature", value: base58(new Uint8Array(64).fill(9)) },
  ])("rejects a value that decodes to $label", ({ value }) => {
    expect(validationErrorOf(() => parseSolanaAddress(value)).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });

  it.each(["0", "O", "I", "l"])("rejects the non-alphabet character %s", (char) => {
    const valid = createTestWallet().address;
    const bad = `${char}${valid.slice(1)}`;
    expect(validationErrorOf(() => parseSolanaAddress(bad)).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "empty", value: "" },
    { label: "whitespace-padded", value: ` ${createTestWallet().address}` },
    { label: "too long", value: "2".repeat(45) },
    { label: "a number", value: 12345 },
    { label: "null", value: null },
    { label: "an object", value: { $ne: "" } },
  ])("rejects $label input", ({ value }) => {
    expect(validationErrorOf(() => parseSolanaAddress(value)).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });
});

describe("parseSignature", () => {
  it("accepts a 64-byte signature in base58 and returns its bytes", () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const bytes = wallet.signBytes(new TextEncoder().encode("hello"));
    expect(parseSignature(base58(bytes))).toEqual(bytes);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "63 bytes", value: base58(new Uint8Array(63).fill(5)) },
    { label: "65 bytes", value: base58(new Uint8Array(65).fill(5)) },
    { label: "a 32-byte key", value: base58(new Uint8Array(32).fill(5)) },
    { label: "a non-alphabet character", value: `0${base58(new Uint8Array(64).fill(5)).slice(1)}` },
    { label: "empty", value: "" },
    { label: "a non-string", value: ["x"] },
  ])("rejects $label", ({ value }) => {
    expect(validationErrorOf(() => parseSignature(value)).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/wallet/ed25519.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { verifyEd25519 } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { RFC8032_VECTORS, createTestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

const hex = (value: string): Uint8Array => new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(value, "hex"));
const utf8 = (value: string): Uint8Array => new TextEncoder().encode(value);

function flipBit(bytes: Uint8Array, index: number): Uint8Array {
  const copy = new Uint8Array(bytes);
  copy[index] = (copy[index] ?? 0) ^ 0x01;
  return copy;
}

describe("verifyEd25519", () => {
  it.each(RFC8032_VECTORS)("accepts RFC 8032 §7.1 $name", ({ publicKey, message, signature }) => {
    expect(verifyEd25519(hex(publicKey), hex(message), hex(signature))).toBe(true);
  });

  it.each(RFC8032_VECTORS)("rejects $name with one signature bit flipped", (vector) => {
    const signature = flipBit(hex(vector.signature), 10);
    expect(verifyEd25519(hex(vector.publicKey), hex(vector.message), signature)).toBe(false);
  });

  it("verifies a fresh keypair and rejects any one-bit change", () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const message = utf8("GHOST WIRE sign-in test message");
    const signature = wallet.signBytes(message);
    expect(verifyEd25519(wallet.publicKey, message, signature)).toBe(true);
    expect(verifyEd25519(wallet.publicKey, flipBit(message, 0), signature)).toBe(false);
    expect(verifyEd25519(wallet.publicKey, message, flipBit(signature, 63))).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects a signature from another key", () => {
    const signer = createTestWallet();
    const other = createTestWallet();
    const message = utf8("hello");
    expect(verifyEd25519(other.publicKey, message, signer.signBytes(message))).toBe(false);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "a 31-byte key", key: new Uint8Array(31), signature: new Uint8Array(64) },
    { label: "a 33-byte key", key: new Uint8Array(33), signature: new Uint8Array(64) },
    { label: "a 63-byte signature", key: new Uint8Array(32), signature: new Uint8Array(63) },
    { label: "an empty signature", key: new Uint8Array(32), signature: new Uint8Array(0) },
    { label: "an all-0xff key", key: new Uint8Array(32).fill(0xff), signature: new Uint8Array(64) },
  ])("returns false without throwing for $label", ({ key, signature }) => {
    expect(() => verifyEd25519(key, utf8("x"), signature)).not.toThrow();
    expect(verifyEd25519(key, utf8("x"), signature)).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/wallet/sign-in-message.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildSignInMessage,
  parseSignInMessage,
  parseSolanaAddress,
  type SignInMessageFields,
} from "@/modules/wallet";

const ADDRESS = parseSolanaAddress("11111111111111111111111111111111");

const FIELDS: SignInMessageFields = {
  purpose: "signin",
  domain: "ghostwire.test",
  address: ADDRESS,
  uri: "https://ghostwire.test",
  network: "devnet",
  nonce: "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA",
  issuedAt: new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"),
  expiresAt: new Date("2026-11-13T10:05:00.000Z"),
};

const GOLDEN_SIGNIN = [
  "ghostwire.test wants you to sign in with your Solana account:",
  "11111111111111111111111111111111",
  "",
  "Sign in to GHOST WIRE. This does not authorize any transaction or move funds.",
  "",
  "URI: https://ghostwire.test",
  "Version: 1",
  "Chain ID: solana:devnet",
  "Nonce: AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA",
  "Issued At: 2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z",
  "Expiration Time: 2026-11-13T10:05:00.000Z",
].join("\n");

describe("buildSignInMessage", () => {
  it("produces the exact spec §5.1 bytes for sign-in", () => {
    const message = buildSignInMessage(FIELDS);
    expect(message).toBe(GOLDEN_SIGNIN);
    expect(message.endsWith("\n")).toBe(false);
    expect(message.includes("\r")).toBe(false);
  });

  it("uses the link statement for link challenges", () => {
    const message = buildSignInMessage({ ...FIELDS, purpose: "link", network: "mainnet-beta" });
    expect(message.split("\n")[3]).toBe(
      "Link this wallet to your GHOST WIRE account. This does not authorize any transaction or move funds.",
    );
    expect(message.split("\n")[7]).toBe("Chain ID: solana:mainnet-beta");
  });
});

describe("parseSignInMessage", () => {
  it.each(["signin", "link"] as const)("round-trips a %s message", (purpose) => {
    const fields = { ...FIELDS, purpose };
    expect(parseSignInMessage(buildSignInMessage(fields))).toEqual(fields);
  });

  it("round-trips an http origin with a port", () => {
    const fields = { ...FIELDS, domain: "localhost:3000", uri: "http://localhost:3000" };
    expect(parseSignInMessage(buildSignInMessage(fields))).toEqual(fields);
  });

  const replaceLine = (index: number, value: string): string => {
    const lines = GOLDEN_SIGNIN.split("\n");
    lines[index] = value;
    return lines.join("\n");
  };

  it.each([
    { label: "an altered header", text: replaceLine(0, "ghostwire.test wants you to sign in:") },
    {
      label: "an empty domain",
      text: replaceLine(0, " wants you to sign in with your Solana account:"),
    },
    { label: "an invalid address", text: replaceLine(1, "0OIl1111111111111111111111111111") },
    { label: "a non-empty separator", text: replaceLine(2, "x") },
    { label: "a wrong statement", text: replaceLine(3, "Sign in to GHOST WIRE.") },
    { label: "a URI with whitespace", text: replaceLine(5, "URI: https://ghostwire.test x") },
    { label: "a missing URI prefix", text: replaceLine(5, "https://ghostwire.test") },
    { label: "Version 2", text: replaceLine(6, "Version: 2") },
    { label: "solana:testnet", text: replaceLine(7, "Chain ID: solana:testnet") },
    { label: "a non-solana chain", text: replaceLine(7, "Chain ID: eip155:1") },
    { label: "a short nonce", text: replaceLine(8, "Nonce: AAAA") },
    {
      label: "a nonce with a non-base64url char",
      text: replaceLine(8, "Nonce: AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA+"),
    },
    { label: "a timestamp without ms", text: replaceLine(9, "Issued At: 2026-11-13T10:00:00Z") },
    {
      label: "an offset timestamp",
      text: replaceLine(10, "Expiration Time: 2026-11-13T11:05:00.000+01:00"),
    },
    { label: "an extra line", text: `${GOLDEN_SIGNIN}\nResources: x` },
    { label: "a trailing newline", text: `${GOLDEN_SIGNIN}\n` },
    { label: "a missing line", text: GOLDEN_SIGNIN.split("\n").slice(0, 10).join("\n") },
    { label: "CRLF line endings", text: GOLDEN_SIGNIN.split("\n").join("\r\n") },
    { label: "an empty string", text: "" },
  ])("returns null for $label", ({ text }) => {
    expect(parseSignInMessage(text)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/base58.ts`:

```ts
import bs58 from "bs58";

/**
 * The only bs58 import in src/ (architecture §1.2). Returns null instead of throwing, so every
 * caller maps a failure to one ValidationError. Callers check the alphabet and length first.
 */
export function decodeBase58(value: string): Uint8Array | null {
  return bs58.decodeUnsafe(value) ?? null;
}
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/address.ts`:

```ts
import { ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { decodeBase58 } from "./base58";

declare const solanaAddressBrand: unique symbol;
/** A base58 string that decodes to exactly 32 bytes. Only parseSolanaAddress produces one. */
export type SolanaAddress = string & { readonly [solanaAddressBrand]: true };

/** Base58 alphabet (no 0, O, I, l); 32 bytes encode to 32-44 characters. */
export const ADDRESS_PATTERN = /^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/;
/** 64 bytes encode to 64-88 characters. */
const SIGNATURE_PATTERN = /^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{64,88}$/;
const PUBLIC_KEY_BYTES = 32;
const SIGNATURE_BYTES = 64;

function decodeExact(value: unknown, pattern: RegExp, bytes: number): Uint8Array | null {
  if (typeof value !== "string" || !pattern.test(value)) return null;
  const decoded = decodeBase58(value);
  return decoded !== null && decoded.length === bytes ? decoded : null;
}

/** @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED) unless value is base58 decoding to 32 bytes */
export function parseSolanaAddress(value: unknown): SolanaAddress {
  if (decodeExact(value, ADDRESS_PATTERN, PUBLIC_KEY_BYTES) === null) {
    throw new ValidationError({ message: "invalid Solana address" });
  }
  return value as SolanaAddress;
}

/** @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED) unless value is base58 decoding to 64 bytes */
export function parseSignature(value: unknown): Uint8Array {
  const decoded = decodeExact(value, SIGNATURE_PATTERN, SIGNATURE_BYTES);
  if (decoded === null) throw new ValidationError({ message: "invalid signature encoding" });
  return decoded;
}

/** Decodes an already-parsed address. Never throws; a corrupt value yields an empty array. */
export function addressToPublicKey(address: SolanaAddress): Uint8Array {
  return decodeBase58(address) ?? new Uint8Array(0);
}
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/ed25519.ts`:

```ts
import { createPublicKey, verify } from "node:crypto";

/** DER SubjectPublicKeyInfo prefix for a raw 32-byte Ed25519 key (RFC 8410). */
const ED25519_SPKI_PREFIX = Buffer.from("302a300506032b6570032100", "hex");

/**
 * True only for a valid Ed25519 signature of `message` by `publicKey`. Never throws: wrong
 * lengths, key bytes Node rejects, or anything else -> false.
 */
export function verifyEd25519(
  publicKey: Uint8Array,
  message: Uint8Array,
  signature: Uint8Array,
): boolean {
  if (publicKey.length !== 32 || signature.length !== 64) return false;
  try {
    const key = createPublicKey({
      key: Buffer.concat([ED25519_SPKI_PREFIX, publicKey]),
      format: "der",
      type: "spki",
    });
    return verify(null, message, key, signature);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/sign-in-message.ts`:

```ts
import { SOLANA_NETWORK_IDS, type SolanaNetwork } from "@/modules/validation";
import { ADDRESS_PATTERN, type SolanaAddress } from "./address";

export type SignInPurpose = "signin" | "link";

export interface SignInMessageFields {
  /** Selects the statement line. */
  readonly purpose: SignInPurpose;
  /** new URL(APP_ORIGIN).host */
  readonly domain: string;
  readonly address: SolanaAddress;
  /** APP_ORIGIN */
  readonly uri: string;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  /** 22-character base64url (16 random bytes). */
  readonly nonce: string;
  readonly issuedAt: Date;
  readonly expiresAt: Date;
}

const STATEMENTS: Readonly<Record<SignInPurpose, string>> = {
  signin: "Sign in to GHOST WIRE. This does not authorize any transaction or move funds.",
  link: "Link this wallet to your GHOST WIRE account. This does not authorize any transaction or move funds.",
};

const HEADER_SUFFIX = " wants you to sign in with your Solana account:";
const NONCE_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{22}$/;
const NO_WHITESPACE = /^\S+$/;
const LINE_COUNT = 11;

/** Exact spec §5.1 layout: "\n" line endings, no trailing newline. Pure. */
export function buildSignInMessage(fields: SignInMessageFields): string {
  return [
    `${fields.domain}${HEADER_SUFFIX}`,
    fields.address,
    "",
    STATEMENTS[fields.purpose],
    "",
    `URI: ${fields.uri}`,
    "Version: 1",
    `Chain ID: solana:${fields.network}`,
    `Nonce: ${fields.nonce}`,
    `Issued At: ${fields.issuedAt.toISOString()}`,
    `Expiration Time: ${fields.expiresAt.toISOString()}`,
  ].join("\n");
}

function after(line: string, prefix: string): string | null {
  return line.startsWith(prefix) ? line.slice(prefix.length) : null;
}

function purposeOf(statement: string): SignInPurpose | null {
  if (statement === STATEMENTS.signin) return "signin";
  if (statement === STATEMENTS.link) return "link";
  return null;
}

function networkOf(value: string | null): SolanaNetwork | null {
  return SOLANA_NETWORK_IDS.find((network) => network === value) ?? null;
}

/** Only an ISO-8601 UTC timestamp with milliseconds that round-trips through toISOString(). */
function isoDate(value: string | null): Date | null {
  if (value === null) return null;
  const date = new Date(value);
  return !Number.isNaN(date.getTime()) && date.toISOString() === value ? date : null;
}

/**
 * Inverse of buildSignInMessage. Returns null for any deviation: a CR anywhere, a line count
 * other than 11, an unknown statement, non-empty separator lines, Version other than 1, a Chain ID
 * that is not solana:<known network>, an address or nonce that fails its pattern, or timestamps
 * that do not round-trip. Invariant: parseSignInMessage(buildSignInMessage(f)) deep-equals f.
 */
export function parseSignInMessage(text: string): SignInMessageFields | null {
  if (text.includes("\r")) return null;
  const lines = text.split("\n");
  if (lines.length !== LINE_COUNT) return null;
  const line = (index: number): string => lines[index] ?? "";

  const domain = line(0).endsWith(HEADER_SUFFIX) ? line(0).slice(0, -HEADER_SUFFIX.length) : "";
  const address = line(1);
  const purpose = purposeOf(line(3));
  const uri = after(line(5), "URI: ");
  const network = networkOf(after(line(7), "Chain ID: solana:"));
  const nonce = after(line(8), "Nonce: ");
  const issuedAt = isoDate(after(line(9), "Issued At: "));
  const expiresAt = isoDate(after(line(10), "Expiration Time: "));

  if (!NO_WHITESPACE.test(domain) || !ADDRESS_PATTERN.test(address)) return null;
  if (line(2) !== "" || line(4) !== "" || line(6) !== "Version: 1") return null;
  if (purpose === null || uri === null || !NO_WHITESPACE.test(uri) || network === null) return null;
  if (nonce === null || !NONCE_PATTERN.test(nonce) || issuedAt === null || expiresAt === null) {
    return null;
  }
  return {
    purpose,
    domain,
    address: address as SolanaAddress,
    uri,
    network,
    nonce,
    issuedAt,
    expiresAt,
  };
}
```

### Task 7: Security module: tokens, Origin, client IP, body reader, rate limiting and security events

Spec §3.2 (`security`), §5.5 and architecture §3.6, §5.5, §5.7. The rate limiter is one atomic `findOneAndUpdate` upsert with `$inc` on `(sha256(key), windowStart)`, with one retry on a duplicate-key race. Security events are best effort: awaited, never rejecting, logged at `warn` on failure. `rateLimit` takes the request instant `at` as a fourth argument (architecture §3.6 clarification).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/security/tokens.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/origin.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/client-ip.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/fixed-window.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/read-json-body.test.ts`, `tests/integration/security/rate-limit.test.ts`, `tests/integration/security/security-events.test.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/tokens.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/origin.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/client-ip.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/body.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/events.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/rate-limit.ts`, `src/modules/security/internal/indexes.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/security/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/security/tokens.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/origin.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/client-ip.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/fixed-window.test.ts`, `tests/unit/security/read-json-body.test.ts`, `tests/integration/security/rate-limit.test.ts`, `tests/integration/security/security-events.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `safeEquals`, `typedCollection`, `objectIdSchema`, `registerIndexes`, `type ObjectId` (`@/modules/database`); `ConflictError`, `ForbiddenOriginError`, `RateLimitedError`, `ValidationError`, `logger` (`@/modules/shared`); `findOneAndUpdate` from Task 3.
- Produces from `@/modules/security` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3):
  - `randomToken(bytes: number): string` (integer bytes in [16, 64], else `RangeError`); `sha256Hex(value: string): string`.
  - `checkOrigin(request: Request, appOrigin: string): void` (GET/HEAD/OPTIONS pass; else `Origin` must `===` appOrigin, else `ForbiddenOriginError`).
  - `clientIp(request: Request, trustProxy: boolean): string` (`"unknown"` unless trusted and the first `X-Forwarded-For` entry passes `net.isIP`).
  - `MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES = 4096`; `readJsonBody(request: Request, maxBytes?: number): Promise<unknown>` (`ValidationError` for overflow, empty, non-UTF-8, non-JSON).
  - `interface FixedWindow { windowStart: Date; windowEnd: Date; retryAfterSeconds: number }`; `fixedWindow(at: Date, windowSeconds: number): FixedWindow` (windowSeconds integer in [1, 86400]).
  - `rateLimit(key: string, limit: number, windowSeconds: number, at: Date): Promise<void>` (throws `RateLimitedError`; logs `rate limited` with `{ route }` = the key prefix; records `rate_limited`).
  - `SECURITY_EVENT_TYPES`, `SECURITY_SEVERITIES`, `SECURITY_REASONS` (`challenge_unavailable`, `message_integrity`, `network_unsupported`, `signature_invalid`, `user_inactive`, `wallet_other_account`, `rate_limit_exceeded`, `logout`, `user_revoked`, `wallet_unlinked`) and their types; `interface SecurityEventInput { type; severity; userId: ObjectId | null; walletId: ObjectId | null; reason: SecurityReason | null; at: Date }`.
  - `recordSecurityEvent(event: SecurityEventInput): Promise<void>` (never rejects); `toSecurityReason(value: string): SecurityReason | null`.
  - `registerSecurityIndexes(): void` (`rate_limits_key_windowStart_unique`, `rate_limits_expiresAt_ttl` (0 s), `security_events_userId_createdAt`).

Create `tests/unit/security/origin.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { checkOrigin } from "@/modules/security";
import { ForbiddenOriginError } from "@/modules/shared";

const APP = "https://ghostwire.test";

function request(method: string, origin?: string): Request {
  const headers = origin === undefined ? undefined : { origin };
  return new Request(`${APP}/api/v1/x`, { method, headers });
}

describe("checkOrigin", () => {
  it.each(["GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"])("lets %s through without an Origin", (method) => {
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request(method), APP)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request(method, "https://evil.test"), APP)).not.toThrow();
  });

  it.each(["POST", "DELETE", "PUT", "PATCH"])("rejects %s with no Origin", (method) => {
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request(method), APP)).toThrow(ForbiddenOriginError);
  });

  it.each([
    "https://evil.test",
    "https://ghostwire.test/",
    "http://ghostwire.test",
    "https://ghostwire.test:443",
    "https://GHOSTWIRE.test",
    "null",
  ])("rejects a POST from Origin %s", (origin) => {
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request("POST", origin), APP)).toThrow(ForbiddenOriginError);
  });

  it("accepts a POST or DELETE whose Origin matches exactly", () => {
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request("POST", APP), APP)).not.toThrow();
    expect(() => checkOrigin(request("DELETE", APP), APP)).not.toThrow();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { clientIp } from "@/modules/security";

function request(forwardedFor?: string): Request {
  const headers = forwardedFor === undefined ? undefined : { "x-forwarded-for": forwardedFor };
  return new Request("https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/x", { headers });
}

describe("clientIp", () => {
  it("returns unknown when the proxy is not trusted, even with X-Forwarded-For", () => {
    expect(clientIp(request("203.0.113.7"), false)).toBe("unknown");
    expect(clientIp(request(), false)).toBe("unknown");
  });

  it("returns the first trimmed X-Forwarded-For entry when the proxy is trusted", () => {
    expect(clientIp(request("203.0.113.7"), true)).toBe("203.0.113.7");
    expect(clientIp(request("  198.51.100.2 , 10.0.0.1"), true)).toBe("198.51.100.2");
  });

  it("accepts IPv6", () => {
    expect(clientIp(request("2001:db8::1, 10.0.0.1"), true)).toBe("2001:db8::1");
  });

  it.each(["", "not-an-ip", "203.0.113.7:8080", "999.1.1.1", ", 203.0.113.7"])(
    "returns unknown for the trusted header %j",
    (header) => {
      expect(clientIp(request(header), true)).toBe("unknown");
    },
  );

  it("returns unknown when the trusted header is missing", () => {
    expect(clientIp(request(), true)).toBe("unknown");
  });
});
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { fixedWindow } from "@/modules/security";

describe("fixedWindow", () => {
  it("aligns a 60 s window to the minute", () => {
    const window = fixedWindow(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:30.250Z"), 60);
    expect(window.windowStart.toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    expect(window.windowEnd.toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:01:00.000Z");
    expect(window.retryAfterSeconds).toBe(30);
  });

  it("aligns a 3600 s window to the hour", () => {
    const window = fixedWindow(new Date("2026-11-13T10:59:59.000Z"), 3600);
    expect(window.windowStart.toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
    expect(window.windowEnd.toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T11:00:00.000Z");
    expect(window.retryAfterSeconds).toBe(1);
  });

  it("returns the full window at its first millisecond and 1 at its last", () => {
    expect(fixedWindow(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"), 60).retryAfterSeconds).toBe(60);
    expect(fixedWindow(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:59.999Z"), 60).retryAfterSeconds).toBe(1);
  });

  it("starts a new window exactly at the boundary", () => {
    const window = fixedWindow(new Date("2026-11-13T10:01:00.000Z"), 60);
    expect(window.windowStart.toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:01:00.000Z");
  });

  it.each([0, 86_401, 1.5, Number.NaN, -60])(
    "throws RangeError for windowSeconds %s",
    (seconds) => {
      expect(() => fixedWindow(new Date(), seconds)).toThrow(RangeError);
    },
  );
});
```

Create `tests/unit/security/read-json-body.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES, readJsonBody } from "@/modules/security";
import { ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";

const URL_ = "https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/x";

function post(body: BodyInit | null, headers?: Record<string, string>): Request {
  return new Request(URL_, { method: "POST", body, headers });
}

/** A JSON string literal of exactly `bytes` bytes. */
function jsonOfBytes(bytes: number): string {
  return `"${"a".repeat(bytes - 2)}"`;
}

async function validationErrorOf(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<ValidationError> {
  const error: unknown = await promise.catch((e: unknown) => e);
  if (!(error instanceof ValidationError)) throw new Error("expected a ValidationError");
  return error;
}

describe("readJsonBody", () => {
  it("parses a JSON body", async () => {
    expect(await readJsonBody(post('{"a":1,"b":[true]}'))).toEqual({ a: 1, b: [true] });
  });

  it("accepts exactly 4096 bytes", async () => {
    expect(MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES).toBe(4096);
    const body = jsonOfBytes(4096);
    expect(await readJsonBody(post(body))).toBe(body.slice(1, -1));
  });

  it("rejects 4097 bytes without a Content-Length", async () => {
    const error = await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(post(jsonOfBytes(4097))));
    expect(error.code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });

  it("rejects a declared Content-Length above the limit without reading the body", async () => {
    const request = post("{}", { "content-length": "1000000000" });
    await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(request));
    expect(request.bodyUsed).toBe(false);
  });

  it("rejects a non-numeric Content-Length", async () => {
    await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(post("{}", { "content-length": "abc" })));
  });

  it("cancels a chunked stream as soon as it passes the limit", async () => {
    let pulls = 0;
    let cancelled = false;
    const stream = new ReadableStream<Uint8Array>({
      pull(controller) {
        pulls += 1;
        controller.enqueue(new Uint8Array(1000).fill(0x20));
        if (pulls > 50) controller.close();
      },
      cancel() {
        cancelled = true;
      },
    });
    const request = new Request(URL_, {
      method: "POST",
      body: stream,
      duplex: "half",
    } as RequestInit);
    await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(request));
    expect(cancelled).toBe(true);
    expect(pulls).toBeLessThan(10);
  });

  it.each([
    { label: "a missing body", request: () => post(null) },
    { label: "an empty body", request: () => post("") },
    { label: "invalid JSON", request: () => post("{not json") },
    { label: "invalid UTF-8", request: () => post(new Uint8Array([0x22, 0xff, 0xfe, 0x22])) },
  ])("rejects $label with VALIDATION_FAILED", async ({ request }) => {
    const error = await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(request()));
    expect(error.code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });

  it("never puts body content in the error message", async () => {
    const error = await validationErrorOf(readJsonBody(post("{SENTINEL")));
    expect(error.message).not.toContain("SENTINEL");
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/security/rate-limit.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ensureIndexes, getDb } from "@/modules/database";
import { rateLimit, registerSecurityIndexes, sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import { RateLimitedError } from "@/modules/shared";

const AT = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:30.000Z");

async function rateLimitDocs(key: string) {
  return (await getDb())
    .collection("rate_limits")
    .find({ key: sha256Hex(key) })
    .toArray();
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  registerSecurityIndexes();
  await ensureIndexes();
});

describe("rateLimit", () => {
  it("allows `limit` hits and rejects the next one with the seconds left in the window", async () => {
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) await rateLimit("t1:ip:unknown", 3, 60, AT);
    const error: unknown = await rateLimit("t1:ip:unknown", 3, 60, AT).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(RateLimitedError);
    expect((error as RateLimitedError).retryAfterSeconds).toBe(30);
    const [doc] = await rateLimitDocs("t1:ip:unknown");
    expect(doc?.count).toBe(4);
  });

  it("stores only the SHA-256 of the key, with expiresAt at the window end", async () => {
    await rateLimit("t2:ip:203.0.113.9", 5, 60, AT);
    const all = await (await getDb()).collection("rate_limits").find({}).toArray();
    expect(JSON.stringify(all)).not.toContain("203.0.113.9");
    const [doc] = await rateLimitDocs("t2:ip:203.0.113.9");
    expect(doc?.windowStart).toEqual(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"));
    expect(doc?.expiresAt).toEqual(new Date("2026-11-13T10:01:00.000Z"));
  });

  it("counts 25 concurrent first hits of a new window exactly, without a ConflictError", async () => {
    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      Array.from({ length: 25 }, () => rateLimit("t3:ip:unknown", 1000, 60, AT)),
    );
    expect(results.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected")).toEqual([]);
    const docs = await rateLimitDocs("t3:ip:unknown");
    expect(docs).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(docs[0]?.count).toBe(25);
  });

  it("resets in the next window", async () => {
    await rateLimit("t4:ip:unknown", 1, 60, AT);
    await expect(rateLimit("t4:ip:unknown", 1, 60, AT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(RateLimitedError);
    await expect(
      rateLimit("t4:ip:unknown", 1, 60, new Date("2026-11-13T10:01:00.000Z")),
    ).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("keeps separate buckets per key", async () => {
    await rateLimit("t5:ip:a", 1, 60, AT);
    await expect(rateLimit("t5:ip:b", 1, 60, AT)).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("records a rate_limited security event with a static reason", async () => {
    await rateLimit("t6:ip:unknown", 1, 60, AT);
    await rateLimit("t6:ip:unknown", 1, 60, AT).catch(() => undefined);
    const event = await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("security_events")
      .findOne({ type: "rate_limited" });
    expect(event).toMatchObject({
      severity: "warning",
      reason: "rate_limit_exceeded",
      userId: null,
      walletId: null,
    });
  });

  it("throws RangeError for an invalid limit", async () => {
    await expect(rateLimit("t7:ip:unknown", 0, 60, AT)).rejects.toBeInstanceOf(RangeError);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { createHash, randomBytes } from "node:crypto";

/** base64url of crypto.randomBytes(bytes). @throws RangeError unless bytes is an integer in [16, 64] */
export function randomToken(bytes: number): string {
  if (!Number.isInteger(bytes) || bytes < 16 || bytes > 64) {
    throw new RangeError("bytes must be an integer in [16, 64]");
  }
  return randomBytes(bytes).toString("base64url");
}

/** Lowercase hex SHA-256 of the UTF-8 bytes of value. */
export function sha256Hex(value: string): string {
  return createHash("sha256").update(value, "utf8").digest("hex");
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/origin.ts`:

```ts
import { ForbiddenOriginError } from "@/modules/shared";

const SAFE_METHODS = new Set(["GET", "HEAD", "OPTIONS"]);

/**
 * Safe methods pass. Every other method needs an Origin header exactly equal to appOrigin.
 * @throws ForbiddenOriginError (403 ORIGIN_REJECTED); the rejected Origin is never logged
 */
export function checkOrigin(request: Request, appOrigin: string): void {
  if (SAFE_METHODS.has(request.method.toUpperCase())) return;
  if (request.headers.get("origin") !== appOrigin) throw new ForbiddenOriginError();
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/client-ip.ts`:

```ts
import { isIP } from "node:net";

const UNKNOWN = "unknown";

/**
 * trustProxy=false -> "unknown" (one shared bucket: fails safe toward stricter limits).
 * trustProxy=true -> the first comma-separated X-Forwarded-For entry, trimmed, only if
 * net.isIP() accepts it; otherwise "unknown". The result is used only inside rate-limit key
 * hashes and is never persisted or logged.
 */
export function clientIp(request: Request, trustProxy: boolean): string {
  if (!trustProxy) return UNKNOWN;
  const header = request.headers.get("x-forwarded-for");
  if (header === null) return UNKNOWN;
  const first = (header.split(",")[0] ?? "").trim();
  return isIP(first) === 0 ? UNKNOWN : first;
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/body.ts`:

```ts
import { ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";

export const MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES = 4096;
const DIGITS = /^[0-9]+$/;

function invalidBody(detail: string): ValidationError {
  return new ValidationError({ message: `invalid request body: ${detail}` });
}

/**
 * Rejects a Content-Length above maxBytes before reading. Otherwise reads the body stream,
 * cancelling it as soon as more than maxBytes bytes arrive, then decodes UTF-8 (fatal) and parses
 * JSON. Empty body, overflow, invalid UTF-8 or invalid JSON -> ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED).
 * Error messages never contain body content.
 */
export async function readJsonBody(
  request: Request,
  maxBytes: number = MAX_JSON_BODY_BYTES,
): Promise<unknown> {
  const declared = request.headers.get("content-length");
  if (declared !== null && (!DIGITS.test(declared) || Number(declared) > maxBytes)) {
    throw invalidBody("too large");
  }
  if (request.body === null) throw invalidBody("empty");
  const reader = request.body.getReader();
  const chunks: Uint8Array[] = [];
  let total = 0;
  for (;;) {
    const { done, value } = await reader.read();
    if (done) break;
    total += value.byteLength;
    if (total > maxBytes) {
      await reader.cancel().catch(() => undefined);
      throw invalidBody("too large");
    }
    chunks.push(value);
  }
  if (total === 0) throw invalidBody("empty");
  let text: string;
  try {
    text = new TextDecoder("utf-8", { fatal: true }).decode(Buffer.concat(chunks));
  } catch {
    throw invalidBody("not UTF-8");
  }
  try {
    return JSON.parse(text) as unknown;
  } catch {
    throw invalidBody("not JSON");
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/events.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { objectIdSchema, typedCollection, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { logger } from "@/modules/shared";

export const SECURITY_EVENT_TYPES = [
  "signin_success",
  "signin_failed",
  "wallet_linked",
  "link_failed",
  "wallet_unlinked",
  "session_revoked",
  "rate_limited",
] as const;
export type SecurityEventType = (typeof SECURITY_EVENT_TYPES)[number];

export const SECURITY_SEVERITIES = ["info", "warning", "critical"] as const;
export type SecuritySeverity = (typeof SECURITY_SEVERITIES)[number];

/** Static reasons only. Never a value derived from the request. */
export const SECURITY_REASONS = [
  "challenge_unavailable",
  "message_integrity",
  "network_unsupported",
  "signature_invalid",
  "user_inactive",
  "wallet_other_account",
  "rate_limit_exceeded",
  "logout",
  "user_revoked",
  "wallet_unlinked",
] as const;
export type SecurityReason = (typeof SECURITY_REASONS)[number];

export interface SecurityEventInput {
  readonly type: SecurityEventType;
  readonly severity: SecuritySeverity;
  readonly userId: ObjectId | null;
  readonly walletId: ObjectId | null;
  readonly reason: SecurityReason | null;
  readonly at: Date;
}

export const SECURITY_EVENTS_COLLECTION = "security_events";

const securityEventSchema = z.strictObject({
  type: z.enum(SECURITY_EVENT_TYPES),
  severity: z.enum(SECURITY_SEVERITIES),
  userId: objectIdSchema.nullable(),
  walletId: objectIdSchema.nullable(),
  reason: z.enum(SECURITY_REASONS).nullable(),
  createdAt: z.date(),
});

const events = typedCollection(SECURITY_EVENTS_COLLECTION, securityEventSchema);

/** The matching static reason, or null when value is not one. */
export function toSecurityReason(value: string): SecurityReason | null {
  return SECURITY_REASONS.find((reason) => reason === value) ?? null;
}

/**
 * Best effort and awaited: never rejects. A failed insert is logged at warn. Never call it inside
 * a withTransaction fn (it would roll back and could run more than once).
 */
export async function recordSecurityEvent(event: SecurityEventInput): Promise<void> {
  try {
    await events.insertOne({
      type: event.type,
      severity: event.severity,
      userId: event.userId,
      walletId: event.walletId,
      reason: event.reason,
      createdAt: event.at,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    logger.warn("security event write failed", { type: event.type, err: error });
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/rate-limit.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { safeEquals, typedCollection } from "@/modules/database";
import { ConflictError, RateLimitedError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import { recordSecurityEvent } from "./events";
import { sha256Hex } from "./tokens";

const HEX64 = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
const MAX_WINDOW_SECONDS = 86_400;

export const RATE_LIMITS_COLLECTION = "rate_limits";

const rateLimitSchema = z.strictObject({
  key: z.string().regex(HEX64),
  windowStart: z.date(),
  count: z.number().int().min(1),
  expiresAt: z.date(),
});

const rateLimits = typedCollection(RATE_LIMITS_COLLECTION, rateLimitSchema);

export interface FixedWindow {
  /** floor(at / windowSeconds) * windowSeconds */
  readonly windowStart: Date;
  /** windowStart + windowSeconds */
  readonly windowEnd: Date;
  /** max(1, ceil((windowEnd - at) / 1000)) */
  readonly retryAfterSeconds: number;
}

/** Pure window maths. @throws RangeError unless windowSeconds is an integer in [1, 86400] */
export function fixedWindow(at: Date, windowSeconds: number): FixedWindow {
  if (!Number.isInteger(windowSeconds) || windowSeconds < 1 || windowSeconds > MAX_WINDOW_SECONDS) {
    throw new RangeError(`windowSeconds must be an integer in [1, ${MAX_WINDOW_SECONDS}]`);
  }
  const windowMs = windowSeconds * 1000;
  const startMs = Math.floor(at.getTime() / windowMs) * windowMs;
  const endMs = startMs + windowMs;
  return {
    windowStart: new Date(startMs),
    windowEnd: new Date(endMs),
    retryAfterSeconds: Math.max(1, Math.ceil((endMs - at.getTime()) / 1000)),
  };
}

function isDuplicateKey(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ConflictError && error.code === "DUPLICATE_KEY";
}

/**
 * Counts one hit for `key` ("<route>:<kind>:<subject>", stored only as sha256Hex(key)) in the
 * current fixed window. Rejected hits still count, so a flood keeps its own bucket full.
 * @throws RangeError for an invalid limit or window
 * @throws RateLimitedError when the post-increment count exceeds limit (after a best-effort
 *         rate_limited event); DependencyUnavailableError when the database is down (fail closed)
 */
export async function rateLimit(
  key: string,
  limit: number,
  windowSeconds: number,
  at: Date,
): Promise<void> {
  if (!Number.isInteger(limit) || limit < 1) throw new RangeError("limit must be an integer >= 1");
  const window = fixedWindow(at, windowSeconds);
  const filter = Object.assign(
    {},
    safeEquals("key", sha256Hex(key)),
    safeEquals("windowStart", window.windowStart),
  );
  const update = { $inc: { count: 1 }, $setOnInsert: { expiresAt: window.windowEnd } };
  const options = { upsert: true, returnDocument: "after" } as const;
  let doc;
  try {
    doc = await rateLimits.findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, options);
  } catch (error) {
    // Concurrent first hits of a new window: the unique index admits one insert. The server
    // normally retries the losers itself; this single retry is defence in depth.
    if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
    doc = await rateLimits.findOneAndUpdate(filter, update, options);
  }
  if (doc === null || doc.count > limit) {
    logger.warn("rate limited", { route: key.split(":")[0] ?? "" });
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "rate_limited",
      severity: "warning",
      userId: null,
      walletId: null,
      reason: "rate_limit_exceeded",
      at,
    });
    throw new RateLimitedError(window.retryAfterSeconds);
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/security/internal/indexes.ts`:

```ts
import { registerIndexes } from "@/modules/database";
import { SECURITY_EVENTS_COLLECTION } from "./events";
import { RATE_LIMITS_COLLECTION } from "./rate-limit";

/** Registers the rate_limits and security_events indexes. Idempotent. Called by registerAuthIndexes(). */
export function registerSecurityIndexes(): void {
  registerIndexes(RATE_LIMITS_COLLECTION, [
    { name: "rate_limits_key_windowStart_unique", key: { key: 1, windowStart: 1 }, unique: true },
    { name: "rate_limits_expiresAt_ttl", key: { expiresAt: 1 }, expireAfterSeconds: 0 },
  ]);
  registerIndexes(SECURITY_EVENTS_COLLECTION, [
    { name: "security_events_userId_createdAt", key: { userId: 1, createdAt: -1 } },
  ]);
}
```

### Task 8: Collections, indexes, readiness, the boot hook and the test client

Spec §4 and architecture §3.8, §4, §6.1–6.2. Declares the `wallets` collection and repository, the three `auth` collections, every index, the memoized `ensureIndexesOnce`, the `ensureAuthReady` request gate and the `prepareDatabase` boot hook. `ensureIndexesOnce`'s memo lives in `index-memo.ts` so `closeDb()` can clear it without a `client.ts` ↔ `indexes.ts` import cycle. The repository adds `touchWallet` (not in the architecture): sign-in must write an already-active wallet without resetting `linkedAt`. Also adds the clock, env and HTTP test helpers and resets the clock after every integration test.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/integration/database/ensure-indexes-once.test.ts`, `tests/integration/wallet/repository.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/indexes.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/clock.ts`, `tests/helpers/env.ts`, `tests/helpers/http.ts`, `tests/unit/helpers/test-client.test.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/index-memo.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/collection.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/internal/repository.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/types.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/collections.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/indexes.ts`, `src/modules/auth/index.ts`
- Modify: `tests/unit/instrumentation.test.ts`, `tests/setup/integration.setup.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/indexes.ts`, `src/modules/database/internal/client.ts`, `src/modules/database/index.ts`, `src/modules/wallet/index.ts`, `src/instrumentation-node.ts`, `src/instrumentation.ts`
- Test: `tests/integration/database/ensure-indexes-once.test.ts`, `tests/integration/wallet/repository.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/indexes.test.ts`, `tests/unit/instrumentation.test.ts`, `tests/unit/helpers/test-client.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3 (`safeEquals`, `objectIdSchema`, typed operations), Task 5 (`solanaNetwork`), Task 6 (`ADDRESS_PATTERN`, `SolanaAddress`, `SolanaNetwork`), Task 7 (`registerSecurityIndexes`).
- Produces:
  - `@/modules/database`: `ensureIndexesOnce(): Promise<void>` (shared concurrent run; a rejection clears the memo; cleared by `closeDb()`, `resetIndexRegistryForTests()` and by registering a new spec name).
  - `@/modules/wallet`: `PROVIDER_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9_-]{1,32}$/`, `WALLET_STATUSES`, `type WalletStatus = "active" | "disconnected"`, `type WalletDoc`, `interface WalletView { id; address; network; provider: string | null; status; linkedAt: string }`, `registerWalletIndexes()`, `findWalletByAddress(address, network, options?: { session? })`, `findWalletForUser(walletId, userId, options?)`, `listWalletsByUser(userId, options?)` (linkedAt ascending, limit 100), `countActiveWallets(userId, { session })`, `insertWallet({ userId, address, network, provider, at }, { session })`, `setWalletStatus(walletId, status, at, { session, provider? })` ("active" also sets `linkedAt`, and `provider` when given), `touchWallet(walletId, at, { session })` (sets `updatedAt` only), `toWalletView(doc)`.
  - `@/modules/auth`: `registerAuthIndexes(): void`, `ensureAuthReady(): Promise<void>`, types `HandlerContext<P>`, `RouteHandler<P>`, `RequestMeta { at; ip; userAgent }`, `CurrentSession { id; userId; walletId; network; createdAt; expiresAt; absoluteExpiresAt }`, `AuthContext`, `SessionHandler<P>`, `ChallengeView`, `SignInResult`, `LinkResult`, `SessionView`, `MeView`, `SessionResolution { session; slid }`, `UnlinkResult`, `RevokeResult`, `UserDoc`, `ChallengeDoc`, `SessionDoc`. Internal: `users`, `challenges`, `sessions` typed collections and `USER_STATUSES`.
  - `src/instrumentation-node.ts`: `prepareDatabase(): Promise<void>` (`DependencyUnavailableError` → `logger.warn("database unavailable at boot; index creation deferred to first request", { err })`; anything else → `logger.error("index preparation failed, exiting", { err })` + `process.exit(1)`). `register()` awaits it after `failFastOnInvalidConfig()`.
