Create `tests/integration/database/ensure-indexes-once.test.ts`:

```ts
import { Collection } from "mongodb";
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import {
  closeDb,
  ensureIndexesOnce,
  getDb,
  registerIndexes,
  resetIndexRegistryForTests,
} from "@/modules/database";
import { InternalError } from "@/modules/shared";

const PROBE = "once_probe";

beforeEach(async () => {
  resetIndexRegistryForTests();
  await (await getDb()).dropCollection(PROBE).catch(() => undefined);
});

describe("ensureIndexesOnce", () => {
  it("shares one run between concurrent callers and memoizes success", async () => {
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Collection.prototype, "createIndexes");
    await Promise.all([ensureIndexesOnce(), ensureIndexesOnce(), ensureIndexesOnce()]);
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });

  it("re-runs after a new spec name is registered, but not for an identical one", async () => {
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Collection.prototype, "createIndexes");
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_other", key: { other: 1 } }]);
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("clears the memo after a rejection so the next call retries", async () => {
    const db = await getDb();
    await db.collection(PROBE).createIndex({ other: 1 }, { name: "once_ref" });
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    const error: unknown = await ensureIndexesOnce().catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(InternalError);
    expect((error as InternalError).code).toBe("INDEX_CONFLICT");
    await db.collection(PROBE).dropIndex("once_ref");
    await expect(ensureIndexesOnce()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });

  it("is cleared by closeDb()", async () => {
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Collection.prototype, "createIndexes");
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    await closeDb();
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });

  it("is cleared by resetIndexRegistryForTests()", async () => {
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    const spy = vi.spyOn(Collection.prototype, "createIndexes");
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    resetIndexRegistryForTests();
    registerIndexes(PROBE, [{ name: "once_ref", key: { ref: 1 } }]);
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
    expect(spy).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/wallet/repository.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeAll, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "@/modules/auth";
import { getDb, parseObjectId, withTransaction, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { ConflictError } from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  countActiveWallets,
  findWalletByAddress,
  findWalletForUser,
  insertWallet,
  listWalletsByUser,
  parseSolanaAddress,
  setWalletStatus,
  toWalletView,
  touchWallet,
} from "@/modules/wallet";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

const T0 = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
const T1 = new Date("2026-11-13T11:00:00.000Z");

function oid(hex: string): ObjectId {
  const id = parseObjectId(hex);
  if (id === null) throw new Error(`bad fixture id ${hex}`);
  return id;
}

const USER_A = oid("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60701");
const USER_B = oid("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f60702");

function newAddress() {
  return parseSolanaAddress(createTestWallet().address);
}

async function insert(userId: ObjectId, at = T0, network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet") {
  const address = newAddress();
  const doc = await withTransaction((session) =>
    insertWallet({ userId, address, network, provider: "phantom", at }, { session }),
  );
  return { address, doc };
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  await ensureAuthReady();
});

beforeEach(async () => {
  await (await getDb()).collection("wallets").deleteMany({});
});

describe("wallet repository", () => {
  it("inserts an active external wallet and finds it by (address, network)", async () => {
    const { address, doc } = await insert(USER_A);
    expect(doc).toMatchObject({
      userId: USER_A,
      address,
      network: "devnet",
      walletType: "external",
      provider: "phantom",
      status: "active",
      linkedAt: T0,
      updatedAt: T0,
    });
    expect(await findWalletByAddress(address, "devnet")).toEqual(doc);
    expect(await findWalletByAddress(address, "mainnet-beta")).toBeNull();
  });

  it("allows the same address on another network but not twice on one network", async () => {
    const { address } = await insert(USER_A);
    await expect(
      withTransaction((session) =>
        insertWallet(
          { userId: USER_B, address, network: "mainnet-beta", provider: null, at: T0 },
          { session },
        ),
      ),
    ).resolves.toMatchObject({ network: "mainnet-beta" });
    const error: unknown = await withTransaction((session) =>
      insertWallet(
        { userId: USER_B, address, network: "devnet", provider: null, at: T0 },
        { session },
      ),
    ).catch((e: unknown) => e);
    expect(error).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
    expect((error as ConflictError).code).toBe("DUPLICATE_KEY");
  });

  it("finds a wallet for its owner only", async () => {
    const { doc } = await insert(USER_A);
    expect(await findWalletForUser(doc._id, USER_A)).toEqual(doc);
    expect(await findWalletForUser(doc._id, USER_B)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("lists a user's wallets by linkedAt and counts the active ones", async () => {
    const second = await insert(USER_A, T1);
    const first = await insert(USER_A, T0);
    await insert(USER_B, T0);
    const listed = await listWalletsByUser(USER_A);
    expect(listed.map((w) => w._id.toHexString())).toEqual([
      first.doc._id.toHexString(),
      second.doc._id.toHexString(),
    ]);
    const active = await withTransaction((session) => countActiveWallets(USER_A, { session }));
    expect(active).toBe(2);
  });

  it("disconnects, then reactivates with linkedAt and provider reset", async () => {
    const { doc } = await insert(USER_A);
    const off = await withTransaction((session) =>
      setWalletStatus(doc._id, "disconnected", T1, { session }),
    );
    expect(off).toMatchObject({ status: "disconnected", updatedAt: T1, linkedAt: T0 });
    expect(await withTransaction((session) => countActiveWallets(USER_A, { session }))).toBe(0);
    const later = new Date("2026-11-13T12:00:00.000Z");
    const on = await withTransaction((session) =>
      setWalletStatus(doc._id, "active", later, { session, provider: "solflare" }),
    );
    expect(on).toMatchObject({ status: "active", linkedAt: later, provider: "solflare" });
  });

  it("touchWallet writes only updatedAt", async () => {
    const { doc } = await insert(USER_A);
    const touched = await withTransaction((session) => touchWallet(doc._id, T1, { session }));
    expect(touched).toEqual({ ...doc, updatedAt: T1 });
  });

  it("returns null when updating a wallet that does not exist", async () => {
    const missing = oid("64b7f0c2a1b2c3d4e5f607ff");
    expect(await withTransaction((session) => touchWallet(missing, T1, { session }))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("maps a wallet to its API view", async () => {
    const { address, doc } = await insert(USER_A);
    expect(toWalletView(doc)).toEqual({
      id: doc._id.toHexString(),
      address,
      network: "devnet",
      provider: "phantom",
      status: "active",
      linkedAt: "2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z",
    });
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/auth/indexes.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "@/modules/auth";
import { getDb } from "@/modules/database";

interface IndexInfo {
  name: string;
  key: Record<string, number>;
  unique?: boolean;
  expireAfterSeconds?: number;
}

async function indexesOf(collection: string): Promise<IndexInfo[]> {
  const list = (await (
    await getDb()
  )
    .collection(collection)
    .listIndexes()
    .toArray()) as IndexInfo[];
  return list
    .filter((index) => index.name !== "_id_")
    .map(({ name, key, unique, expireAfterSeconds }) => ({
      name,
      key,
      ...(unique === undefined ? {} : { unique }),
      ...(expireAfterSeconds === undefined ? {} : { expireAfterSeconds }),
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.name.localeCompare(b.name));
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  await ensureAuthReady();
});

describe("ensureAuthReady", () => {
  it.each([
    {
      collection: "wallets",
      expected: [
        { name: "wallets_address_network_unique", key: { address: 1, network: 1 }, unique: true },
        { name: "wallets_userId", key: { userId: 1 } },
      ],
    },
    {
      collection: "auth_challenges",
      expected: [
        { name: "auth_challenges_expiresAt_ttl", key: { expiresAt: 1 }, expireAfterSeconds: 3600 },
        { name: "auth_challenges_nonceHash_unique", key: { nonceHash: 1 }, unique: true },
      ],
    },
    {
      collection: "sessions",
      expected: [
        {
          name: "sessions_absoluteExpiresAt_ttl",
          key: { absoluteExpiresAt: 1 },
          expireAfterSeconds: 0,
        },
        { name: "sessions_tokenHash_unique", key: { tokenHash: 1 }, unique: true },
        { name: "sessions_userId", key: { userId: 1 } },
      ],
    },
    {
      collection: "rate_limits",
      expected: [
        { name: "rate_limits_expiresAt_ttl", key: { expiresAt: 1 }, expireAfterSeconds: 0 },
        {
          name: "rate_limits_key_windowStart_unique",
          key: { key: 1, windowStart: 1 },
          unique: true,
        },
      ],
    },
    {
      collection: "security_events",
      expected: [{ name: "security_events_userId_createdAt", key: { userId: 1, createdAt: -1 } }],
    },
  ])("creates exactly the $collection indexes", async ({ collection, expected }) => {
    expect(await indexesOf(collection)).toEqual(expected);
  });

  it("is idempotent", async () => {
    await expect(ensureAuthReady()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    await expect(ensureAuthReady()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
  });
});
```

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { register } from "@/instrumentation";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/modules/config";
import {
  ConfigError,
  DependencyUnavailableError,
  InternalError,
  logger,
  setLogLevel,
} from "@/modules/shared";

// The unit project has no database: ensureIndexesOnce is replaced, everything else is real.
const { ensureIndexesOnce } = vi.hoisted(() => ({
  ensureIndexesOnce: vi.fn<() => Promise<void>>(),
}));
vi.mock("@/modules/database", async (importOriginal) => ({
  ...(await importOriginal<typeof import("@/modules/database")>()),
  ensureIndexesOnce,
}));

/** Port 1: nothing listens there, so the unit project can never reach a developer mongod. */
const VALID = {
  NODE_ENV: "test",
  MONGODB_URI: "mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0",
  MONGODB_DB_NAME: "ghostwire_test",
  LOG_LEVEL: "error",
  APP_ORIGIN: "https://ghostwire.test",
};

const SERVER = { ...VALID, NEXT_RUNTIME: "nodejs", NEXT_PHASE: "phase-production-server" };

function stubEnv(env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): void {
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(env)) vi.stubEnv(key, value);
  resetConfigForTests();
}

function stubExit() {
  return vi.spyOn(process, "exit").mockImplementation(((code?: number) => {
    throw new Error(`exit:${code}`);
  }) as never);
}

beforeEach(() => {
  ensureIndexesOnce.mockReset();
  ensureIndexesOnce.mockResolvedValue(undefined);
});

afterEach(() => {
  vi.restoreAllMocks();
  resetConfigForTests();
  setLogLevel("info");
  vi.unstubAllEnvs();
});

describe("instrumentation register()", () => {
  it("skips config validation on the edge runtime", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv({ NEXT_RUNTIME: "edge", MONGODB_URI: "" });
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(ensureIndexesOnce).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("skips config validation during the production build phase", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv({ NEXT_RUNTIME: "nodejs", NEXT_PHASE: "phase-production-build", MONGODB_URI: "" });
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(ensureIndexesOnce).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("logs the invalid keys and exits with code 1 on the Node runtime when config is invalid", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    const error = vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    stubEnv({ ...SERVER, MONGODB_URI: "" });
    await expect(register()).rejects.toThrow("exit:1");
    expect(exit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
    const [message, fields] = error.mock.calls[0] ?? [];
    expect(message).toBe("invalid configuration, exiting");
    const logged = fields?.err;
    expect(logged).toBeInstanceOf(ConfigError);
    expect((logged as ConfigError).message).toContain("MONGODB_URI");
    expect((logged as ConfigError).message).not.toContain("ghostwire_test");
    expect(ensureIndexesOnce).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("loads config and prepares the database on the Node runtime when config is valid", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    stubEnv(SERVER);
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(ensureIndexesOnce).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1);
  });
});

describe("instrumentation prepareDatabase()", () => {
  it("logs at warn and keeps serving when the database is unavailable at boot", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    const warn = vi.spyOn(logger, "warn").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    ensureIndexesOnce.mockRejectedValue(new DependencyUnavailableError("database"));
    stubEnv(SERVER);
    await expect(register()).resolves.toBeUndefined();
    expect(exit).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
    expect(warn).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "database unavailable at boot; index creation deferred to first request",
      expect.objectContaining({ err: expect.any(DependencyUnavailableError) }),
    );
  });

  it("logs at error and exits with code 1 on INDEX_CONFLICT", async () => {
    const exit = stubExit();
    const error = vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const conflict = new InternalError({ code: "INDEX_CONFLICT" });
    ensureIndexesOnce.mockRejectedValue(conflict);
    stubEnv(SERVER);
    await expect(register()).rejects.toThrow("exit:1");
    expect(exit).toHaveBeenCalledWith(1);
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledWith("index preparation failed, exiting", { err: conflict });
  });
});
```

Create `tests/helpers/clock.ts`:

```ts
import { setClockForTests } from "@/modules/shared";

export interface TestClock {
  now(): Date;
  advance(ms: number): void;
  set(at: Date): void;
}

/**
 * Drives the shared clock (now()) from a test. Only application time moves; the MongoDB driver
 * and timers keep real time. The integration setup resets the clock after every test; unit
 * tests call resetClockForTests() themselves.
 */
export function useTestClock(start: Date): TestClock {
  let current = start.getTime();
  setClockForTests(() => new Date(current));
  return {
    now: () => new Date(current),
    advance(ms) {
      current += ms;
    },
    set(at) {
      current = at.getTime();
    },
  };
}
```

Create `tests/helpers/env.ts`:

```ts
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/modules/config";

/**
 * Sets (or, for undefined, deletes) process.env keys and drops the memoized config.
 * Returns a restore function that puts the previous values back and drops the config again.
 */
export function useEnv(overrides: Readonly<Record<string, string | undefined>>): () => void {
  const env = process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>;
  const previous = new Map<string, string | undefined>();
  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(overrides)) {
    previous.set(key, env[key]);
    if (value === undefined) delete env[key];
    else env[key] = value;
  }
  resetConfigForTests();
  return () => {
    for (const [key, value] of previous) {
      if (value === undefined) delete env[key];
      else env[key] = value;
    }
    resetConfigForTests();
  };
}
```

Create `tests/helpers/http.ts`:

```ts
import type { RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";

export const TEST_APP_ORIGIN = "https://ghostwire.test";

export interface CallOptions {
  readonly method: "GET" | "POST" | "DELETE";
  /** e.g. "/api/v1/auth/challenge" */
  readonly path: string;
  /** JSON.stringify'd, with Content-Type: application/json. */
  readonly body?: unknown;
  /** Sent as-is, for size and garbage tests. Wins over body. */
  readonly rawBody?: string;
  /** Becomes { params: Promise.resolve(params) }. */
  readonly params?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  /** Default: the client's appOrigin for non-GET, none for GET. null omits the header. */
  readonly origin?: string | null;
  readonly headers?: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
}

export interface CallResult {
  readonly status: number;
  readonly headers: Headers;
  readonly setCookies: string[];
  /** null for an empty body (204). */
  readonly json: unknown;
}

const SESSION_SET_COOKIE = /^(__Host-gw_session|gw_session)=([^;]*)/;

/**
 * Calls route handlers directly with real Request objects and keeps a one-cookie jar for the
 * session cookie: every call sends it, and every Set-Cookie for it updates it
 * (Max-Age=0 or an empty value clears it).
 */
export class TestClient {
  private readonly appOrigin: string;
  private token: string | null = null;

  constructor(options: { appOrigin?: string } = {}) {
    this.appOrigin = options.appOrigin ?? TEST_APP_ORIGIN;
  }

  /** The raw session token currently in the jar. */
  get cookie(): string | null {
    return this.token;
  }

  /** Puts a raw value in the jar, for forged, expired or foreign-token tests. */
  setCookie(value: string | null): void {
    this.token = value;
  }

  private cookieName(): string {
    return this.appOrigin.startsWith("https://") ? "__Host-gw_session" : "gw_session";
  }

  async call<P extends object>(
    handler: RouteHandler<P>,
    options: CallOptions,
  ): Promise<CallResult> {
    const headers = new Headers(options.headers);
    const origin =
      options.origin === undefined
        ? options.method === "GET"
          ? null
          : this.appOrigin
        : options.origin;
    if (origin !== null) headers.set("origin", origin);
    if (this.token !== null) headers.set("cookie", `${this.cookieName()}=${this.token}`);
    let body: string | undefined;
    if (options.rawBody !== undefined) {
      body = options.rawBody;
    } else if (options.body !== undefined) {
      body = JSON.stringify(options.body);
      headers.set("content-type", "application/json");
    }
    const request = new Request(new URL(options.path, this.appOrigin), {
      method: options.method,
      headers,
      body,
    });
    const params = Promise.resolve((options.params ?? {}) as P);
    const response = await handler(request, { params });
    const setCookies = response.headers.getSetCookie();
    for (const value of setCookies) {
      const match = SESSION_SET_COOKIE.exec(value);
      if (match === null) continue;
      const cookieValue = match[2] ?? "";
      this.token = cookieValue === "" || /;\s*Max-Age=0(;|$)/i.test(value) ? null : cookieValue;
    }
    const text = await response.text();
    return {
      status: response.status,
      headers: response.headers,
      setCookies,
      json: text === "" ? null : (JSON.parse(text) as unknown),
    };
  }
}
```

Create `tests/unit/helpers/test-client.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import type { RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { ConfigError, now, resetClockForTests } from "@/modules/shared";
import { useTestClock } from "../../helpers/clock";
import { useEnv } from "../../helpers/env";
import { TestClient } from "../../helpers/http";

const TOKEN = "A".repeat(43);

/** Echoes what it received and sets whatever cookie the test asks for. */
const echo: RouteHandler<{ id: string }> = async (request, context) => {
  const { id } = await context.params;
  const setCookie = request.headers.get("x-set-cookie");
  const headers = new Headers({ "content-type": "application/json" });
  if (setCookie !== null) headers.append("Set-Cookie", setCookie);
  return new Response(
    JSON.stringify({
      method: request.method,
      url: request.url,
      origin: request.headers.get("origin"),
      cookie: request.headers.get("cookie"),
      body: request.body === null ? null : await request.text(),
      id: id ?? null,
    }),
    { status: 200, headers },
  );
};

afterEach(() => {
  resetClockForTests();
});

describe("TestClient", () => {
  it("sends Origin on non-GET only, by default", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const post = await client.call(echo, { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/x" });
    const get = await client.call(echo, { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/x" });
    expect(post.json).toMatchObject({ origin: "https://ghostwire.test", method: "POST" });
    expect(get.json).toMatchObject({ origin: null, url: "https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/x" });
  });

  it("omits Origin for null and overrides it with a string", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const none = await client.call(echo, { method: "POST", path: "/x", origin: null });
    const evil = await client.call(echo, { method: "POST", path: "/x", origin: "https://e.test" });
    expect(none.json).toMatchObject({ origin: null });
    expect(evil.json).toMatchObject({ origin: "https://e.test" });
  });

  it("serializes body as JSON, sends rawBody as-is, and passes params", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const json = await client.call(echo, { method: "POST", path: "/x", body: { a: 1 } });
    const raw = await client.call(echo, { method: "POST", path: "/x", rawBody: "{bad" });
    const withId = await client.call(echo, { method: "DELETE", path: "/x/1", params: { id: "1" } });
    expect(json.json).toMatchObject({ body: '{"a":1}' });
    expect(raw.json).toMatchObject({ body: "{bad" });
    expect(withId.json).toMatchObject({ id: "1" });
  });

  it("stores the session cookie from Set-Cookie, sends it back, and clears it on Max-Age=0", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    await client.call(echo, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/x",
      headers: { "x-set-cookie": `__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}; Path=/; Max-Age=60; HttpOnly` },
    });
    expect(client.cookie).toBe(TOKEN);
    const next = await client.call(echo, { method: "GET", path: "/x" });
    expect(next.json).toMatchObject({ cookie: `__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}` });
    await client.call(echo, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/x",
      headers: { "x-set-cookie": "__Host-gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly" },
    });
    expect(client.cookie).toBeNull();
  });

  it("uses gw_session for an http origin and ignores unrelated cookies", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient({ appOrigin: "http://localhost:3000" });
    client.setCookie(TOKEN);
    const result = await client.call(echo, {
      method: "GET",
      path: "/x",
      headers: { "x-set-cookie": "other=1; Path=/" },
    });
    expect(result.json).toMatchObject({ cookie: `gw_session=${TOKEN}` });
    expect(client.cookie).toBe(TOKEN);
  });
});

describe("useTestClock and useEnv", () => {
  it("drives now() and restores env values", () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"));
    clock.advance(1500);
    expect(now().toISOString()).toBe("2026-11-13T10:00:01.500Z");
    clock.set(new Date("2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z"));
    expect(now().toISOString()).toBe("2027-01-01T00:00:00.000Z");

    const restore = useEnv({
      NODE_ENV: "test",
      MONGODB_URI: "mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0",
      MONGODB_DB_NAME: "helpers_test",
      APP_ORIGIN: "https://ghostwire.test",
      SESSION_IDLE_DAYS: "3",
      GW_HELPER_PROBE: undefined,
    });
    expect(loadConfig().SESSION_IDLE_DAYS).toBe(3);
    restore();
    // The unit project has no MONGODB_URI, so after restore the config is invalid again.
    expect(() => loadConfig()).toThrow(ConfigError);
  });
});
```

```ts
});
```

Create `src/modules/database/internal/index-memo.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The ensureIndexesOnce() memo, in its own file so client.ts (closeDb) and indexes.ts can both
 * clear it without importing each other.
 */
let memo: Promise<void> | undefined;

export function readIndexMemo(): Promise<void> | undefined {
  return memo;
}

export function writeIndexMemo(value: Promise<void> | undefined): void {
  memo = value;
}

export function clearIndexMemo(): void {
  memo = undefined;
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/modules/database/internal/indexes.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { MongoServerError, type IndexDescription } from "mongodb";
import { InternalError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { getDb } from "./client";
import { mapMongoError } from "./errors";
import { clearIndexMemo, readIndexMemo, writeIndexMemo } from "./index-memo";
import { assertCollectionName } from "./names";

export type IndexDirection = 1 | -1;

export interface IndexSpec {
  /** /^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,64}$/, unique per collection. */
  readonly name: string;
  /** At least one field; insertion order is the index key order. */
  readonly key: Readonly<Record<string, IndexDirection>>;
  readonly unique?: boolean;
  readonly sparse?: boolean;
  /** Integer >= 0. */
  readonly expireAfterSeconds?: number;
  readonly partialFilterExpression?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>;
}

const INDEX_NAME = /^[A-Za-z0-9_]{1,64}$/;
const DIRECTIONS = new Set<unknown>([1, -1]);
/** IndexOptionsConflict (85) and IndexKeySpecsConflict (86). */
const INDEX_CONFLICT_CODES = new Set<unknown>([85, 86]);

const registry = new Map<string, Map<string, IndexSpec>>();

function invalid(collection: string, detail: string): InternalError {
  return new InternalError({ code: "INDEX_SPEC_INVALID", message: `${collection}: ${detail}` });
}

function validateSpec(collection: string, spec: IndexSpec): void {
  if (!INDEX_NAME.test(spec.name)) {
    throw invalid(collection, `invalid index name ${JSON.stringify(spec.name)}`);
  }
  const fields = Object.entries(spec.key);
  if (fields.length === 0) throw invalid(collection, `index ${spec.name} has no key fields`);
  if (fields.some(([, direction]) => !DIRECTIONS.has(direction))) {
    throw invalid(collection, `index ${spec.name} directions must be 1 or -1`);
  }
  const ttl = spec.expireAfterSeconds;
  if (ttl !== undefined && (!Number.isInteger(ttl) || ttl < 0)) {
    throw invalid(collection, `index ${spec.name} expireAfterSeconds must be an integer >= 0`);
  }
}

/** Order-sensitive for `key` (index key order matters); identical source literals always match. */
function fingerprint(spec: IndexSpec): string {
  return JSON.stringify([
    Object.entries(spec.key),
    spec.unique ?? false,
    spec.sparse ?? false,
    spec.expireAfterSeconds ?? null,
    spec.partialFilterExpression ?? null,
  ]);
}

/**
 * All-or-nothing per call. Re-registering an identical spec is a no-op (safe under HMR).
 * Registering a spec name that was not registered before clears the ensureIndexesOnce() memo.
 * @throws InternalError INDEX_REGISTRY_CONFLICT | INDEX_SPEC_INVALID | COLLECTION_NAME_INVALID
 */
export function registerIndexes(collection: string, specs: readonly IndexSpec[]): void {
  assertCollectionName(collection);
  const current = registry.get(collection) ?? new Map<string, IndexSpec>();
  const pending = new Map<string, IndexSpec>();
  for (const spec of specs) {
    validateSpec(collection, spec);
    const prior = pending.get(spec.name) ?? current.get(spec.name);
    if (prior !== undefined && fingerprint(prior) !== fingerprint(spec)) {
      throw new InternalError({
        code: "INDEX_REGISTRY_CONFLICT",
        message: `${collection}.${spec.name} is already registered with a different spec`,
      });
    }
    pending.set(spec.name, Object.freeze({ ...spec, key: Object.freeze({ ...spec.key }) }));
  }
  if (pending.size === 0) return;
  let added = false;
  for (const [name, spec] of pending) {
    if (!current.has(name)) added = true;
    current.set(name, spec);
  }
  registry.set(collection, current);
  if (added) clearIndexMemo();
}

function toIndexDescription(spec: IndexSpec): IndexDescription {
  return {
    name: spec.name,
    key: { ...spec.key },
    ...(spec.unique === undefined ? {} : { unique: spec.unique }),
    ...(spec.sparse === undefined ? {} : { sparse: spec.sparse }),
    ...(spec.expireAfterSeconds === undefined
      ? {}
      : { expireAfterSeconds: spec.expireAfterSeconds }),
    ...(spec.partialFilterExpression === undefined
      ? {}
      : { partialFilterExpression: { ...spec.partialFilterExpression } }),
  };
}

/**
 * Creates every registered index. Names are explicit, so a second run is a server no-op.
 * Application code calls ensureIndexesOnce() instead (boot hook and the auth request gate).
 * @throws InternalError("INDEX_CONFLICT") on server codes 85/86; DependencyUnavailableError on connectivity
 */
export async function ensureIndexes(): Promise<void> {
  if (registry.size === 0) return;
  const db = await getDb();
  for (const [collection, specs] of registry) {
    try {
      await db.collection(collection).createIndexes([...specs.values()].map(toIndexDescription));
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof MongoServerError && INDEX_CONFLICT_CODES.has(error.code)) {
        throw new InternalError({
          code: "INDEX_CONFLICT",
          message: `${collection}: an existing index conflicts with the registered spec; write an index migration`,
          cause: error,
        });
      }
      throw mapMongoError(error);
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Memoized ensureIndexes(). Concurrent callers share one run. A rejection clears the memo so the
 * next call retries. The memo is also cleared by closeDb(), resetIndexRegistryForTests() and
 * whenever registerIndexes() adds a spec name that was not registered before.
 * @throws same as ensureIndexes()
 */
export function ensureIndexesOnce(): Promise<void> {
  const existing = readIndexMemo();
  if (existing !== undefined) return existing;
  const run = ensureIndexes();
  writeIndexMemo(run);
  run.catch(() => {
    if (readIndexMemo() === run) clearIndexMemo();
  });
  return run;
}

export function resetIndexRegistryForTests(): void {
  registry.clear();
  clearIndexMemo();
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/modules/database/internal/client.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { MongoClient, type Db, type MongoClientOptions } from "mongodb";
import { loadConfig, type AppConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { ConfigError, DependencyUnavailableError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import { clearIndexMemo } from "./index-memo";

/** Every external call is bounded (architecture §5.2). */
const CLIENT_OPTIONS: MongoClientOptions = {
  appName: "ghostwire",
  serverSelectionTimeoutMS: 2000,
  connectTimeoutMS: 2000,
  socketTimeoutMS: 30_000,
  maxPoolSize: 10,
  minPoolSize: 0,
  maxIdleTimeMS: 60_000,
  retryWrites: true,
  retryReads: true,
};

interface ClientEntry {
  readonly mongo: MongoClient;
  readonly connected: Promise<MongoClient>;
}

/** Development only: survives Next dev hot reloads so pools are not leaked. */
const GLOBAL_KEY = Symbol.for("ghostwire.mongoClient");
const globalSlot = globalThis as unknown as Record<symbol, ClientEntry | undefined>;
let moduleEntry: ClientEntry | undefined;

function readEntry(): ClientEntry | undefined {
  return moduleEntry ?? globalSlot[GLOBAL_KEY];
}

function writeEntry(entry: ClientEntry | undefined, config?: AppConfig): void {
  moduleEntry = entry;
  if (entry === undefined || config?.NODE_ENV === "development") globalSlot[GLOBAL_KEY] = entry;
}

function createClient(uri: string): MongoClient {
  try {
    return new MongoClient(uri, CLIENT_OPTIONS);
  } catch {
    // The driver's parse error can echo the URI, so it is deliberately not attached as cause.
    throw new ConfigError([{ key: "MONGODB_URI", reason: "Rejected by the MongoDB driver" }]);
  }
}

async function connect(mongo: MongoClient): Promise<MongoClient> {
  try {
    return await mongo.connect();
  } catch (cause) {
    if (readEntry()?.mongo === mongo) writeEntry(undefined);
    await mongo.close().catch(() => undefined);
    throw new DependencyUnavailableError("database", {
      message: "MongoDB connection failed",
      cause,
    });
  }
}

/**
 * Internal: the shared connected client. Concurrent callers share one connect; a failed connect
 * clears the cache so the next call retries.
 * @throws ConfigError, DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function getClient(): Promise<MongoClient> {
  const config = loadConfig();
  const existing = readEntry();
  if (existing !== undefined) return existing.connected;
  const mongo = createClient(config.MONGODB_URI);
  const entry: ClientEntry = { mongo, connected: connect(mongo) };
  writeEntry(entry, config);
  return entry.connected;
}

/** @throws DependencyUnavailableError (connect failed/timed out), ConfigError (invalid config) */
export async function getDb(): Promise<Db> {
  const client = await getClient();
  return client.db(loadConfig().MONGODB_DB_NAME);
}

/**
 * Idempotent; never throws (close errors are logged at warn). Also clears the
 * ensureIndexesOnce() memo, because the next client may point at another server.
 */
export async function closeDb(): Promise<void> {
  const entry = readEntry();
  writeEntry(undefined);
  clearIndexMemo();
  if (entry === undefined) return;
  try {
    await entry.mongo.close();
  } catch (error) {
    logger.warn("mongodb close failed", { err: error });
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/collection.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { objectIdSchema, registerIndexes, typedCollection, type WithId } from "@/modules/database";
import { solanaNetwork } from "@/modules/validation";
import { ADDRESS_PATTERN } from "./address";

export const WALLETS_COLLECTION = "wallets";
export const WALLET_STATUSES = ["active", "disconnected"] as const;
export type WalletStatus = (typeof WALLET_STATUSES)[number];
/** Informational wallet label, e.g. "phantom". Never trusted for behaviour. */
export const PROVIDER_PATTERN = /^[a-z0-9_-]{1,32}$/;

/** No .default() and no .refine(): invariants are enforced by the code that writes. */
export const walletSchema = z.strictObject({
  userId: objectIdSchema,
  address: z.string().regex(ADDRESS_PATTERN),
  network: solanaNetwork,
  walletType: z.literal("external"),
  provider: z.string().regex(PROVIDER_PATTERN).nullable(),
  status: z.enum(WALLET_STATUSES),
  linkedAt: z.date(),
  updatedAt: z.date(),
});

export type WalletDoc = WithId<z.output<typeof walletSchema>>;

export const wallets = typedCollection(WALLETS_COLLECTION, walletSchema);

/** Registers the wallets indexes. Idempotent. Called by registerAuthIndexes(). */
export function registerWalletIndexes(): void {
  registerIndexes(WALLETS_COLLECTION, [
    { name: "wallets_address_network_unique", key: { address: 1, network: 1 }, unique: true },
    { name: "wallets_userId", key: { userId: 1 } },
  ]);
}
```

Create `src/modules/wallet/internal/repository.ts`:

```ts
import { safeEquals, type ClientSession, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import type { SolanaNetwork } from "@/modules/validation";
import type { SolanaAddress } from "./address";
import { wallets, type WalletDoc, type WalletStatus } from "./collection";

const MAX_WALLETS_LISTED = 100;

export interface WalletView {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly address: string;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  readonly provider: string | null;
  readonly status: WalletStatus;
  /** ISO-8601 */
  readonly linkedAt: string;
}

export function findWalletByAddress(
  address: SolanaAddress,
  network: SolanaNetwork,
  options: { session?: ClientSession } = {},
): Promise<WalletDoc | null> {
  return wallets.findOne(
    Object.assign({}, safeEquals("address", address), safeEquals("network", network)),
    { session: options.session },
  );
}

export function findWalletForUser(
  walletId: ObjectId,
  userId: ObjectId,
  options: { session?: ClientSession } = {},
): Promise<WalletDoc | null> {
  return wallets.findOne(
    Object.assign({}, safeEquals("_id", walletId), safeEquals("userId", userId)),
    { session: options.session },
  );
}

/** Sorted by linkedAt ascending, limit 100. */
export function listWalletsByUser(
  userId: ObjectId,
  options: { session?: ClientSession } = {},
): Promise<WalletDoc[]> {
  return wallets.find(safeEquals("userId", userId), {
    limit: MAX_WALLETS_LISTED,
    sort: { linkedAt: 1, _id: 1 },
    session: options.session,
  });
}

export function countActiveWallets(
  userId: ObjectId,
  options: { session: ClientSession },
): Promise<number> {
  return wallets.countDocuments(
    Object.assign({}, safeEquals("userId", userId), { status: "active" as const }),
    {
      session: options.session,
    },
  );
}

/** @throws ConflictError("DUPLICATE_KEY") when (address, network) already exists */
export function insertWallet(
  input: {
    userId: ObjectId;
    address: SolanaAddress;
    network: SolanaNetwork;
    provider: string | null;
    at: Date;
  },
  options: { session: ClientSession },
): Promise<WalletDoc> {
  return wallets.insertOne(
    {
      userId: input.userId,
      address: input.address,
      network: input.network,
      walletType: "external",
      provider: input.provider,
      status: "active",
      linkedAt: input.at,
      updatedAt: input.at,
    },
    { session: options.session },
  );
}

/**
 * Sets status and updatedAt=at. Setting "active" is a (re)link: it also sets linkedAt=at, and
 * provider when options.provider is given. Returns the updated doc, or null if it does not exist.
 */
export function setWalletStatus(
  walletId: ObjectId,
  status: WalletStatus,
  at: Date,
  options: { session: ClientSession; provider?: string | null },
): Promise<WalletDoc | null> {
  const $set =
    status === "active"
      ? Object.assign(
          { status, updatedAt: at, linkedAt: at },
          options.provider === undefined ? {} : { provider: options.provider },
        )
      : { status, updatedAt: at };
  return wallets.findOneAndUpdate(
    safeEquals("_id", walletId),
    { $set },
    {
      session: options.session,
    },
  );
}

/**
 * Writes updatedAt=at and nothing else. Sign-in calls it on an already-active wallet so that a
 * concurrent unlink of the same wallet write-conflicts with the sign-in (architecture §5.3).
 */
export function touchWallet(
  walletId: ObjectId,
  at: Date,
  options: { session: ClientSession },
): Promise<WalletDoc | null> {
  return wallets.findOneAndUpdate(
    safeEquals("_id", walletId),
    { $set: { updatedAt: at } },
    {
      session: options.session,
    },
  );
}

export function toWalletView(doc: WalletDoc): WalletView {
  return {
    id: doc._id.toHexString(),
    address: doc.address,
    network: doc.network,
    provider: doc.provider,
    status: doc.status,
    linkedAt: doc.linkedAt.toISOString(),
  };
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/types.ts`:

```ts
import type { ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import type { SolanaNetwork, WalletView } from "@/modules/wallet";

/** Named HandlerContext, not RouteContext, to avoid Next's global RouteContext<'/route'>. */
export interface HandlerContext<P extends object = object> {
  readonly params: Promise<P>;
}

/** The shape Next 16 route handlers satisfy: (request, { params: Promise<P> }) => Promise<Response>. */
export type RouteHandler<P extends object = object> = (
  request: Request,
  context: HandlerContext<P>,
) => Promise<Response>;

export interface RequestMeta {
  /** now() once per request. */
  readonly at: Date;
  /** clientIp(request, TRUST_PROXY). Never persisted or logged. */
  readonly ip: string;
  /** Control characters stripped, at most 128 characters, "" -> null. */
  readonly userAgent: string | null;
}

export interface CurrentSession {
  readonly id: ObjectId;
  readonly userId: ObjectId;
  readonly walletId: ObjectId;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  readonly createdAt: Date;
  /** After sliding. */
  readonly expiresAt: Date;
  readonly absoluteExpiresAt: Date;
}

export interface AuthContext extends RequestMeta {
  readonly session: CurrentSession;
}

export type SessionHandler<P extends object = object> = (
  request: Request,
  auth: AuthContext,
  context: HandlerContext<P>,
) => Promise<Response>;

export interface ChallengeView {
  readonly challengeId: string;
  readonly message: string;
  readonly expiresAt: string;
}

export interface SignInResult {
  readonly body: {
    user: { id: string };
    wallet: { id: string; address: string; network: SolanaNetwork };
  };
  readonly setCookie: string;
}

export interface LinkResult {
  readonly wallet: WalletView;
  readonly created: boolean;
}

export interface SessionView {
  readonly id: string;
  readonly current: boolean;
  readonly createdAt: string;
  readonly lastSeenAt: string;
  readonly expiresAt: string;
  readonly userAgent: string | null;
}

export interface MeView {
  readonly user: { id: string; createdAt: string };
  readonly session: { id: string; network: SolanaNetwork; expiresAt: string };
  readonly wallets: WalletView[];
}

export interface SessionResolution {
  readonly session: CurrentSession;
  readonly slid: boolean;
}

export interface UnlinkResult {
  readonly revokedCurrent: boolean;
}

export interface RevokeResult {
  readonly revokedCurrent: boolean;
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/collections.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { objectIdSchema, typedCollection, type WithId } from "@/modules/database";
import { SOLANA_NETWORK_IDS } from "@/modules/wallet";

const HEX64 = /^[0-9a-f]{64}$/;
const ADDRESS = /^[1-9A-HJ-NP-Za-km-z]{32,44}$/;
const network = z.enum(SOLANA_NETWORK_IDS);

export const USERS_COLLECTION = "users";
export const CHALLENGES_COLLECTION = "auth_challenges";
export const SESSIONS_COLLECTION = "sessions";

export const USER_STATUSES = ["active", "suspended", "deleted"] as const;

/** No .default() and no .refine() in any schema: invariants are enforced by the writers. */
export const userSchema = z.strictObject({
  status: z.enum(USER_STATUSES),
  createdAt: z.date(),
  updatedAt: z.date(),
});

export const challengeSchema = z.strictObject({
  /** sha256Hex(nonce). The raw nonce exists only inside `message`. */
  nonceHash: z.string().regex(HEX64),
  /** The exact signed text. */
  message: z.string().min(1).max(1024),
  address: z.string().regex(ADDRESS),
  network,
  purpose: z.enum(["signin", "link"]),
  /** Set for link (the session user), null for signin. */
  userId: objectIdSchema.nullable(),
  createdAt: z.date(),
  expiresAt: z.date(),
  consumedAt: z.date().nullable(),
});

export const sessionSchema = z.strictObject({
  /** sha256Hex(token). The raw token exists only in the cookie. */
  tokenHash: z.string().regex(HEX64),
  userId: objectIdSchema,
  /** The wallet used to sign in. */
  walletId: objectIdSchema,
  network,
  createdAt: z.date(),
  lastSeenAt: z.date(),
  /** Idle expiry, sliding (written at most once per hour). */
  expiresAt: z.date(),
  absoluteExpiresAt: z.date(),
  revokedAt: z.date().nullable(),
  userAgent: z.string().max(128).nullable(),
});

export type UserDoc = WithId<z.output<typeof userSchema>>;
export type ChallengeDoc = WithId<z.output<typeof challengeSchema>>;
export type SessionDoc = WithId<z.output<typeof sessionSchema>>;

export const users = typedCollection(USERS_COLLECTION, userSchema);
export const challenges = typedCollection(CHALLENGES_COLLECTION, challengeSchema);
export const sessions = typedCollection(SESSIONS_COLLECTION, sessionSchema);
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/indexes.ts`:

```ts
import { ensureIndexesOnce, registerIndexes } from "@/modules/database";
import { registerSecurityIndexes } from "@/modules/security";
import { registerWalletIndexes } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { CHALLENGES_COLLECTION, SESSIONS_COLLECTION } from "./collections";

/**
 * Registers every index the auth flows rely on: wallets, rate_limits, security_events,
 * auth_challenges and sessions. users has none. Idempotent (identical specs are a no-op).
 */
export function registerAuthIndexes(): void {
  registerWalletIndexes();
  registerSecurityIndexes();
  registerIndexes(CHALLENGES_COLLECTION, [
    { name: "auth_challenges_nonceHash_unique", key: { nonceHash: 1 }, unique: true },
    { name: "auth_challenges_expiresAt_ttl", key: { expiresAt: 1 }, expireAfterSeconds: 3600 },
  ]);
  registerIndexes(SESSIONS_COLLECTION, [
    { name: "sessions_tokenHash_unique", key: { tokenHash: 1 }, unique: true },
    { name: "sessions_userId", key: { userId: 1 } },
    {
      name: "sessions_absoluteExpiresAt_ttl",
      key: { absoluteExpiresAt: 1 },
      expireAfterSeconds: 0,
    },
  ]);
}

/**
 * The request gate: the unique indexes are correctness constraints, so every auth service
 * function awaits this before its first database operation. A resolved memo after the first
 * success. @throws InternalError("INDEX_CONFLICT"), DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function ensureAuthReady(): Promise<void> {
  registerAuthIndexes();
  await ensureIndexesOnce();
}
```

```ts
} from "./internal/types";
```

Replace the whole of `src/instrumentation-node.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { registerAuthIndexes } from "@/modules/auth";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { ensureIndexesOnce } from "@/modules/database";
import { ConfigError, DependencyUnavailableError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";

/**
 * Node-only boot check, kept in its own file so the Edge instrumentation bundle never
 * contains process.exit. Next 16 catches an error thrown from register() and keeps serving,
 * so an invalid config logs the key names and exits with code 1 instead.
 */
export function failFastOnInvalidConfig(): void {
  try {
    loadConfig();
  } catch (error) {
    if (!(error instanceof ConfigError)) throw error;
    logger.error("invalid configuration, exiting", { err: error });
    process.exit(1);
  }
}

/**
 * Creates the auth indexes at boot. Database unreachable -> logged at warn and deferred to the
 * first request (ensureAuthReady), because a database outage must not crash-loop the app.
 * Anything else (INDEX_CONFLICT: an index migration is required, or an invalid spec) -> logged
 * at error and process.exit(1), like bad config.
 */
export async function prepareDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  try {
    registerAuthIndexes();
    await ensureIndexesOnce();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof DependencyUnavailableError) {
      logger.warn("database unavailable at boot; index creation deferred to first request", {
        err: error,
      });
      return;
    }
    logger.error("index preparation failed, exiting", { err: error });
    process.exit(1);
  }
}
```

Replace the whole of `src/instrumentation.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
/**
 * Fail fast on invalid config and create the auth indexes at server boot (`next start` /
 * `next dev`). Skipped during `next build`, so a fresh clone builds without a .env. The Node-only
 * work lives in ./instrumentation-node behind a positive NEXT_RUNTIME check, so the Edge bundle
 * excludes it. Next runtime file: reading process.env here is an allowed exception to the
 * config-only rule.
 */
export async function register(): Promise<void> {
  if (process.env.NEXT_RUNTIME === "nodejs") {
    if (process.env.NEXT_PHASE === "phase-production-build") return;
    const { failFastOnInvalidConfig, prepareDatabase } = await import("./instrumentation-node");
    failFastOnInvalidConfig();
    await prepareDatabase();
  }
}
```

### Task 9: Auth core: challenge issue and atomic consume, sign-in, and sessions

Spec §5.2, §5.4 and architecture §5.1, §5.2, §5.4. The challenge is consumed by one `findOneAndUpdate` outside the sign-in transaction, so a bad signature still burns it. The sign-in transaction always writes the wallet document. **Gap filled:** MongoDB skips an update that sets a field to its current value, and a skipped update takes no write lock, so a guard write equal to the stored value cannot conflict. `strictlyAfter(at, previous)` makes every guard write change the document (used here by sign-in and in Task 12 by unlink). `ensureAuthReady()` runs first in every service function, before the rate-limit upsert, so the `rate_limits` unique index exists before its first write (architecture §3.8 wins over the ordering sketch in §5.1).

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/auth/cookies.test.ts`, `tests/unit/auth/request.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/sign-in.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/sessions.test.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/errors.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/guard.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/request.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/cookies.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/challenges.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/sessions.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/sign-in.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/auth/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/auth/cookies.test.ts`, `tests/unit/auth/request.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/sign-in.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/sessions.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3–8 (database, shared, config, wallet, security, `users`/`challenges`/`sessions`, `ensureAuthReady`).
- Produces from `@/modules/auth` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3):
  - `sessionCookieName(appOrigin: string): "__Host-gw_session" | "gw_session"` and `type SessionCookieName`; `buildSessionCookie(token: string, maxAgeSeconds: number, appOrigin: string): string`; `clearSessionCookie(appOrigin: string): string`; `readSessionToken(request: Request, appOrigin: string): string | null` (first well-formed 43-char base64url value under the configured name).
  - `requestMeta(request: Request): RequestMeta` (reads `TRUST_PROXY` per call; user agent control-stripped, ≤ 128 chars, `""` → null).
  - `issueChallenge(body: unknown, meta: RequestMeta): Promise<ChallengeView>`; `verifySignIn(body: unknown, meta: RequestMeta): Promise<SignInResult>`.
  - `createSession(input: { tokenHash; userId; walletId; network; userAgent; at }, options: { session: ClientSession }): Promise<SessionDoc>`; `resolveSession(token: string | null, at: Date): Promise<SessionResolution | null>`; `logout(auth: AuthContext): Promise<void>`.
  - Internal, used by Tasks 10–12: `networkUnsupported()`, `walletAlreadyLinked()`, `lastWallet()`, `authFailed(reason: SecurityReason)` (`errors.ts`); `strictlyAfter(at: Date, previous: Date): Date` (`guard.ts`); `parseChallengeBody(body, allowed)`, `parseVerifyBody(body)` (`request.ts`); `insertChallenge({ purpose, address, network, userId, at })`, `consumeChallenge({ challengeId, purpose, userId, at })`, `checkChallenge(doc, signature, allowed): SolanaAddress`, `CHALLENGE_TTL_MS = 300_000` (`challenges.ts`); `hasSessionCookie(response, appOrigin)`, `idleMaxAgeSeconds(idleDays)`, `remainingMaxAgeSeconds(expiresAt, at)` (`cookies.ts`).

Create `tests/unit/auth/cookies.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  buildSessionCookie,
  clearSessionCookie,
  readSessionToken,
  sessionCookieName,
} from "@/modules/auth";

const HTTPS = "https://ghostwire.test";
const HTTP = "http://localhost:3000";
const TOKEN = "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzABCDEFGHIJKLMNO_-";
const OTHER = "Z".repeat(43);

function withCookie(cookie: string): Request {
  return new Request(`${HTTPS}/api/v1/me`, { headers: { cookie } });
}

describe("session cookie strings", () => {
  it("uses __Host-gw_session with Secure for an https origin", () => {
    expect(sessionCookieName(HTTPS)).toBe("__Host-gw_session");
    expect(buildSessionCookie(TOKEN, 604_800, HTTPS)).toBe(
      `__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}; Path=/; Max-Age=604800; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax`,
    );
    expect(clearSessionCookie(HTTPS)).toBe(
      "__Host-gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax",
    );
  });

  it("uses gw_session without Secure for an http origin", () => {
    expect(sessionCookieName(HTTP)).toBe("gw_session");
    expect(buildSessionCookie(TOKEN, 60, HTTP)).toBe(
      `gw_session=${TOKEN}; Path=/; Max-Age=60; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax`,
    );
    expect(clearSessionCookie(HTTP)).toBe("gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly; SameSite=Lax");
  });
});

describe("readSessionToken", () => {
  it("reads the configured cookie among others", () => {
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(`a=1; __Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}; b=2`), HTTPS)).toBe(TOKEN);
  });

  it("ignores the other name for the configured origin", () => {
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(`gw_session=${TOKEN}`), HTTPS)).toBeNull();
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(`__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}`), HTTP)).toBeNull();
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(`gw_session=${TOKEN}`), HTTP)).toBe(TOKEN);
  });

  it.each([
    "__Host-gw_session=short",
    `__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}x`,
    `__Host-gw_session=${TOKEN.slice(0, 42)}!`,
    "__Host-gw_session=",
    "__Host-gw_session",
  ])("ignores a malformed value: %s", (cookie) => {
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(cookie), HTTPS)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("takes the first well-formed duplicate", () => {
    const cookie = `__Host-gw_session=bad; __Host-gw_session=${TOKEN}; __Host-gw_session=${OTHER}`;
    expect(readSessionToken(withCookie(cookie), HTTPS)).toBe(TOKEN);
  });

  it("returns null without a Cookie header", () => {
    expect(readSessionToken(new Request(`${HTTPS}/x`), HTTPS)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `tests/unit/auth/request.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import { requestMeta } from "@/modules/auth";
import { resetClockForTests } from "@/modules/shared";
import { useTestClock } from "../../helpers/clock";
import { useEnv } from "../../helpers/env";

/** Port 1: nothing listens there; requestMeta never connects anyway. */
const UNIT_ENV = {
  NODE_ENV: "test",
  MONGODB_URI: "mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0",
  MONGODB_DB_NAME: "auth_unit",
  APP_ORIGIN: "https://ghostwire.test",
};

let restore: (() => void) | undefined;

afterEach(() => {
  restore?.();
  restore = undefined;
  resetClockForTests();
});

function request(headers: Record<string, string>): Request {
  return new Request("https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/x", { headers });
}

describe("requestMeta", () => {
  it("takes `at` from the shared clock and ignores X-Forwarded-For without TRUST_PROXY", () => {
    restore = useEnv({ ...UNIT_ENV, TRUST_PROXY: "false" });
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"));
    const meta = requestMeta(request({ "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.7", "user-agent": "UA/1" }));
    expect(meta).toEqual({
      at: new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"),
      ip: "unknown",
      userAgent: "UA/1",
    });
  });

  it("uses X-Forwarded-For with TRUST_PROXY=true", () => {
    restore = useEnv({ ...UNIT_ENV, TRUST_PROXY: "true" });
    expect(requestMeta(request({ "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.7" })).ip).toBe("203.0.113.7");
  });

  it("strips control characters, caps the user agent at 128 chars and maps empty to null", () => {
    restore = useEnv(UNIT_ENV);
    expect(requestMeta(request({ "user-agent": `a\tb${"x".repeat(200)}` })).userAgent).toBe(
      `ab${"x".repeat(126)}`,
    );
    expect(requestMeta(request({ "user-agent": "\t" })).userAgent).toBeNull();
    expect(requestMeta(request({})).userAgent).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/auth/sign-in.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  issueChallenge,
  resolveSession,
  verifySignIn,
  type ChallengeView,
  type RequestMeta,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { getDb, parseObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import { AuthFailedError, ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { buildSignInMessage, parseSignInMessage } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { createTestWallet, type TestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

const T0 = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");

function meta(at: Date = T0): RequestMeta {
  return { at, ip: "unknown", userAgent: "vitest" };
}

function tokenOf(setCookie: string): string {
  const match = /^__Host-gw_session=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43});/.exec(setCookie);
  if (match?.[1] === undefined) throw new Error(`no session token in ${setCookie}`);
  return match[1];
}

async function challengeFor(wallet: TestWallet, network = "devnet"): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  return issueChallenge({ address: wallet.address, network }, meta());
}

async function errorOf(promise: Promise<unknown>): Promise<unknown> {
  return promise.catch((e: unknown) => e);
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  const db = await getDb();
  for (const name of ["users", "wallets", "auth_challenges", "sessions", "security_events"]) {
    await db.collection(name).deleteMany({});
  }
});

describe("issueChallenge", () => {
  it("stores the challenge with only the nonce hash and returns the exact message", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    expect(view.challengeId).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{24}$/);
    expect(view.expiresAt).toBe("2026-11-13T10:05:00.000Z");
    const fields = parseSignInMessage(view.message);
    expect(fields).not.toBeNull();
    expect(view.message).toBe(
      buildSignInMessage({
        purpose: "signin",
        domain: "ghostwire.test",
        address: wallet.address as never,
        uri: "https://ghostwire.test",
        network: "devnet",
        nonce: fields?.nonce ?? "",
        issuedAt: T0,
        expiresAt: new Date("2026-11-13T10:05:00.000Z"),
      }),
    );
    const stored = await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("auth_challenges")
      .findOne({ _id: parseObjectId(view.challengeId) ?? undefined });
    expect(stored).toMatchObject({
      nonceHash: sha256Hex(fields?.nonce ?? ""),
      message: view.message,
      address: wallet.address,
      network: "devnet",
      purpose: "signin",
      userId: null,
      createdAt: T0,
      consumedAt: null,
    });
    expect(Object.values(stored ?? {})).not.toContain(fields?.nonce);
  });

  it("rejects a malformed address and an unsupported network", async () => {
    const bad = await errorOf(issueChallenge({ address: "0x123", network: "devnet" }, meta()));
    expect(bad).toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
    expect((bad as ValidationError).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
    const testnet = await errorOf(challengeFor(createTestWallet(), "testnet"));
    expect((testnet as ValidationError).code).toBe("NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED");
    const nonString = await errorOf(
      issueChallenge({ address: createTestWallet().address, network: 1 }, meta()),
    );
    expect((nonString as ValidationError).code).toBe("VALIDATION_FAILED");
  });
});

describe("verifySignIn", () => {
  it("creates the user, wallet and session on first sign-in", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const result = await verifySignIn(
      { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message), provider: "phantom" },
      meta(),
    );
    expect(result.body.wallet).toMatchObject({ address: wallet.address, network: "devnet" });
    expect(result.setCookie).toMatch(
      /^__Host-gw_session=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}; Path=\/; Max-Age=604800; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax$/,
    );
    const token = tokenOf(result.setCookie);
    const db = await getDb();
    const session = await db.collection("sessions").findOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(token) });
    expect(session).toMatchObject({
      network: "devnet",
      createdAt: T0,
      lastSeenAt: T0,
      expiresAt: new Date("2026-11-20T10:00:00.000Z"),
      absoluteExpiresAt: new Date("2026-12-13T10:00:00.000Z"),
      revokedAt: null,
      userAgent: "vitest",
    });
    expect(session?.userId.toHexString()).toBe(result.body.user.id);
    const stored = await db.collection("wallets").findOne({ address: wallet.address });
    expect(stored).toMatchObject({ provider: "phantom", status: "active", walletType: "external" });
    const events = await db.collection("security_events").find({}).toArray();
    expect(events.map((e) => e.type)).toEqual(["signin_success"]);
  });

  it("signs the same wallet in to the same user without resetting linkedAt", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const first = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const one = await verifySignIn(
      { challengeId: first.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(first.message) },
      meta(),
    );
    const later = new Date("2026-11-14T10:00:00.000Z");
    const second = await issueChallenge(
      { address: wallet.address, network: "devnet" },
      meta(later),
    );
    const two = await verifySignIn(
      { challengeId: second.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(second.message) },
      meta(later),
    );
    expect(two.body.user.id).toBe(one.body.user.id);
    expect(two.body.wallet.id).toBe(one.body.wallet.id);
    const stored = await (await getDb()).collection("wallets").findOne({ address: wallet.address });
    expect(stored).toMatchObject({ linkedAt: T0, updatedAt: later });
    expect(await (await getDb()).collection("users").countDocuments({})).toBe(1);
  });

  it("burns the challenge on a bad signature and records signin_failed", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const forged = await errorOf(
      verifySignIn(
        { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: createTestWallet().sign(view.message) },
        meta(),
      ),
    );
    expect(forged).toBeInstanceOf(AuthFailedError);
    expect((forged as AuthFailedError).reason).toBe("signature_invalid");
    const replay = await errorOf(
      verifySignIn({ challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message) }, meta()),
    );
    expect((replay as AuthFailedError).reason).toBe("challenge_unavailable");
    const reasons = (await (await getDb()).collection("security_events").find({}).toArray()).map(
      (e) => [e.type, e.reason],
    );
    expect(reasons).toEqual([
      ["signin_failed", "signature_invalid"],
      ["signin_failed", "challenge_unavailable"],
    ]);
  });

  it("rejects an expired challenge", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const error = await errorOf(
      verifySignIn(
        { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message) },
        meta(new Date("2026-11-13T10:05:00.000Z")),
      ),
    );
    expect((error as AuthFailedError).reason).toBe("challenge_unavailable");
  });

  it("rejects a malformed body before touching the challenge", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    for (const body of [
      { challengeId: "nope", signature: wallet.sign(view.message) },
      { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: "0OIl" },
      { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message), provider: "Bad!" },
      { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message), extra: 1 },
    ]) {
      expect(await errorOf(verifySignIn(body, meta()))).toBeInstanceOf(ValidationError);
    }
    await expect(
      verifySignIn({ challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message) }, meta()),
    ).resolves.toBeDefined();
  });

  it("rejects a suspended user", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const first = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const one = await verifySignIn(
      { challengeId: first.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(first.message) },
      meta(),
    );
    await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("users")
      .updateOne(
        { _id: parseObjectId(one.body.user.id) ?? undefined },
        { $set: { status: "suspended" } },
      );
    const second = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const error = await errorOf(
      verifySignIn(
        { challengeId: second.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(second.message) },
        meta(),
      ),
    );
    expect((error as AuthFailedError).reason).toBe("user_inactive");
  });

  it("returns a session token that resolves", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const view = await challengeFor(wallet);
    const result = await verifySignIn(
      { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message) },
      meta(),
    );
    const resolution = await resolveSession(tokenOf(result.setCookie), T0);
    expect(resolution?.session.userId.toHexString()).toBe(result.body.user.id);
    expect(resolution?.slid).toBe(false);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/auth/sessions.test.ts`:

```ts
import { beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  issueChallenge,
  logout,
  resolveSession,
  verifySignIn,
  type AuthContext,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { getDb, parseObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { randomToken, sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

const T0 = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
const MIN = 60_000;
const HOUR = 60 * MIN;
const DAY = 24 * HOUR;

async function signIn(): Promise<{ token: string; userId: string }> {
  const wallet = createTestWallet();
  const meta = { at: T0, ip: "unknown", userAgent: null };
  const view = await issueChallenge({ address: wallet.address, network: "devnet" }, meta);
  const result = await verifySignIn(
    { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: wallet.sign(view.message) },
    meta,
  );
  const token = /=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43});/.exec(result.setCookie)?.[1];
  if (token === undefined) throw new Error("no token");
  return { token, userId: result.body.user.id };
}

async function sessionDoc(token: string) {
  return (await getDb()).collection("sessions").findOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(token) });
}

beforeEach(async () => {
  const db = await getDb();
  for (const name of ["users", "wallets", "auth_challenges", "sessions", "security_events"]) {
    await db.collection(name).deleteMany({});
  }
});

describe("resolveSession", () => {
  it("returns null for no token and for an unknown token", async () => {
    expect(await resolveSession(null, T0)).toBeNull();
    expect(await resolveSession(randomToken(32), T0)).toBeNull();
  });

  it("does not write within the first hour, then slides once and reports it", async () => {
    const { token } = await signIn();
    const early = await resolveSession(token, new Date(T0.getTime() + 10 * MIN));
    expect(early?.slid).toBe(false);
    expect((await sessionDoc(token))?.lastSeenAt).toEqual(T0);
    const at = new Date(T0.getTime() + 61 * MIN);
    const late = await resolveSession(token, at);
    expect(late?.slid).toBe(true);
    expect(late?.session.expiresAt).toEqual(new Date(at.getTime() + 7 * DAY));
    expect(await sessionDoc(token)).toMatchObject({
      lastSeenAt: at,
      expiresAt: late?.session.expiresAt,
    });
    const again = await resolveSession(token, new Date(at.getTime() + MIN));
    expect(again?.slid).toBe(false);
  });

  it("writes once when concurrent requests race the hourly slide", async () => {
    const { token } = await signIn();
    const at = new Date(T0.getTime() + 2 * HOUR);
    const results = await Promise.all([1, 2, 3, 4].map(() => resolveSession(token, at)));
    expect(results.every((r) => r !== null)).toBe(true);
    expect(results.filter((r) => r?.slid === true)).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it("expires after the idle lifetime without activity", async () => {
    const stillValid = await signIn();
    const idle = await signIn();
    expect(
      await resolveSession(stillValid.token, new Date(T0.getTime() + 7 * DAY - 1)),
    ).not.toBeNull();
    expect(await resolveSession(idle.token, new Date(T0.getTime() + 7 * DAY))).toBeNull();
  });

  it("caps sliding at the absolute lifetime", async () => {
    const { token } = await signIn();
    let at = T0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {
      at = new Date(at.getTime() + 23 * HOUR);
      const resolution = await resolveSession(token, at);
      if (at.getTime() >= T0.getTime() + 30 * DAY) {
        expect(resolution).toBeNull();
        return;
      }
      expect(resolution?.session.expiresAt.getTime()).toBeLessThanOrEqual(T0.getTime() + 30 * DAY);
    }
    throw new Error("absolute expiry was never reached");
  });

  it("returns null for a revoked session and for a suspended user", async () => {
    const revoked = await signIn();
    await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("sessions")
      .updateOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(revoked.token) }, { $set: { revokedAt: T0 } });
    expect(await resolveSession(revoked.token, T0)).toBeNull();
    const suspended = await signIn();
    await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("users")
      .updateOne(
        { _id: parseObjectId(suspended.userId) ?? undefined },
        { $set: { status: "suspended" } },
      );
    expect(await resolveSession(suspended.token, T0)).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("logout", () => {
  it("revokes the session once and records one session_revoked event", async () => {
    const { token } = await signIn();
    const resolution = await resolveSession(token, T0);
    if (resolution === null) throw new Error("session did not resolve");
    const auth: AuthContext = {
      at: T0,
      ip: "unknown",
      userAgent: null,
      session: resolution.session,
    };
    await logout(auth);
    await logout(auth);
    expect(await resolveSession(token, T0)).toBeNull();
    const events = await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("security_events")
      .find({ type: "session_revoked" })
      .toArray();
    expect(events).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(events[0]).toMatchObject({ reason: "logout", severity: "info" });
  });
});
```
