```ts
import type { SecurityReason } from "@/modules/security";
import { AuthFailedError, ConflictError, ValidationError } from "@/modules/shared";

/** 400 NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED: a string network outside config SOLANA_NETWORKS. */
export function networkUnsupported(): ValidationError {
  return new ValidationError({ code: "NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED", safeMessage: "Network not supported" });
}

/** 409 WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED */
export function walletAlreadyLinked(): ConflictError {
  return new ConflictError({
    code: "WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED",
    safeMessage: "Wallet is linked to another account",
  });
}

/** 409 LAST_WALLET */
export function lastWallet(): ConflictError {
  return new ConflictError({
    code: "LAST_WALLET",
    safeMessage: "Cannot unlink the only active wallet",
  });
}

/** 401 AUTH_FAILED with a static reason that is also a security_events reason. */
export function authFailed(reason: SecurityReason): AuthFailedError {
  return new AuthFailedError(reason);
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/guard.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * A write-skew guard must really change the document: MongoDB treats an update that sets a field
 * to its current value as a no-op, and a no-op takes no write lock, so it cannot conflict.
 * Returns `at`, or one millisecond after `previous` when `at` is not later than it.
 */
export function strictlyAfter(at: Date, previous: Date): Date {
  return new Date(Math.max(at.getTime(), previous.getTime() + 1));
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/request.ts`:

```ts
import { z } from "zod";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { parseObjectId, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { clientIp } from "@/modules/security";
import { ValidationError, now } from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  PROVIDER_PATTERN,
  parseSignature,
  parseSolanaAddress,
  type SolanaAddress,
  type SolanaNetwork,
} from "@/modules/wallet";
import { networkUnsupported } from "./errors";
import type { RequestMeta } from "./types";

const MAX_USER_AGENT = 128;

/** Removes C0 control characters and DEL. */
function stripControl(value: string): string {
  return Array.from(value)
    .filter((char) => {
      const code = char.codePointAt(0) ?? 0;
      return code >= 0x20 && code !== 0x7f;
    })
    .join("");
}

/** Reads config (TRUST_PROXY) lazily, per call. */
export function requestMeta(request: Request): RequestMeta {
  const { TRUST_PROXY } = loadConfig();
  const raw = request.headers.get("user-agent");
  const userAgent = raw === null ? "" : stripControl(raw).slice(0, MAX_USER_AGENT);
  return {
    at: now(),
    ip: clientIp(request, TRUST_PROXY),
    userAgent: userAgent === "" ? null : userAgent,
  };
}

const challengeBody = z.strictObject({
  address: z.string().max(64),
  network: z.string().max(32),
});

const verifyBody = z.strictObject({
  challengeId: z.string().max(24),
  signature: z.string().max(128),
  provider: z.string().regex(PROVIDER_PATTERN).optional(),
});

export interface ChallengeRequest {
  readonly address: SolanaAddress;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
}

export interface VerifyRequest {
  readonly challengeId: ObjectId;
  readonly signature: Uint8Array;
  readonly provider: string | null;
}

/**
 * Body of POST /auth/challenge and /wallets/link/challenge.
 * @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED) for a malformed body or address;
 *         ValidationError (NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED) for a string network outside `allowed`
 */
export function parseChallengeBody(
  body: unknown,
  allowed: readonly SolanaNetwork[],
): ChallengeRequest {
  const parsed = challengeBody.safeParse(body);
  if (!parsed.success) throw new ValidationError({ message: "invalid challenge body" });
  const address = parseSolanaAddress(parsed.data.address);
  const network = allowed.find((candidate) => candidate === parsed.data.network);
  if (network === undefined) throw networkUnsupported();
  return { address, network };
}

/**
 * Body of POST /auth/verify and /wallets/link/verify. A well-formed but unknown challengeId is
 * not decided here (that is AUTH_FAILED at consume time).
 * @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED) for a malformed body, challengeId, signature or provider
 */
export function parseVerifyBody(body: unknown): VerifyRequest {
  const parsed = verifyBody.safeParse(body);
  if (!parsed.success) throw new ValidationError({ message: "invalid verify body" });
  const challengeId = parseObjectId(parsed.data.challengeId);
  if (challengeId === null) throw new ValidationError({ message: "invalid challengeId" });
  return {
    challengeId,
    signature: parseSignature(parsed.data.signature),
    provider: parsed.data.provider ?? null,
  };
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/cookies.ts`:

```ts
const TOKEN_PATTERN = /^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/;
const SECONDS_PER_DAY = 86_400;

export type SessionCookieName = "__Host-gw_session" | "gw_session";

/** https origins get the __Host- prefix (and Secure); http (local dev only) gets gw_session. */
export function sessionCookieName(appOrigin: string): SessionCookieName {
  return appOrigin.startsWith("https://") ? "__Host-gw_session" : "gw_session";
}

function attributes(appOrigin: string, maxAgeSeconds: number): string {
  const secure = appOrigin.startsWith("https://") ? "; Secure" : "";
  return `Path=/; Max-Age=${maxAgeSeconds}; HttpOnly${secure}; SameSite=Lax`;
}

export function buildSessionCookie(
  token: string,
  maxAgeSeconds: number,
  appOrigin: string,
): string {
  return `${sessionCookieName(appOrigin)}=${token}; ${attributes(appOrigin, maxAgeSeconds)}`;
}

/** The same name and attributes with an empty value and Max-Age=0. */
export function clearSessionCookie(appOrigin: string): string {
  return `${sessionCookieName(appOrigin)}=; ${attributes(appOrigin, 0)}`;
}

/**
 * The first cookie with the configured name whose value is a 43-char base64url token, else null.
 * Malformed values never reach the database.
 */
export function readSessionToken(request: Request, appOrigin: string): string | null {
  const header = request.headers.get("cookie");
  if (header === null) return null;
  const name = sessionCookieName(appOrigin);
  for (const part of header.split(";")) {
    const pair = part.trim();
    const eq = pair.indexOf("=");
    if (eq === -1 || pair.slice(0, eq) !== name) continue;
    const value = pair.slice(eq + 1);
    if (TOKEN_PATTERN.test(value)) return value;
  }
  return null;
}

/** True if the response already sets the session cookie (so a logout clear wins over a slide). */
export function hasSessionCookie(response: Response, appOrigin: string): boolean {
  const prefix = `${sessionCookieName(appOrigin)}=`;
  return response.headers.getSetCookie().some((value) => value.startsWith(prefix));
}

/** Max-Age for a freshly issued session. */
export function idleMaxAgeSeconds(idleDays: number): number {
  return idleDays * SECONDS_PER_DAY;
}

/** Max-Age after a slide: whole seconds from `at` to the session's (capped) expiresAt. */
export function remainingMaxAgeSeconds(expiresAt: Date, at: Date): number {
  return Math.max(0, Math.floor((expiresAt.getTime() - at.getTime()) / 1000));
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/challenges.ts`:

```ts
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { safeEquals, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { randomToken, sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import { ConflictError, InternalError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  addressToPublicKey,
  buildSignInMessage,
  parseSignInMessage,
  verifyEd25519,
  type SignInPurpose,
  type SolanaAddress,
  type SolanaNetwork,
} from "@/modules/wallet";
import { challenges, type ChallengeDoc } from "./collections";
import { authFailed } from "./errors";
import type { ChallengeView } from "./types";

export const CHALLENGE_TTL_MS = 300_000;
const NONCE_BYTES = 16;

function isDuplicateKey(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ConflictError && error.code === "DUPLICATE_KEY";
}

/**
 * Builds the sign-in message and stores the challenge (nonce only as its hash). A nonceHash
 * collision (128 random bits, so effectively never) is retried once with a new nonce.
 * @throws InternalError("NONCE_COLLISION") on a second collision; DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function insertChallenge(input: {
  readonly purpose: SignInPurpose;
  readonly address: SolanaAddress;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  readonly userId: ObjectId | null;
  readonly at: Date;
}): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  const { APP_ORIGIN } = loadConfig();
  const expiresAt = new Date(input.at.getTime() + CHALLENGE_TTL_MS);
  for (let attempt = 1; ; attempt += 1) {
    const nonce = randomToken(NONCE_BYTES);
    const message = buildSignInMessage({
      purpose: input.purpose,
      domain: new URL(APP_ORIGIN).host,
      address: input.address,
      uri: APP_ORIGIN,
      network: input.network,
      nonce,
      issuedAt: input.at,
      expiresAt,
    });
    try {
      const doc = await challenges.insertOne({
        nonceHash: sha256Hex(nonce),
        message,
        address: input.address,
        network: input.network,
        purpose: input.purpose,
        userId: input.userId,
        createdAt: input.at,
        expiresAt,
        consumedAt: null,
      });
      return {
        challengeId: doc._id.toHexString(),
        message,
        expiresAt: expiresAt.toISOString(),
      };
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
      if (attempt >= 2) {
        throw new InternalError({
          code: "NONCE_COLLISION",
          message: "auth_challenges: nonceHash collided twice",
        });
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * Atomically consumes an unconsumed, unexpired challenge of `purpose` (and, for link, of
 * `userId`). Of two concurrent consumers exactly one matches. The challenge stays consumed
 * whatever happens next, so one challenge allows one signature attempt.
 * @throws AuthFailedError("challenge_unavailable") when nothing matches
 */
export async function consumeChallenge(input: {
  readonly challengeId: ObjectId;
  readonly purpose: SignInPurpose;
  readonly userId: ObjectId | null;
  readonly at: Date;
}): Promise<ChallengeDoc> {
  const filter = Object.assign(
    {},
    safeEquals("_id", input.challengeId),
    { purpose: input.purpose, consumedAt: null, expiresAt: { $gt: input.at } },
    input.userId === null ? {} : safeEquals("userId", input.userId),
  );
  const doc = await challenges.findOneAndUpdate(filter, { $set: { consumedAt: input.at } });
  if (doc === null) throw authFailed("challenge_unavailable");
  return doc;
}

/**
 * Checks a consumed challenge: its network is still allowed, its stored message still matches
 * the stored fields (a data-integrity signal otherwise), and the signature verifies over the
 * exact stored message bytes against the stored address.
 * @throws AuthFailedError network_unsupported | message_integrity | signature_invalid
 * @returns the parsed message address
 */
export function checkChallenge(
  doc: ChallengeDoc,
  signature: Uint8Array,
  allowed: readonly SolanaNetwork[],
): SolanaAddress {
  if (!allowed.includes(doc.network)) throw authFailed("network_unsupported");
  const fields = parseSignInMessage(doc.message);
  if (
    fields === null ||
    fields.address !== doc.address ||
    fields.network !== doc.network ||
    fields.purpose !== doc.purpose ||
    sha256Hex(fields.nonce) !== doc.nonceHash
  ) {
    logger.error("challenge message integrity check failed", { cid: doc._id.toHexString() });
    throw authFailed("message_integrity");
  }
  const message = new TextEncoder().encode(doc.message);
  if (!verifyEd25519(addressToPublicKey(fields.address), message, signature)) {
    throw authFailed("signature_invalid");
  }
  return fields.address;
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/sessions.ts`:

```ts
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { safeEquals, type ClientSession, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { recordSecurityEvent, sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import type { SolanaNetwork } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { sessions, users, type SessionDoc } from "./collections";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "./indexes";
import type { AuthContext, SessionResolution } from "./types";

const DAY_MS = 86_400_000;
/** lastSeenAt and expiresAt are written at most once per hour per session. */
const SLIDE_INTERVAL_MS = 3_600_000;

/** Inside the sign-in transaction only. Lifetimes come from SESSION_IDLE_DAYS / SESSION_MAX_DAYS. */
export function createSession(
  input: {
    readonly tokenHash: string;
    readonly userId: ObjectId;
    readonly walletId: ObjectId;
    readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
    readonly userAgent: string | null;
    readonly at: Date;
  },
  options: { session: ClientSession },
): Promise<SessionDoc> {
  const { SESSION_IDLE_DAYS, SESSION_MAX_DAYS } = loadConfig();
  const atMs = input.at.getTime();
  return sessions.insertOne(
    {
      tokenHash: input.tokenHash,
      userId: input.userId,
      walletId: input.walletId,
      network: input.network,
      createdAt: input.at,
      lastSeenAt: input.at,
      expiresAt: new Date(atMs + SESSION_IDLE_DAYS * DAY_MS),
      absoluteExpiresAt: new Date(atMs + SESSION_MAX_DAYS * DAY_MS),
      revokedAt: null,
      userAgent: input.userAgent,
    },
    { session: options.session },
  );
}

/**
 * null for: no token, unknown hash, revoked, expired (idle or absolute), user missing or not
 * active. On success slides expiresAt to min(at + idle, absoluteExpiresAt), at most once per
 * hour, with a compare-and-set on lastSeenAt so concurrent requests write once.
 */
export async function resolveSession(
  token: string | null,
  at: Date,
): Promise<SessionResolution | null> {
  if (token === null) return null;
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const doc = await sessions.findOne(safeEquals("tokenHash", sha256Hex(token)));
  const atMs = at.getTime();
  if (
    doc === null ||
    doc.revokedAt !== null ||
    doc.expiresAt.getTime() <= atMs ||
    doc.absoluteExpiresAt.getTime() <= atMs
  ) {
    return null;
  }
  const user = await users.findOne(safeEquals("_id", doc.userId));
  if (user === null || user.status !== "active") return null;

  let expiresAt = doc.expiresAt;
  let slid = false;
  if (atMs - doc.lastSeenAt.getTime() >= SLIDE_INTERVAL_MS) {
    const { SESSION_IDLE_DAYS } = loadConfig();
    const next = new Date(
      Math.min(atMs + SESSION_IDLE_DAYS * DAY_MS, doc.absoluteExpiresAt.getTime()),
    );
    const result = await sessions.updateOne(
      Object.assign({}, safeEquals("_id", doc._id), safeEquals("lastSeenAt", doc.lastSeenAt), {
        revokedAt: null,
      }),
      { $set: { lastSeenAt: at, expiresAt: next } },
    );
    if (result.modifiedCount === 1) {
      slid = true;
      expiresAt = next;
    }
  }
  return {
    session: {
      id: doc._id,
      userId: doc.userId,
      walletId: doc.walletId,
      network: doc.network,
      createdAt: doc.createdAt,
      expiresAt,
      absoluteExpiresAt: doc.absoluteExpiresAt,
    },
    slid,
  };
}

/** Revokes the current session. A session_revoked event is recorded only if it was active. */
export async function logout(auth: AuthContext): Promise<void> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const result = await sessions.updateOne(
    Object.assign(
      {},
      safeEquals("_id", auth.session.id),
      safeEquals("userId", auth.session.userId),
      { revokedAt: null },
    ),
    { $set: { revokedAt: auth.at } },
  );
  if (result.modifiedCount === 1) {
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "session_revoked",
      severity: "info",
      userId: auth.session.userId,
      walletId: auth.session.walletId,
      reason: "logout",
      at: auth.at,
    });
  }
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/sign-in.ts`:

```ts
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { safeEquals, withTransaction, type ObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import {
  randomToken,
  rateLimit,
  recordSecurityEvent,
  sha256Hex,
  toSecurityReason,
} from "@/modules/security";
import { AuthFailedError, ConflictError, InternalError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  findWalletByAddress,
  insertWallet,
  setWalletStatus,
  touchWallet,
  type SolanaAddress,
  type SolanaNetwork,
  type WalletDoc,
} from "@/modules/wallet";
import { checkChallenge, consumeChallenge, insertChallenge } from "./challenges";
import { users } from "./collections";
import { buildSessionCookie, idleMaxAgeSeconds } from "./cookies";
import { authFailed } from "./errors";
import { strictlyAfter } from "./guard";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "./indexes";
import { parseChallengeBody, parseVerifyBody } from "./request";
import { createSession } from "./sessions";
import type { ChallengeView, RequestMeta, SignInResult } from "./types";

const SESSION_TOKEN_BYTES = 32;
const MINUTE = 60;
const HOUR = 3600;

function isDuplicateKey(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ConflictError && error.code === "DUPLICATE_KEY";
}

/**
 * POST /auth/challenge. The IP limit runs before the body is parsed, so malformed floods count.
 * @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED | NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED), RateLimitedError, DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function issueChallenge(body: unknown, meta: RequestMeta): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const config = loadConfig();
  await rateLimit(
    `signin_challenge:ip:${meta.ip}`,
    config.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN,
    MINUTE,
    meta.at,
  );
  const { address, network } = parseChallengeBody(body, config.SOLANA_NETWORKS);
  await rateLimit(
    `signin_challenge:address:${address}`,
    config.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS,
    HOUR,
    meta.at,
  );
  return insertChallenge({ purpose: "signin", address, network, userId: null, at: meta.at });
}

interface SignInWrite {
  readonly address: SolanaAddress;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  readonly provider: string | null;
  readonly tokenHash: string;
  readonly userAgent: string | null;
  readonly at: Date;
}

/**
 * The sign-in transaction (safe to re-run: database writes only, all with `session`). Always
 * writes the wallet doc, even when it is already active, so a concurrent unlink conflicts.
 * An outer loop of at most 2 runs retries only a DUPLICATE_KEY from a racing first sign-in.
 */
async function writeSignIn(input: SignInWrite): Promise<{ userId: ObjectId; wallet: WalletDoc }> {
  for (let run = 1; ; run += 1) {
    try {
      return await withTransaction(async (session) => {
        const existing = await findWalletByAddress(input.address, input.network, { session });
        let userId: ObjectId;
        let wallet: WalletDoc | null;
        if (existing === null) {
          const user = await users.insertOne(
            { status: "active", createdAt: input.at, updatedAt: input.at },
            { session },
          );
          userId = user._id;
          wallet = await insertWallet(
            {
              userId,
              address: input.address,
              network: input.network,
              provider: input.provider,
              at: input.at,
            },
            { session },
          );
        } else {
          const user = await users.findOne(safeEquals("_id", existing.userId), { session });
          if (user === null || user.status !== "active") throw authFailed("user_inactive");
          userId = user._id;
          wallet =
            existing.status === "disconnected"
              ? await setWalletStatus(existing._id, "active", input.at, {
                  session,
                  provider: input.provider,
                })
              : await touchWallet(existing._id, strictlyAfter(input.at, existing.updatedAt), {
                  session,
                });
          if (wallet === null) {
            throw new InternalError({
              code: "DATA_INTEGRITY",
              message: "wallets: wallet disappeared inside the sign-in transaction",
            });
          }
        }
        await createSession(
          {
            tokenHash: input.tokenHash,
            userId,
            walletId: wallet._id,
            network: input.network,
            userAgent: input.userAgent,
            at: input.at,
          },
          { session },
        );
        return { userId, wallet };
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
      if (run >= 2) {
        throw new InternalError({
          code: "SIGNIN_CONFLICT",
          message: "sign-in hit a duplicate key twice",
          cause: error,
        });
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * POST /auth/verify. Consumption happens outside the transaction, so a bad signature still
 * burns the challenge. Every AuthFailedError records signin_failed with its static reason.
 * @throws ValidationError, AuthFailedError, RateLimitedError, DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function verifySignIn(body: unknown, meta: RequestMeta): Promise<SignInResult> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const config = loadConfig();
  await rateLimit(`signin_verify:ip:${meta.ip}`, config.RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN, MINUTE, meta.at);
  const request = parseVerifyBody(body);
  try {
    const challenge = await consumeChallenge({
      challengeId: request.challengeId,
      purpose: "signin",
      userId: null,
      at: meta.at,
    });
    const address = checkChallenge(challenge, request.signature, config.SOLANA_NETWORKS);
    const token = randomToken(SESSION_TOKEN_BYTES);
    const { userId, wallet } = await writeSignIn({
      address,
      network: challenge.network,
      provider: request.provider,
      tokenHash: sha256Hex(token),
      userAgent: meta.userAgent,
      at: meta.at,
    });
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "signin_success",
      severity: "info",
      userId,
      walletId: wallet._id,
      reason: null,
      at: meta.at,
    });
    return {
      body: {
        user: { id: userId.toHexString() },
        wallet: { id: wallet._id.toHexString(), address: wallet.address, network: wallet.network },
      },
      setCookie: buildSessionCookie(
        token,
        idleMaxAgeSeconds(config.SESSION_IDLE_DAYS),
        config.APP_ORIGIN,
      ),
    };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof AuthFailedError) {
      logger.info("auth failed", { reason: error.reason, cid: request.challengeId.toHexString() });
      await recordSecurityEvent({
        type: "signin_failed",
        severity: "warning",
        userId: null,
        walletId: null,
        reason: toSecurityReason(error.reason),
        at: meta.at,
      });
    }
    throw error;
  }
}
```

### Task 10: Route wrappers, cookies on the wire, and the challenge, verify, logout and me routes

Spec §3.3 and architecture §3.7.3–3.7.4. `withOrigin` and `withSession` are the error boundary: every thrown value becomes `toErrorResponse`, so no route throws to Next. A slid session gets its cookie re-sent unless the handler already set one (so logout's clear wins). Route files export `runtime = "nodejs"`, `dynamic = "force-dynamic"` and one closure; no config is read at import.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/unit/auth/wrappers.test.ts`, `tests/helpers/auth.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/happy-path.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/sign-in-failures.test.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/wrappers.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/account.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/me/route.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/auth/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/unit/auth/wrappers.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/happy-path.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/sign-in-failures.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 9 (`requestMeta`, cookies, `issueChallenge`, `verifySignIn`, `resolveSession`, `logout`), `readJsonBody` and `checkOrigin` (Task 7), `noStoreJson`, `noContent`, `toErrorResponse`, `UnauthenticatedError` (Tasks 2, 4).
- Produces:

```ts
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it, vi } from "vitest";
import { withOrigin, withSession, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { ValidationError, logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import { useEnv } from "../../helpers/env";

/** Port 1: nothing listens there. No test here reaches the database. */
const UNIT_ENV = {
  NODE_ENV: "test",
  MONGODB_URI: "mongodb://127.0.0.1:1/?replicaSet=rs0",
  MONGODB_DB_NAME: "wrappers_unit",
  APP_ORIGIN: "https://ghostwire.test",
};

let restore: () => void;

beforeEach(() => {
  restore = useEnv(UNIT_ENV);
  vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
});

afterEach(() => {
  restore();
});

const CONTEXT = { params: Promise.resolve({}) };

function post(origin?: string): Request {
  const headers = origin === undefined ? undefined : { origin };
  return new Request("https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/x", { method: "POST", headers });
}

describe("withOrigin", () => {
  it("returns 403 ORIGIN_REJECTED without calling the handler for a foreign or missing Origin", async () => {
    const handler = vi.fn<RouteHandler>(async () => new Response(null, { status: 204 }));
    const wrapped = withOrigin(handler);
    for (const request of [post("https://evil.test"), post()]) {
      const response = await wrapped(request, CONTEXT);
      expect(response.status).toBe(403);
      expect(response.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
      expect(await response.json()).toEqual({
        code: "ORIGIN_REJECTED",
        message: "Origin not allowed",
      });
    }
    expect(handler).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("calls the handler for a matching Origin and passes its response through", async () => {
    const wrapped = withOrigin(async () => new Response("ok", { status: 201 }));
    const response = await wrapped(post("https://ghostwire.test"), CONTEXT);
    expect(response.status).toBe(201);
  });

  it("maps a thrown ValidationError to a 400 body", async () => {
    const wrapped = withOrigin(async () => {
      throw new ValidationError({ message: "SENTINEL detail" });
    });
    const response = await wrapped(post("https://ghostwire.test"), CONTEXT);
    expect(response.status).toBe(400);
    expect(await response.text()).toBe('{"code":"VALIDATION_FAILED","message":"Invalid request"}');
  });

  it("maps a thrown plain Error to 500 INTERNAL", async () => {
    const wrapped = withOrigin(async () => {
      throw new Error("SENTINEL boom");
    });
    const response = await wrapped(post("https://ghostwire.test"), CONTEXT);
    expect(response.status).toBe(500);
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" });
  });

  it("returns a 500 instead of throwing when config is invalid at request time", async () => {
    restore();
    restore = useEnv({ ...UNIT_ENV, APP_ORIGIN: undefined });
    const response = await withOrigin(async () => new Response())(post("x"), CONTEXT);
    expect(response.status).toBe(500);
  });
});

describe("withSession", () => {
  it("returns 401 UNAUTHENTICATED without a session cookie and never calls the handler", async () => {
    const handler = vi.fn(async () => new Response());
    const response = await withSession(handler)(
      new Request("https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/me"),
      CONTEXT,
    );
    expect(response.status).toBe(401);
    expect(await response.json()).toEqual({
      code: "UNAUTHENTICATED",
      message: "Authentication required",
    });
    expect(handler).not.toHaveBeenCalled();
  });

  it("returns 401 for a malformed cookie without touching the database", async () => {
    const request = new Request("https://ghostwire.test/api/v1/me", {
      headers: { cookie: "__Host-gw_session=not-a-token" },
    });
    const response = await withSession(async () => new Response())(request, CONTEXT);
    expect(response.status).toBe(401);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/helpers/auth.ts`:

```ts
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import type { ChallengeView } from "@/modules/auth";
import type { TestWallet } from "./ed25519";
import type { CallResult, TestClient } from "./http";

export interface SignedIn {
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly walletId: string;
  readonly challenge: ChallengeView;
  readonly signature: string;
}

/** POSTs a challenge for `wallet` and returns its view. Throws unless the route returns 200. */
export async function requestChallenge(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  const result = await client.call(challengeRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
    body: { address: wallet.address, network },
  });
  if (result.status !== 200) throw new Error(`challenge failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.json)}`);
  return result.json as ChallengeView;
}

/** POSTs a verify for a challenge view, signed by `wallet`. Returns the raw result. */
export function verify(
  client: TestClient,
  challenge: ChallengeView,
  signature: string,
  extra: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> = {},
): Promise<CallResult> {
  return client.call(verifyRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/verify",
    body: { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature, ...extra },
  });
}

/** Full sign-in through the routes; the client's jar then holds the session cookie. */
export async function signIn(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<SignedIn> {
  const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, network);
  const signature = wallet.sign(challenge.message);
  const result = await verify(client, challenge, signature);
  if (result.status !== 200) throw new Error(`verify failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.json)}`);
  const body = result.json as { user: { id: string }; wallet: { id: string } };
  return { userId: body.user.id, walletId: body.wallet.id, challenge, signature };
}
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/happy-path.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as logoutRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import * as meRoute from "@/app/api/v1/me/route";
import { requestChallenge, verify } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";

const ME = { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" } as const;

describe("sign-in happy path", () => {
  it("challenge -> verify -> cookie -> /me -> logout -> /me 401", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();

    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, "devnet");
    expect(challenge.message.split("\n")[0]).toBe(
      "ghostwire.test wants you to sign in with your Solana account:",
    );

    const verified = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message), {
      provider: "phantom",
    });
    expect(verified.status).toBe(200);
    expect(verified.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const body = verified.json as {
      user: { id: string };
      wallet: { id: string; address: string; network: string };
    };
    expect(body.wallet).toEqual({ id: body.wallet.id, address: wallet.address, network: "devnet" });
    expect(verified.setCookies).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(verified.setCookies[0]).toMatch(
      /^__Host-gw_session=[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}; Path=\/; Max-Age=604800; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax$/,
    );
    expect(client.cookie).not.toBeNull();

    const me = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
    expect(me.status).toBe(200);
    expect(me.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    expect(me.json).toEqual({
      user: { id: body.user.id, createdAt: expect.any(String) },
      session: {
        id: expect.stringMatching(/^[0-9a-f]{24}$/),
        network: "devnet",
        expiresAt: expect.any(String),
      },
      wallets: [
        {
          id: body.wallet.id,
          address: wallet.address,
          network: "devnet",
          provider: "phantom",
          status: "active",
          linkedAt: expect.any(String),
        },
      ],
    });

    const out = await client.call(logoutRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/logout",
    });
    expect(out.status).toBe(204);
    expect(out.json).toBeNull();
    expect(out.setCookies).toEqual([
      "__Host-gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax",
    ]);
    expect(client.cookie).toBeNull();

    const after = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
    expect(after.status).toBe(401);
    expect(after.json).toEqual({ code: "UNAUTHENTICATED", message: "Authentication required" });
  });

  it("rejects the old token after logout even if the client still sends it", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet);
    await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    const token = client.cookie;
    await client.call(logoutRoute.POST, { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/auth/logout" });
    client.setCookie(token);
    expect((await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("exports nodejs runtime and force-dynamic from every auth route", () => {
    for (const route of [challengeRoute, verifyRoute, logoutRoute, meRoute]) {
      expect(route.runtime).toBe("nodejs");
      expect(route.dynamic).toBe("force-dynamic");
    }
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/sign-in-failures.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import { getDb, parseObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import { requestChallenge, signIn, verify } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { useTestClock } from "../../../helpers/clock";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { useEnv } from "../../../helpers/env";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";

const AUTH_FAILED = { code: "AUTH_FAILED", message: "Authentication failed" };

let restoreEnv: (() => void) | undefined;

afterEach(() => {
  restoreEnv?.();
  restoreEnv = undefined;
});

describe("POST /auth/verify failures", () => {
  it("rejects a replay of a consumed challenge with 401 AUTH_FAILED", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const { challenge, signature } = await signIn(client, wallet);
    const replay = await verify(new TestClient(), challenge, signature);
    expect(replay.status).toBe(401);
    expect(replay.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
    expect(replay.setCookies).toEqual([]);
  });

  it("rejects an expired challenge (clock + 301 s)", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"));
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet);
    clock.advance(301_000);
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
  });

  it("rejects a signature from another key", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, createTestWallet());
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, createTestWallet().sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
  });

  it("rejects a signature over a tampered message (Chain ID changed to mainnet-beta)", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, "devnet");
    const tampered = challenge.message.replace("solana:devnet", "solana:mainnet-beta");
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(tampered));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
  });

  it("rejects a link challenge used at /auth/verify (wrong purpose)", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const owner = new TestClient();
    await signIn(owner, wallet);
    const db = await getDb();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), createTestWallet());
    await db
      .collection("auth_challenges")
      .updateOne(
        { _id: parseObjectId(challenge.challengeId) ?? undefined },
        { $set: { purpose: "link" } },
      );
    const result = await verify(new TestClient(), challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("rejects a well-formed but unknown challengeId with 401", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet);
    const unknown = { ...challenge, challengeId: "0123456789abcdef01234567" };
    const result = await verify(client, unknown, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("rejects a suspended user with 401 AUTH_FAILED", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const { userId } = await signIn(new TestClient(), wallet);
    await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("users")
      .updateOne({ _id: parseObjectId(userId) ?? undefined }, { $set: { status: "suspended" } });
    const client = new TestClient();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet);
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
  });

  it("lets exactly one of two concurrent verifies of one challenge succeed", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
    const signature = wallet.sign(challenge.message);
    const results = await Promise.all([
      verify(new TestClient(), challenge, signature),
      verify(new TestClient(), challenge, signature),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status).sort()).toEqual([200, 401]);
    expect(await (await getDb()).collection("sessions").countDocuments({})).toBeGreaterThan(0);
  });

  it("serializes two concurrent first sign-ins of one new wallet into one user", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const first = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
    const second = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
    const results = await Promise.all([
      verify(new TestClient(), first, wallet.sign(first.message)),
      verify(new TestClient(), second, wallet.sign(second.message)),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((r) => r.status)).toEqual([200, 200]);
    const ids = results.map((r) => (r.json as { user: { id: string } }).user.id);
    expect(ids[0]).toBe(ids[1]);
  });
});

describe("POST /auth/challenge network rules", () => {
  it("rejects testnet with 400 NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED", async () => {
    const result = await new TestClient().call(challengeRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
      body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "testnet" },
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED", message: "Network not supported" });
  });

  it("rejects mainnet-beta when SOLANA_NETWORKS=devnet", async () => {
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ SOLANA_NETWORKS: "devnet" });
    const result = await new TestClient().call(challengeRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
      body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "mainnet-beta" },
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED", message: "Network not supported" });
  });

  it("accepts mainnet-beta by default and states the network in the response", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, "mainnet-beta");
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    expect((result.json as { wallet: { network: string } }).wallet.network).toBe("mainnet-beta");
  });

  it("fails a mainnet-beta challenge at verify once mainnet-beta is removed from the allowlist", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, "mainnet-beta");
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ SOLANA_NETWORKS: "devnet" });
    const result = await verify(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(AUTH_FAILED);
  });
});

    );

});
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/wrappers.ts`:

```ts
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { checkOrigin } from "@/modules/security";
import { UnauthenticatedError, toErrorResponse } from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  buildSessionCookie,
  hasSessionCookie,
  readSessionToken,
  remainingMaxAgeSeconds,
} from "./cookies";
import { requestMeta } from "./request";
import { resolveSession } from "./sessions";
import type { AuthContext, RouteHandler, SessionHandler } from "./types";

/**
 * Origin check (config read per call, never at import), then the handler. Every thrown value
 * becomes a toErrorResponse, so no route can throw to Next. Outermost on every non-GET route.
 */
export function withOrigin<P extends object>(handler: RouteHandler<P>): RouteHandler<P> {
  return async (request, context) => {
    try {
      checkOrigin(request, loadConfig().APP_ORIGIN);
      return await handler(request, context);
    } catch (error) {
      return toErrorResponse(error);
    }
  };
}

/**
 * Resolves the session cookie (401 UNAUTHENTICATED otherwise) and passes an AuthContext. When the
 * session slid, re-sends the cookie with the new Max-Age unless the handler set it itself.
 */
export function withSession<P extends object>(handler: SessionHandler<P>): RouteHandler<P> {
  return async (request, context) => {
    try {
      const { APP_ORIGIN } = loadConfig();
      const meta = requestMeta(request);
      const token = readSessionToken(request, APP_ORIGIN);
      const resolution = await resolveSession(token, meta.at);
      if (token === null || resolution === null) throw new UnauthenticatedError();
      const auth: AuthContext = { ...meta, session: resolution.session };
      const response = await handler(request, auth, context);
      if (resolution.slid && !hasSessionCookie(response, APP_ORIGIN)) {
        const maxAge = remainingMaxAgeSeconds(resolution.session.expiresAt, meta.at);
        response.headers.append("Set-Cookie", buildSessionCookie(token, maxAge, APP_ORIGIN));
      }
      return response;
    } catch (error) {
      return toErrorResponse(error);
    }
  };
}
```

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/account.ts`:

```ts
import { safeEquals } from "@/modules/database";
import { UnauthenticatedError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { listWalletsByUser, toWalletView, type WalletView } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { users } from "./collections";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "./indexes";
import type { AuthContext, MeView } from "./types";

/** GET /me. Lists every wallet of the user, disconnected ones included, with their status. */
export async function getMe(auth: AuthContext): Promise<MeView> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const user = await users.findOne(safeEquals("_id", auth.session.userId));
  if (user === null) throw new UnauthenticatedError();
  const wallets = await listWalletsByUser(auth.session.userId);
  return {
    user: { id: user._id.toHexString(), createdAt: user.createdAt.toISOString() },
    session: {
      id: auth.session.id.toHexString(),
      network: auth.session.network,
      expiresAt: auth.session.expiresAt.toISOString(),
    },
    wallets: wallets.map((wallet) => toWalletView(wallet)),
  };
}

/** GET /wallets. Same view as /me, disconnected wallets included. */
export async function listWallets(auth: AuthContext): Promise<WalletView[]> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const wallets = await listWalletsByUser(auth.session.userId);
  return wallets.map((wallet) => toWalletView(wallet));
}
```

Create `src/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route.ts`:

```ts
import { issueChallenge, requestMeta, withOrigin, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { readJsonBody } from "@/modules/security";
import { noStoreJson } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** POST /api/v1/auth/challenge { address, network } -> 200 { challengeId, message, expiresAt } */
export const POST: RouteHandler = withOrigin(async (request) => {
  const meta = requestMeta(request);
  return noStoreJson(await issueChallenge(await readJsonBody(request), meta), { status: 200 });
});
```

Create `src/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route.ts`:

```ts
import { requestMeta, verifySignIn, withOrigin, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { readJsonBody } from "@/modules/security";
import { noStoreJson } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** POST /api/v1/auth/verify { challengeId, signature, provider? } -> 200 { user, wallet } + Set-Cookie */
export const POST: RouteHandler = withOrigin(async (request) => {
  const meta = requestMeta(request);
  const result = await verifySignIn(await readJsonBody(request), meta);
  const response = noStoreJson(result.body, { status: 200 });
  response.headers.append("Set-Cookie", result.setCookie);
  return response;
});
```

Create `src/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route.ts`:

```ts
import {
  clearSessionCookie,
  logout,
  withOrigin,
  withSession,
  type RouteHandler,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { noContent } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** POST /api/v1/auth/logout -> 204, revokes the current session and clears the cookie. Body never read. */
export const POST: RouteHandler = withOrigin(
  withSession(async (_request, auth) => {
    await logout(auth);
    return noContent({ headers: { "Set-Cookie": clearSessionCookie(loadConfig().APP_ORIGIN) } });
  }),
);
```

Create `src/app/api/v1/me/route.ts`:

```ts
import { getMe, withSession, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { noStoreJson } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** GET /api/v1/me -> 200 { user, session, wallets } */
export const GET: RouteHandler = withSession(async (_request, auth) =>
  noStoreJson(await getMe(auth)),
);
```

### Task 11: Sessions routes: list and revoke

Spec §3.3 and architecture §5.4. Lists only the caller's live sessions (not revoked, not idle-expired, not absolutely expired), marks the current one, and revokes by owner and id. A malformed, nonexistent or foreign id is a 404. Revoking the current session also clears the cookie (architecture §3.7.4 decision). `listSessions` and `revokeSession` live in `session-management.ts` (the architecture names `sessions.ts`) so Task 9's file is not rewritten.

**Files:**
- Create: `tests/integration/routes/v1/sessions.test.ts`, `src/modules/auth/internal/session-management.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/sessions/route.ts`, `src/app/api/v1/sessions/[id]/route.ts`
- Modify: `src/modules/auth/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/integration/routes/v1/sessions.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `sessions` collection, `ensureAuthReady`, `withOrigin`/`withSession`, `clearSessionCookie`, `parseObjectId`, `recordSecurityEvent`, `NotFoundError`, `noStoreJson`, `noContent`.
- Produces from `@/modules/auth` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3): `listSessions(auth: AuthContext): Promise<SessionView[]>` (lastSeenAt descending, limit 100); `revokeSession(sessionId: string, auth: AuthContext): Promise<RevokeResult>` (an already-revoked own session is a 204 no-op; `session_revoked`/`user_revoked` recorded only when it was active). Routes: `GET /api/v1/sessions` (`RouteHandler`), `DELETE /api/v1/sessions/[id]` (`RouteHandler<{ id: string }>`).

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as revokeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/sessions/[id]/route";
import * as sessionsRoute from "@/app/api/v1/sessions/route";

const MIN = 60_000;

const DAY = 24 * HOUR;
const T0 = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");

const ME = { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" } as const;
const LIST = { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/sessions" } as const;

}

    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    expect(result.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
    const { sessions } = result.json as SessionsBody;
    expect(sessions).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(sessions.filter((s) => s.current)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(sessions[0]).toEqual({

      createdAt: "2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z",

      userAgent: null,
    });
  });
});

describe("DELETE /sessions/:id", () => {
  it("revokes another own session (204, no cookie change) and that cookie then gets 401", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const phone = new TestClient();
    await signIn(phone, wallet);
    const laptop = new TestClient();
    await signIn(laptop, wallet);
    const { sessions } = (await laptop.call(sessionsRoute.GET, LIST)).json as SessionsBody;
    const other = sessions.find((s) => !s.current);
    if (other === undefined) throw new Error("expected a second session");
    const result = await revoke(laptop, other.id);
    expect(result.status).toBe(204);
    expect(result.setCookies).toEqual([]);
    expect((await phone.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await laptop.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await revoke(laptop, other.id)).status).toBe(204);

    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    const { sessions } = (await client.call(sessionsRoute.GET, LIST)).json as SessionsBody;
    const current = sessions.find((s) => s.current);
    if (current === undefined) throw new Error("expected a current session");
    const result = await revoke(client, current.id);
    expect(result.status).toBe(204);
    expect(result.setCookies).toEqual([
      "__Host-gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax",
    ]);
    expect(client.cookie).toBeNull();
  });

  it("returns 404 for another user's session, a nonexistent id and a malformed id", async () => {
    const alice = new TestClient();
    await signIn(alice, createTestWallet());
    const bob = new TestClient();
    await signIn(bob, createTestWallet());
    const { sessions } = (await bob.call(sessionsRoute.GET, LIST)).json as SessionsBody;
    const bobSession = sessions[0]?.id ?? "";
    for (const id of [bobSession, "0123456789abcdef01234567", "not-an-id"]) {
      const result = await revoke(alice, id);
      expect(result.status).toBe(404);
      expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "Not found" });
    }
    expect((await bob.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("session lifetimes through the routes", () => {
  it("idle expiry: 401 after SESSION_IDLE_DAYS without activity", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(T0);
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    clock.advance(7 * DAY + 1);
    expect((await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(401);
  });

  it("absolute expiry: 401 at SESSION_MAX_DAYS even when active every 23 h", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(T0);
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    let lastOk = T0;
    for (let i = 0; i < 40; i += 1) {
      clock.advance(23 * HOUR);
      const result = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
      if (result.status === 401) {
        expect(clock.now().getTime()).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(T0.getTime() + 30 * DAY);
        expect(lastOk.getTime()).toBeLessThan(T0.getTime() + 30 * DAY);
        return;
      }
      expect(result.status).toBe(200);
      lastOk = clock.now();
    }
    throw new Error("absolute expiry was never reached");
  });

  it("slides at most once per hour and re-sends the cookie when it does", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(T0);
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    const token = client.cookie ?? "";
    const db = await getDb();
    clock.advance(10 * MIN);
    const early = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
    expect(early.setCookies).toEqual([]);
    expect(
      (await db.collection("sessions").findOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(token) }))?.lastSeenAt,
    ).toEqual(T0);
    clock.advance(51 * MIN);
    const late = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
    expect(late.setCookies).toEqual([
      `__Host-gw_session=${token}; Path=/; Max-Age=604800; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax`,
    ]);
    const stored = await db.collection("sessions").findOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(token) });
    expect(stored?.lastSeenAt).toEqual(clock.now());
  });

  it("caps the re-sent Max-Age at the absolute expiry", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(T0);
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    for (let day = 1; day <= 29; day += 1) {
      clock.set(new Date(T0.getTime() + day * DAY));
      expect((await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(200);
    }
    clock.set(new Date(T0.getTime() + 29 * DAY + 12 * HOUR));
    const result = await client.call(meRoute.GET, ME);
    expect(result.setCookies[0]).toContain("Max-Age=43200;");
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the implementation**

Create `src/modules/auth/internal/session-management.ts`:

```ts
import { parseObjectId, safeEquals } from "@/modules/database";
import { recordSecurityEvent } from "@/modules/security";
import { NotFoundError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { sessions } from "./collections";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "./indexes";
import type { AuthContext, RevokeResult, SessionView } from "./types";

const MAX_SESSIONS_LISTED = 100;

/** Active sessions of the caller (not revoked, not expired), lastSeenAt descending, limit 100. */
export async function listSessions(auth: AuthContext): Promise<SessionView[]> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const docs = await sessions.find(
    Object.assign({}, safeEquals("userId", auth.session.userId), {
      revokedAt: null,
      expiresAt: { $gt: auth.at },
      absoluteExpiresAt: { $gt: auth.at },
    }),
    { limit: MAX_SESSIONS_LISTED, sort: { lastSeenAt: -1, _id: -1 } },
  );
  return docs.map((doc) => ({
    id: doc._id.toHexString(),
    current: doc._id.equals(auth.session.id),
    createdAt: doc.createdAt.toISOString(),
    lastSeenAt: doc.lastSeenAt.toISOString(),
    expiresAt: doc.expiresAt.toISOString(),
    userAgent: doc.userAgent,
  }));
}

/**
 * DELETE /sessions/:id. Only the caller's own sessions. Revoking an already-revoked own session
 * is a 204 no-op. A session_revoked event (reason user_revoked) is recorded only when the session
 * was active before.
 * @throws NotFoundError when the id is malformed, nonexistent or owned by another user
 */
export async function revokeSession(sessionId: string, auth: AuthContext): Promise<RevokeResult> {
  const id = parseObjectId(sessionId);
  if (id === null) throw new NotFoundError();
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const owned = Object.assign({}, safeEquals("_id", id), safeEquals("userId", auth.session.userId));
  const before = await sessions.findOne(owned);
  if (before === null) throw new NotFoundError();
  const result = await sessions.updateOne(owned, { $set: { revokedAt: auth.at } });
  if (result.matchedCount === 0) throw new NotFoundError();
  if (before.revokedAt === null) {
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "session_revoked",
      severity: "info",
      userId: auth.session.userId,
      walletId: before.walletId,
      reason: "user_revoked",
      at: auth.at,
    });
  }
  return { revokedCurrent: id.equals(auth.session.id) };
}
```

```ts
import { listSessions, withSession, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { noStoreJson } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** GET /api/v1/sessions -> 200 { sessions: [{ id, current, createdAt, lastSeenAt, expiresAt, userAgent }] } */
export const GET: RouteHandler = withSession(async (_request, auth) =>
  noStoreJson({ sessions: await listSessions(auth) }),
);
```

Create `src/app/api/v1/sessions/[id]/route.ts`:

```ts
import {
  clearSessionCookie,
  revokeSession,
  withOrigin,
  withSession,
  type RouteHandler,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { noContent } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * DELETE /api/v1/sessions/:id -> 204 (own sessions only; otherwise 404). Revoking the current
 * session also clears the cookie. Body never read.
 */
export const DELETE: RouteHandler<{ id: string }> = withOrigin(
  withSession(async (_request, auth, context) => {
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const { revokedCurrent } = await revokeSession(id, auth);
    return revokedCurrent
      ? noContent({ headers: { "Set-Cookie": clearSessionCookie(loadConfig().APP_ORIGIN) } })
      : noContent();
  }),
);
```

### Task 12: Wallet routes: list, link challenge and verify, and unlink with the write-skew guard

Spec §5.3 and architecture §5.3. Link mirrors sign-in with `purpose: "link"` and the session user, under `link_*` rate-limit keys (architecture §7 item 2). A wallet under another user is 409 `WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED`; a new wallet is 201; an own active or reactivated wallet is 200. Unlink's transaction first writes the owning `users` document (to a value strictly later than the stored one), so two concurrent unlinks of one user write-conflict and the retried loser sees the new count (`maxAttempts: 5`). Unlinking revokes the sessions created with that wallet and clears the cookie when it was the current session's wallet. The concurrency test runs at the service level (deterministic) and at the route level (where the loser may also see 401 once its shared session was revoked).

- Modify: `tests/helpers/auth.ts`, `src/modules/auth/index.ts`
- Test: `tests/integration/routes/v1/wallets.test.ts`, `tests/integration/auth/unlink-race.test.ts`

- Produces from `@/modules/auth` (complete `index.ts` in Step 3): `issueLinkChallenge(body: unknown, auth: AuthContext): Promise<ChallengeView>`; `verifyLink(body: unknown, auth: AuthContext): Promise<LinkResult>`; `unlinkWallet(walletId: string, auth: AuthContext): Promise<UnlinkResult>`.
```ts
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import * as linkChallengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge/route";
import * as linkVerifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/verify/route";
import type { ChallengeView } from "@/modules/auth";
import type { TestWallet } from "./ed25519";
import type { CallResult, TestClient } from "./http";

export interface SignedIn {
  readonly userId: string;
  readonly walletId: string;
  readonly challenge: ChallengeView;
  readonly signature: string;
}

/** POSTs a challenge for `wallet` and returns its view. Throws unless the route returns 200. */
export async function requestChallenge(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  const result = await client.call(challengeRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
    body: { address: wallet.address, network },
  });
  if (result.status !== 200) throw new Error(`challenge failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.json)}`);
  return result.json as ChallengeView;
}

/** POSTs a verify for a challenge view, signed by `wallet`. Returns the raw result. */
export function verify(
  client: TestClient,
  challenge: ChallengeView,
  signature: string,
  extra: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> = {},
): Promise<CallResult> {
  return client.call(verifyRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/verify",
    body: { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature, ...extra },
  });
}

/** Full sign-in through the routes; the client's jar then holds the session cookie. */
export async function signIn(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<SignedIn> {
  const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet, network);
  const signature = wallet.sign(challenge.message);
  const result = await verify(client, challenge, signature);
  if (result.status !== 200) throw new Error(`verify failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.json)}`);
  const body = result.json as { user: { id: string }; wallet: { id: string } };
  return { userId: body.user.id, walletId: body.wallet.id, challenge, signature };
}

/** POSTs a link challenge for `wallet` as the client's session user. Throws unless 200. */
export async function requestLinkChallenge(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  const result = await client.call(linkChallengeRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge",
    body: { address: wallet.address, network },
  });
  if (result.status !== 200)
    throw new Error(`link challenge failed: ${JSON.stringify(result.json)}`);
  return result.json as ChallengeView;
}

/** POSTs a link verify for a challenge view. Returns the raw result. */
export function verifyLinkCall(
  client: TestClient,
  challenge: ChallengeView,
  signature: string,
  extra: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>> = {},
): Promise<CallResult> {
  return client.call(linkVerifyRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/verify",
    body: { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature, ...extra },
  });
}

/** Full link of `wallet` to the client's session user. Returns the verify result (201 or 200). */
export async function linkWallet(
  client: TestClient,
  wallet: TestWallet,
  network: "devnet" | "mainnet-beta" = "devnet",
): Promise<CallResult> {
  const challenge = await requestLinkChallenge(client, wallet, network);
  return verifyLinkCall(client, challenge, wallet.sign(challenge.message));
}
```

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as meRoute from "@/app/api/v1/me/route";
import * as unlinkRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/[id]/route";
import * as linkChallengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge/route";
import * as walletsRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/route";
import { getDb, parseObjectId } from "@/modules/database";
import {
  linkWallet,
  requestChallenge,
  requestLinkChallenge,
  signIn,
  verifyLinkCall,
} from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";

const ME = { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" } as const;
const LIST = { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/wallets" } as const;

interface WalletBody {
  id: string;
  address: string;
  network: string;
  provider: string | null;
  status: "active" | "disconnected";
  linkedAt: string;
}

function unlink(client: TestClient, id: string) {
  return client.call(unlinkRoute.DELETE, {
    method: "DELETE",
    path: `/api/v1/wallets/${id}`,
    params: { id },
  });
}

async function walletsOf(client: TestClient): Promise<WalletBody[]> {
  const result = await client.call(walletsRoute.GET, LIST);
  expect(result.status).toBe(200);
  return (result.json as { wallets: WalletBody[] }).wallets;
}

describe("linking wallets", () => {
  it("links a second wallet (201) that then signs in to the same user", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const first = createTestWallet();
    const second = createTestWallet();
    const { userId } = await signIn(client, first);
    const challenge = await requestLinkChallenge(client, second, "mainnet-beta");
    expect(challenge.message.split("\n")[3]).toBe(
      "Link this wallet to your GHOST WIRE account. This does not authorize any transaction or move funds.",
    );
    const linked = await verifyLinkCall(client, challenge, second.sign(challenge.message), {
      provider: "solflare",
    });
    expect(linked.status).toBe(201);
    expect((linked.json as { wallet: WalletBody }).wallet).toMatchObject({
      address: second.address,
      network: "mainnet-beta",
      provider: "solflare",
      status: "active",
    });
    expect((await walletsOf(client)).map((w) => w.address)).toEqual([
      first.address,
      second.address,
    ]);
    const again = await signIn(new TestClient(), second, "mainnet-beta");
    expect(again.userId).toBe(userId);
  });

  it("returns 200 with the existing wallet when re-linking an already active own wallet", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const { walletId } = await signIn(client, wallet);
    const result = await linkWallet(client, wallet);
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    expect((result.json as { wallet: WalletBody }).wallet.id).toBe(walletId);
  });

  it("returns 409 WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED for a wallet owned by another user", async () => {
    const owned = createTestWallet();
    await signIn(new TestClient(), owned);
    const other = new TestClient();
    await signIn(other, createTestWallet());
    const result = await linkWallet(other, owned);
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({
      code: "WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED",
      message: "Wallet is linked to another account",
    });
    const event = await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("security_events")
      .findOne({ type: "link_failed", reason: "wallet_other_account" });
    expect(event).not.toBeNull();
  });

  it("rejects user A's link challenge verified by user B with 401 AUTH_FAILED", async () => {
    const alice = new TestClient();
    await signIn(alice, createTestWallet());
    const bob = new TestClient();
    await signIn(bob, createTestWallet());
    const target = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestLinkChallenge(alice, target);
    const result = await verifyLinkCall(bob, challenge, target.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "AUTH_FAILED", message: "Authentication failed" });
  });

  it("rejects a sign-in challenge used at /wallets/link/verify (wrong purpose)", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    const target = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), target);
    const result = await verifyLinkCall(client, challenge, target.sign(challenge.message));
    expect(result.status).toBe(401);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "AUTH_FAILED", message: "Authentication failed" });
  });

  it("requires a session for the wallet routes", async () => {
    const anonymous = new TestClient();
    expect((await anonymous.call(walletsRoute.GET, LIST)).status).toBe(401);
    const challenge = await anonymous.call(linkChallengeRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge",
      body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "devnet" },
    });
    expect(challenge.status).toBe(401);
  });
});

describe("unlinking wallets", () => {
  it("refuses to unlink the last active wallet with 409 LAST_WALLET", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const { walletId } = await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    const result = await unlink(client, walletId);
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({
      code: "LAST_WALLET",
      message: "Cannot unlink the only active wallet",
    });
    expect((await walletsOf(client))[0]?.status).toBe("active");
  });

  it("unlinks a second wallet, revokes its sessions, keeps it listed as disconnected", async () => {
    const main = new TestClient();
    await signIn(main, createTestWallet());
    const extra = createTestWallet();
    const linked = await linkWallet(main, extra);
    const extraId = (linked.json as { wallet: WalletBody }).wallet.id;
    const extraSession = new TestClient();
    await signIn(extraSession, extra);
    const result = await unlink(main, extraId);
    expect(result.status).toBe(204);
    expect(result.setCookies).toEqual([]);
    expect((await extraSession.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(401);
    expect((await main.call(meRoute.GET, ME)).status).toBe(200);
    const listed = await walletsOf(main);
    expect(listed.find((w) => w.id === extraId)?.status).toBe("disconnected");
    expect((await unlink(main, extraId)).status).toBe(204);
  });

  it("clears the cookie when unlinking the wallet of the current session", async () => {
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const client = new TestClient();
    const { walletId } = await signIn(client, wallet);
    await linkWallet(client, createTestWallet());
    const result = await unlink(client, walletId);
    expect(result.status).toBe(204);
    expect(result.setCookies).toEqual([
      "__Host-gw_session=; Path=/; Max-Age=0; HttpOnly; Secure; SameSite=Lax",
    ]);
    expect(client.cookie).toBeNull();
  });

  it("reactivates a disconnected wallet on sign-in and on re-link", async () => {
    const main = new TestClient();
    const { userId } = await signIn(main, createTestWallet());
    const extra = createTestWallet();
    const extraId = ((await linkWallet(main, extra)).json as { wallet: WalletBody }).wallet.id;
    await unlink(main, extraId);
    const relinked = await linkWallet(main, extra);
    expect(relinked.status).toBe(200);
    expect((relinked.json as { wallet: WalletBody }).wallet.status).toBe("active");
    await unlink(main, extraId);
    const back = await signIn(new TestClient(), extra);
    expect(back.userId).toBe(userId);
    expect((await walletsOf(main)).find((w) => w.id === extraId)?.status).toBe("active");
  });

  it("leaves exactly one active wallet when the last two are unlinked concurrently", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const { userId, walletId: first } = await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    const second = ((await linkWallet(client, createTestWallet())).json as { wallet: WalletBody })
      .wallet.id;
    const other = new TestClient();
    other.setCookie(client.cookie);
    const results = await Promise.all([unlink(client, first), unlink(other, second)]);
    const statuses = results.map((r) => r.status).sort();
    // One unlink wins. The other loses with 409 LAST_WALLET, or with 401 when the winner was
    // the wallet of the shared session and revoked it before the loser resolved the cookie.
    expect(statuses[0]).toBe(204);
    expect([401, 409]).toContain(statuses[1]);
    const active = await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("wallets")
      .countDocuments({ userId: parseObjectId(userId) ?? undefined, status: "active" });
    expect(active).toBe(1);
  });

  it("returns 404 for another user's wallet, a nonexistent id and a malformed id", async () => {
    const alice = new TestClient();
    await signIn(alice, createTestWallet());
    await linkWallet(alice, createTestWallet());
    const bob = new TestClient();
    const { walletId: bobWallet } = await signIn(bob, createTestWallet());
    for (const id of [bobWallet, "0123456789abcdef01234567", "xyz"]) {
      const result = await unlink(alice, id);
      expect(result.status).toBe(404);
      expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "NOT_FOUND", message: "Not found" });
    }
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/auth/unlink-race.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import {
  issueChallenge,
  issueLinkChallenge,
  resolveSession,
  unlinkWallet,
  verifyLink,
  verifySignIn,
  type AuthContext,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { getDb } from "@/modules/database";
import { ConflictError } from "@/modules/shared";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../helpers/ed25519";

const AT = new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z");
const META = { at: AT, ip: "unknown", userAgent: null };

/** Signs in with one wallet, links a second, and returns the session context and both ids. */
async function userWithTwoWallets(): Promise<{ auth: AuthContext; ids: [string, string] }> {
  const first = createTestWallet();
  const view = await issueChallenge({ address: first.address, network: "devnet" }, META);
  const signedIn = await verifySignIn(
    { challengeId: view.challengeId, signature: first.sign(view.message) },
    META,
  );
  const token = /=([A-Za-z0-9_-]{43});/.exec(signedIn.setCookie)?.[1] ?? null;
  const resolution = await resolveSession(token, AT);
  if (resolution === null) throw new Error("session did not resolve");
  const auth: AuthContext = { ...META, session: resolution.session };
  const second = createTestWallet();
  const link = await issueLinkChallenge({ address: second.address, network: "devnet" }, auth);
  const linked = await verifyLink(
    { challengeId: link.challengeId, signature: second.sign(link.message) },
    auth,
  );
  return { auth, ids: [signedIn.body.wallet.id, linked.wallet.id] };
}

describe("unlink write-skew guard", () => {
  it.each([1, 2, 3, 4, 5])(
    "run %i: two concurrent unlinks of the last two wallets -> one success, one LAST_WALLET",
    async () => {
      const { auth, ids } = await userWithTwoWallets();
      const results = await Promise.allSettled(ids.map((id) => unlinkWallet(id, auth)));
      const fulfilled = results.filter((r) => r.status === "fulfilled");
      const rejected = results.filter((r) => r.status === "rejected");
      expect(fulfilled).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(rejected).toHaveLength(1);
      const reason = (rejected[0] as PromiseRejectedResult).reason as unknown;
      expect(reason).toBeInstanceOf(ConflictError);
      expect((reason as ConflictError).code).toBe("LAST_WALLET");
      const active = await (
        await getDb()
      )
        .collection("wallets")
        .countDocuments({ userId: auth.session.userId, status: "active" });
      expect(active).toBe(1);
    },
  );
});
```
