```ts
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { parseObjectId, safeEquals, withTransaction } from "@/modules/database";
import { rateLimit, recordSecurityEvent, toSecurityReason } from "@/modules/security";
import {
  AuthFailedError,
  ConflictError,
  InternalError,
  NotFoundError,
  logger,
} from "@/modules/shared";
import {
  countActiveWallets,
  findWalletByAddress,
  findWalletForUser,
  insertWallet,
  setWalletStatus,
  toWalletView,
  type SolanaAddress,
  type SolanaNetwork,
  type WalletDoc,
} from "@/modules/wallet";
import { checkChallenge, consumeChallenge, insertChallenge } from "./challenges";
import { sessions, users } from "./collections";
import { lastWallet, walletAlreadyLinked } from "./errors";
import { strictlyAfter } from "./guard";
import { ensureAuthReady } from "./indexes";
import { parseChallengeBody, parseVerifyBody } from "./request";
import type { AuthContext, ChallengeView, LinkResult, UnlinkResult } from "./types";

const MINUTE = 60;
const HOUR = 3600;
/** Unlink retries a write conflict on the users guard document up to 5 times (architecture §5.3). */
const UNLINK_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5;

function isDuplicateKey(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ConflictError && error.code === "DUPLICATE_KEY";
}

function isWalletAlreadyLinked(error: unknown): boolean {
  return error instanceof ConflictError && error.code === "WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED";
}

/**
 * POST /wallets/link/challenge. Same limits as sign-in under link_* keys (architecture §7 item 2).
 * @throws ValidationError (VALIDATION_FAILED | NETWORK_UNSUPPORTED), RateLimitedError, DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function issueLinkChallenge(body: unknown, auth: AuthContext): Promise<ChallengeView> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const config = loadConfig();
  await rateLimit(
    `link_challenge:ip:${auth.ip}`,
    config.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN,
    MINUTE,
    auth.at,
  );
  const { address, network } = parseChallengeBody(body, config.SOLANA_NETWORKS);
  await rateLimit(
    `link_challenge:address:${address}`,
    config.RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS,
    HOUR,
    auth.at,
  );
  return insertChallenge({
    purpose: "link",
    address,
    network,
    userId: auth.session.userId,
    at: auth.at,
  });
}

interface LinkWrite {
  readonly address: SolanaAddress;
  readonly network: SolanaNetwork;
  readonly provider: string | null;
  readonly auth: AuthContext;
}

/** The link transaction, with the same outer DUPLICATE_KEY loop as sign-in. */
async function writeLink(input: LinkWrite): Promise<{ wallet: WalletDoc; created: boolean }> {
  const userId = input.auth.session.userId;
  const at = input.auth.at;
  for (let run = 1; ; run += 1) {
    try {
      return await withTransaction(async (session) => {
        const existing = await findWalletByAddress(input.address, input.network, { session });
        if (existing === null) {
          const wallet = await insertWallet(
            {
              userId,
              address: input.address,
              network: input.network,
              provider: input.provider,
              at,
            },
            { session },
          );
          return { wallet, created: true };
        }
        if (!existing.userId.equals(userId)) throw walletAlreadyLinked();
        if (existing.status === "active") return { wallet: existing, created: false };
        const wallet = await setWalletStatus(existing._id, "active", at, {
          session,
          provider: input.provider,
        });
        if (wallet === null) {
          throw new InternalError({
            code: "DATA_INTEGRITY",
            message: "wallets: wallet disappeared inside the link transaction",
          });
        }
        return { wallet, created: false };
      });
    } catch (error) {
      if (!isDuplicateKey(error)) throw error;
      if (run >= 2) {
        throw new InternalError({
          code: "LINK_CONFLICT",
          message: "link hit a duplicate key twice",
          cause: error,
        });
      }
    }
  }
}

/**
 * POST /wallets/link/verify. The challenge must be a link challenge of the session user.
 * New wallet -> created: true (201). Same user, disconnected -> reactivated, created: false (200).
 * Same user, active -> returned unchanged, created: false (200).
 * @throws ValidationError, AuthFailedError, ConflictError(WALLET_ALREADY_LINKED), RateLimitedError, DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function verifyLink(body: unknown, auth: AuthContext): Promise<LinkResult> {
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const config = loadConfig();
  await rateLimit(`link_verify:ip:${auth.ip}`, config.RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN, MINUTE, auth.at);
  const request = parseVerifyBody(body);
  const userId = auth.session.userId;
  try {
    const challenge = await consumeChallenge({
      challengeId: request.challengeId,
      purpose: "link",
      userId,
      at: auth.at,
    });
    const address = checkChallenge(challenge, request.signature, config.SOLANA_NETWORKS);
    const { wallet, created } = await writeLink({
      address,
      network: challenge.network,
      provider: request.provider,
      auth,
    });
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "wallet_linked",
      severity: "info",
      userId,
      walletId: wallet._id,
      reason: null,
      at: auth.at,
    });
    return { wallet: toWalletView(wallet), created };
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof AuthFailedError || isWalletAlreadyLinked(error)) {
      const reason =
        error instanceof AuthFailedError ? toSecurityReason(error.reason) : "wallet_other_account";
      logger.info("link failed", { reason, cid: request.challengeId.toHexString() });
      await recordSecurityEvent({
        type: "link_failed",
        severity: "warning",
        userId,
        walletId: null,
        reason,
        at: auth.at,
      });
    }
    throw error;
  }
}

/**
 * DELETE /wallets/:id. Disconnects the wallet and revokes the sessions created with it. Refused
 * with LAST_WALLET when it would leave zero active wallets. The transaction first writes the users
 * document (to a value that always differs from the stored one), so two concurrent unlinks for
 * one user write-conflict and the loser is retried from a fresh snapshot (it then sees the
 * updated count). Unlinking an already-disconnected own wallet
 * is a 204 no-op. Disconnecting never affects onchain assets.
 * @throws NotFoundError (malformed, nonexistent or not owned), ConflictError(LAST_WALLET), DependencyUnavailableError
 */
export async function unlinkWallet(walletId: string, auth: AuthContext): Promise<UnlinkResult> {
  const id = parseObjectId(walletId);
  if (id === null) throw new NotFoundError();
  await ensureAuthReady();
  const userId = auth.session.userId;
  const at = auth.at;
  const { changed } = await withTransaction(
    async (session) => {
      // The guard: both concurrent unlinks of one user write this document, so the second one
      // write-conflicts and is retried from a fresh snapshot.
      const user = await users.findOne(safeEquals("_id", userId), { session });
      if (user === null) throw new NotFoundError();
      await users.updateOne(
        safeEquals("_id", userId),
        { $set: { updatedAt: strictlyAfter(at, user.updatedAt) } },
        { session },
      );
      const wallet = await findWalletForUser(id, userId, { session });
      if (wallet === null) throw new NotFoundError();
      if (wallet.status === "disconnected") return { changed: false };
      if ((await countActiveWallets(userId, { session })) <= 1) throw lastWallet();
      await setWalletStatus(id, "disconnected", at, { session });
      await sessions.updateMany(
        Object.assign({}, safeEquals("userId", userId), safeEquals("walletId", id), {
          revokedAt: null,
        }),
        { $set: { revokedAt: at } },
        { session },
      );
      return { changed: true };
    },
    { maxAttempts: UNLINK_MAX_ATTEMPTS },
  );
  if (changed) {
    await recordSecurityEvent({
      type: "wallet_unlinked",
      severity: "info",
      userId,
      walletId: id,
      reason: "wallet_unlinked",
      at,
    });
  }
  return { revokedCurrent: changed && auth.session.walletId.equals(id) };
}
```

import { verifyLink, withOrigin, withSession, type RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { readJsonBody } from "@/modules/security";
import { noStoreJson } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/** POST /api/v1/wallets/link/verify { challengeId, signature, provider? } -> 201 { wallet } (200 if it already existed) */
export const POST: RouteHandler = withOrigin(
  withSession(async (request, auth) => {
    const { wallet, created } = await verifyLink(await readJsonBody(request), auth);
    return noStoreJson({ wallet }, { status: created ? 201 : 200 });
  }),
);

```ts
import {
  clearSessionCookie,
  unlinkWallet,
  withOrigin,
  withSession,
  type RouteHandler,
} from "@/modules/auth";
import { loadConfig } from "@/modules/config";
import { noContent } from "@/modules/shared";

export const runtime = "nodejs";
export const dynamic = "force-dynamic";

/**
 * DELETE /api/v1/wallets/:id -> 204; 409 LAST_WALLET for the only active wallet; 404 if not
 * owned. Unlinking the wallet the current session was created with also clears the cookie.
 */
export const DELETE: RouteHandler<{ id: string }> = withOrigin(
  withSession(async (_request, auth, context) => {
    const { id } = await context.params;
    const { revokedCurrent } = await unlinkWallet(id, auth);
    return revokedCurrent
      ? noContent({ headers: { "Set-Cookie": clearSessionCookie(loadConfig().APP_ORIGIN) } })
      : noContent();
  }),
);
```

### Task 13: Cross-cutting tests: Origin, rate limits, the error table, best-effort events, logging and persistence

Spec §8 and §9 items 1 and 3, architecture §6.4–6.5. These tests exercise finished behaviour across every route, so they are expected to pass on first run: this task proves the cross-cutting guarantees rather than adding code. Each guarantee that could pass vacuously has a positive control inside the test (the leak detector is checked against a planted secret; the persistence scan asserts it found documents). If any test fails, that is a real defect: stop, use the systematic-debugging skill, and fix the code in the task that owns it (with a regression test there) rather than weakening this test.

- Create: `tests/helpers/logs.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/origin.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/rate-limits.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/errors.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/security-events.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/logging.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/persistence.test.ts`
- Test: `tests/integration/routes/v1/origin.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/rate-limits.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/errors.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/security-events.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/logging.test.ts`, `tests/integration/routes/v1/persistence.test.ts`

- Produces: `tests/helpers/logs.ts`: `interface CapturedLogs { lines(): string[]; restore(): void }`; `captureLogLines(): CapturedLogs` (sets `LOG_LEVEL=debug` in env and the logger, spies on `process.stdout.write`/`process.stderr.write`; `restore()` puts back the env and level `error`).

Create `tests/helpers/logs.ts`:

```ts
import { vi } from "vitest";
import { resetConfigForTests } from "@/modules/config";
import { setLogLevel } from "@/modules/shared";

export interface CapturedLogs {
  /** Every captured line, one JSON log line per entry. */
  lines(): string[];
  restore(): void;
}

/**
 * Captures every log line at debug level by spying on process.stdout/stderr.write. Sets
 * LOG_LEVEL=debug in the env too, so a config reload during the test keeps the debug level.
 */
export function captureLogLines(): CapturedLogs {
  const env = process.env as Record<string, string | undefined>;
  const previousLevel = env.LOG_LEVEL;
  env.LOG_LEVEL = "debug";
  resetConfigForTests();
  setLogLevel("debug");
  const chunks: string[] = [];
  const capture = (chunk: unknown): boolean => {
    chunks.push(String(chunk));
    return true;
  };
  const out = vi.spyOn(process.stdout, "write").mockImplementation(capture);
  const err = vi.spyOn(process.stderr, "write").mockImplementation(capture);
  return {
    lines: () =>
      chunks
        .join("")
        .split("\n")
        .filter((line) => line !== ""),
    restore() {
      out.mockRestore();
      err.mockRestore();
      if (previousLevel === undefined) delete env.LOG_LEVEL;
      else env.LOG_LEVEL = previousLevel;
      resetConfigForTests();
      setLogLevel("error");
    },
  };
}
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/origin.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as logoutRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import * as meRoute from "@/app/api/v1/me/route";
import * as revokeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/sessions/[id]/route";
import * as sessionsRoute from "@/app/api/v1/sessions/route";
import * as unlinkRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/[id]/route";
import * as linkChallengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge/route";
import * as linkVerifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/verify/route";
import * as walletsRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/route";
import type { RouteHandler } from "@/modules/auth";
import { signIn } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient, type CallOptions } from "../../../helpers/http";

const ORIGIN_REJECTED = { code: "ORIGIN_REJECTED", message: "Origin not allowed" };
const ID = "0123456789abcdef01234567";

interface NonGetRoute {
  readonly name: string;
  readonly handler: RouteHandler<{ id: string }> | RouteHandler;
  readonly options: CallOptions;
}

const NON_GET: readonly NonGetRoute[] = [
  {
    name: "POST /auth/challenge",
    handler: challengeRoute.POST,
    options: {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
      body: { address: "x", network: "devnet" },
    },
  },
  {
    name: "POST /auth/verify",
    handler: verifyRoute.POST,
    options: { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/auth/verify", body: {} },
  },
  {
    name: "POST /auth/logout",
    handler: logoutRoute.POST,
    options: { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/auth/logout" },
  },
  {
    name: "DELETE /sessions/:id",
    handler: revokeRoute.DELETE,
    options: { method: "DELETE", path: `/api/v1/sessions/${ID}`, params: { id: ID } },
  },
  {
    name: "POST /wallets/link/challenge",
    handler: linkChallengeRoute.POST,
    options: { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge", body: {} },
  },
  {
    name: "POST /wallets/link/verify",
    handler: linkVerifyRoute.POST,
    options: { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/verify", body: {} },
  },
  {
    name: "DELETE /wallets/:id",
    handler: unlinkRoute.DELETE,
    options: { method: "DELETE", path: `/api/v1/wallets/${ID}`, params: { id: ID } },
  },
];

async function signedInClient(): Promise<TestClient> {
  const client = new TestClient();
  await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
  return client;
}

function call(client: TestClient, route: NonGetRoute, origin: string | null | undefined) {
  return client.call(route.handler as RouteHandler<object>, { ...route.options, origin });
}

describe("Origin check on every non-GET route", () => {
  it.each(NON_GET)("$name: no Origin -> 403 ORIGIN_REJECTED", async (route) => {
    const result = await call(await signedInClient(), route, null);
    expect(result.status).toBe(403);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(ORIGIN_REJECTED);
  });

  it.each(NON_GET)("$name: foreign Origin -> 403 ORIGIN_REJECTED", async (route) => {
    const result = await call(await signedInClient(), route, "https://evil.test");
    expect(result.status).toBe(403);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(ORIGIN_REJECTED);
  });

  it.each(NON_GET)("$name: matching Origin -> not 403", async (route) => {
    const result = await call(await signedInClient(), route, undefined);
    expect(result.status).not.toBe(403);
  });

  it("rejects a foreign Origin before the session is checked (no cookie -> still 403)", async () => {
    const result = await new TestClient().call(logoutRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/logout",
      origin: "https://evil.test",
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(403);
  });

  it.each([
    { name: "GET /me", handler: meRoute.GET, path: "/api/v1/me" },
    { name: "GET /sessions", handler: sessionsRoute.GET, path: "/api/v1/sessions" },
    { name: "GET /wallets", handler: walletsRoute.GET, path: "/api/v1/wallets" },
  ])("$name needs no Origin", async ({ handler, path }) => {
    const client = await signedInClient();
    const result = await client.call(handler, { method: "GET", path, origin: null });
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/rate-limits.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import * as linkChallengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge/route";
import { signIn } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { useTestClock } from "../../../helpers/clock";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { useEnv } from "../../../helpers/env";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";

const RATE_LIMITED = { code: "RATE_LIMITED", message: "Too many requests" };

let restoreEnv: (() => void) | undefined;

afterEach(() => {
  restoreEnv?.();
  restoreEnv = undefined;
});

function challenge(client: TestClient, address: string, headers?: Record<string, string>) {
  return client.call(challengeRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
    body: { address, network: "devnet" },
    headers,
  });
}

describe("rate limits", () => {
  it("returns 429 with Retry-After on the (limit+1)th challenge per IP per minute", async () => {
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:15.000Z"));
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: "3" });
    const client = new TestClient();
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i += 1) {
      expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address)).status).toBe(200);
    }
    const limited = await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address);
    expect(limited.status).toBe(429);
    expect(limited.json).toEqual(RATE_LIMITED);
    expect(limited.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("45");
    expect(limited.headers.get("cache-control")).toBe("no-store");
  });

  it("counts malformed requests against the IP limit", async () => {
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T11:00:00.000Z"));
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: "2" });
    const client = new TestClient();
    expect((await challenge(client, "bad")).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await challenge(client, "bad")).status).toBe(400);
    expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address)).status).toBe(429);
  });

  it("limits challenges per address per hour", async () => {
    const clock = useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T12:00:00.000Z"));
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_HOUR_ADDRESS: "2" });
    const address = createTestWallet().address;
    const client = new TestClient();
    expect((await challenge(client, address)).status).toBe(200);
    clock.advance(61_000);
    expect((await challenge(client, address)).status).toBe(200);
    clock.advance(61_000);
    const limited = await challenge(client, address);
    expect(limited.status).toBe(429);
    const retryAfter = Number(limited.headers.get("retry-after"));
    expect(retryAfter).toBeGreaterThan(60);
    expect(retryAfter).toBeLessThanOrEqual(3600);
    expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address)).status).toBe(200);
  });

  it("limits verify per IP per minute", async () => {
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T13:00:00.000Z"));
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_VERIFY_PER_MIN: "2" });
    const client = new TestClient();
    const body = { challengeId: "0123456789abcdef01234567", signature: "x" };
    for (let i = 0; i < 2; i += 1) {
      const result = await client.call(verifyRoute.POST, {
        method: "POST",
        path: "/api/v1/auth/verify",
        body,
      });
      expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    }
    const limited = await client.call(verifyRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/verify",
      body,
    });
    expect(limited.status).toBe(429);
    expect(limited.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("60");
  });

  it("applies the same per-minute limit to the link challenge route under its own key", async () => {
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T14:00:00.000Z"));
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: "1" });
    const link = () =>
      client.call(linkChallengeRoute.POST, {
        method: "POST",
        path: "/api/v1/wallets/link/challenge",
        body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "devnet" },
      });
    expect((await link()).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await link()).status).toBe(429);
  });

  it("keeps separate buckets per X-Forwarded-For client when TRUST_PROXY=true", async () => {
    useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T15:00:00.000Z"));
    restoreEnv = useEnv({ RATE_LIMIT_CHALLENGE_PER_MIN: "1", TRUST_PROXY: "true" });
    const client = new TestClient();
    const a = { "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.1" };
    const b = { "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.2" };
    expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address, a)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address, b)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await challenge(client, createTestWallet().address, a)).status).toBe(429);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/errors.test.ts`:

```ts
import { afterEach, describe, expect, inject, it, vi } from "vitest";
import * as challengeRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/challenge/route";
import * as verifyRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/verify/route";
import * as meRoute from "@/app/api/v1/me/route";
import { getDb } from "@/modules/database";
import { sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";
import { logger } from "@/modules/shared";
import { requestChallenge, signIn } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";
import { startStandalone, useDatabaseUri } from "../../../helpers/mongo";

const VALIDATION_FAILED = { code: "VALIDATION_FAILED", message: "Invalid request" };

async function stoppedUri(): Promise<string> {
  const standalone = await startStandalone();
  const uri = standalone.getUri();
  await standalone.stop({ doCleanup: true });
  return uri;
}

afterEach(async () => {
  await useDatabaseUri(inject("mongoUri"));
});

function postChallenge(client: TestClient, options: { body?: unknown; rawBody?: string }) {
  return client.call(challengeRoute.POST, {
    method: "POST",
    path: "/api/v1/auth/challenge",
    ...options,
  });
}

function postVerify(client: TestClient, body: unknown) {
  return client.call(verifyRoute.POST, { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/auth/verify", body });
}

describe("400 VALIDATION_FAILED", () => {
  it.each([
    { label: "a 4097-byte body", rawBody: `"${"a".repeat(4095)}"` },
    { label: "a non-JSON body", rawBody: "{not json" },
    { label: "an empty body", rawBody: "" },
  ])("rejects $label", async ({ rawBody }) => {
    const result = await postChallenge(new TestClient(), { rawBody });
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(VALIDATION_FAILED);
  });

  it.each([
    {
      label: "an unknown key",
      body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "devnet", x: 1 },
    },
    { label: "a malformed address", body: { address: "0OIl", network: "devnet" } },
    { label: "a non-string network", body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: 1 } },
    { label: "an array body", body: [] },
  ])("rejects a challenge with $label", async ({ body }) => {
    const result = await postChallenge(new TestClient(), { body });
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    expect(result.json).toEqual(VALIDATION_FAILED);
  });

  it("rejects verify bodies with a malformed signature, provider or challengeId", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const challenge = await requestChallenge(client, wallet);
    const signature = wallet.sign(challenge.message);
    for (const body of [
      { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature: "0OIl" },
      { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature: signature.slice(0, 40) },
      { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature, provider: "Bad!" },
      { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature, provider: "x".repeat(33) },
      { challengeId: "not-an-object-id", signature },
      { challengeId: challenge.challengeId },
    ]) {
      const result = await postVerify(client, body);
      expect(result.status).toBe(400);
      expect(result.json).toEqual(VALIDATION_FAILED);
    }
    expect(
      (await postVerify(client, { challengeId: challenge.challengeId, signature })).status,
    ).toBe(200);
  });
});

describe("503 DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE", () => {
  it("returns 503 with Retry-After: 5 when the database is down", async () => {
    vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    await useDatabaseUri(await stoppedUri());
    const result = await postChallenge(new TestClient(), {
      body: { address: createTestWallet().address, network: "devnet" },
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(503);
    expect(result.headers.get("retry-after")).toBe("5");
    expect(result.json).toEqual({
      code: "DEPENDENCY_UNAVAILABLE",
      message: "Service temporarily unavailable",
    });
  });
});

describe("500 INTERNAL", () => {
  it("collapses a DATA_INTEGRITY failure to INTERNAL and logs the original code", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    await signIn(client, createTestWallet());
    await (
      await getDb()
    )
      .collection("sessions")
      .updateOne({ tokenHash: sha256Hex(client.cookie ?? "") }, { $set: { userAgent: 5 } });
    const error = vi.spyOn(logger, "error").mockImplementation(() => undefined);
    const result = await client.call(meRoute.GET, { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" });
    expect(result.status).toBe(500);
    expect(result.json).toEqual({ code: "INTERNAL", message: "Internal error" });
    expect(error).toHaveBeenCalledWith(
      "request failed",
      expect.objectContaining({ code: "DATA_INTEGRITY" }),
    );
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/logging.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as logoutRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route";
import * as meRoute from "@/app/api/v1/me/route";
import * as sessionsRoute from "@/app/api/v1/sessions/route";
import * as unlinkRoute from "@/app/api/v1/wallets/[id]/route";
import { parseSignInMessage } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { linkWallet, requestChallenge, signIn, verify } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { useTestClock } from "../../../helpers/clock";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";
import { captureLogLines } from "../../../helpers/logs";
import { sha256Hex } from "@/modules/security";

function leaks(lines: readonly string[], secrets: readonly string[]): string[] {
  return secrets.filter((secret) => lines.some((line) => line.includes(secret)));
}

describe("logs never contain credentials", () => {
  it("has a working leak detector (positive control)", () => {
    expect(leaks(['{"msg":"x secret-abc"}'], ["secret-abc", "other"])).toEqual(["secret-abc"]);
  });

  it("logs no token, token hash, nonce, signature or message across success and failure paths", async () => {
    const capture = captureLogLines();
    const secrets: string[] = [];
    try {
      const clock = useTestClock(new Date("2026-11-13T10:00:00.000Z"));
      const client = new TestClient();
      const wallet = createTestWallet();
      const signedIn = await signIn(client, wallet);
      const token = client.cookie ?? "";
      const nonce = parseSignInMessage(signedIn.challenge.message)?.nonce ?? "";
      secrets.push(
        token,
        sha256Hex(token),
        nonce,
        sha256Hex(nonce),
        signedIn.signature,
        signedIn.challenge.message,
      );

      // Failure paths: replay, foreign signature, tampered message, expired challenge.
      await verify(new TestClient(), signedIn.challenge, signedIn.signature);
      const second = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
      const forged = createTestWallet().sign(second.message);
      secrets.push(forged, second.message, parseSignInMessage(second.message)?.nonce ?? "");
      await verify(new TestClient(), second, forged);
      const third = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
      const tampered = wallet.sign(third.message.replace("solana:devnet", "solana:mainnet-beta"));
      secrets.push(tampered);
      await verify(new TestClient(), third, tampered);
      const fourth = await requestChallenge(new TestClient(), wallet);
      clock.advance(301_000);
      const late = wallet.sign(fourth.message);
      secrets.push(late);
      await verify(new TestClient(), fourth, late);

      // Session paths: slide, list, a 409, a forged cookie, logout.
      clock.advance(2 * 3_600_000);
      await client.call(meRoute.GET, { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" });
      await client.call(sessionsRoute.GET, { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/sessions" });
      await linkWallet(client, createTestWallet());
      await client.call(unlinkRoute.DELETE, {
        method: "DELETE",
        path: `/api/v1/wallets/${signedIn.walletId}`,
        params: { id: signedIn.walletId },
      });
      const forgedCookie = "F".repeat(43);
      secrets.push(forgedCookie);
      const stranger = new TestClient();
      stranger.setCookie(forgedCookie);
      await stranger.call(meRoute.GET, { method: "GET", path: "/api/v1/me" });
      await client.call(logoutRoute.POST, { method: "POST", path: "/api/v1/auth/logout" });
    } finally {
      capture.restore();
    }
    const lines = capture.lines();
    expect(lines.length).toBeGreaterThan(5);
    expect(lines.some((line) => line.includes('"reason":"signature_invalid"'))).toBe(true);
    expect(
      leaks(
        lines,
        secrets.filter((s) => s !== ""),
      ),
    ).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

Create `tests/integration/routes/v1/persistence.test.ts`:

```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from "vitest";
import * as logoutRoute from "@/app/api/v1/auth/logout/route";
import { getDb } from "@/modules/database";
import { parseSignInMessage } from "@/modules/wallet";
import { linkWallet, signIn } from "../../../helpers/auth";
import { createTestWallet } from "../../../helpers/ed25519";
import { TestClient } from "../../../helpers/http";

const COLLECTIONS = [
  "users",
  "wallets",
  "auth_challenges",
  "sessions",
  "rate_limits",
  "security_events",
] as const;

/** Every string value anywhere in a document, with its dotted path. */
function strings(value: unknown, path = ""): { path: string; value: string }[] {
  if (typeof value === "string") return [{ path, value }];
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return value.flatMap((item, i) => strings(item, `${path}.${i}`));
  if (value !== null && typeof value === "object" && !(value instanceof Date)) {
    if ("toHexString" in value) return [];
    return Object.entries(value).flatMap(([key, item]) =>
      strings(item, path === "" ? key : `${path}.${key}`),
    );
  }
  return [];
}

describe("acceptance criterion 3: nothing sensitive is persisted", () => {
  it("stores no raw token, signature, nonce field, IP or email after the happy path", async () => {
    const client = new TestClient();
    const wallet = createTestWallet();
    const signedIn = await signIn(client, wallet);
    const token = client.cookie ?? "";
    const nonce = parseSignInMessage(signedIn.challenge.message)?.nonce ?? "";
    await linkWallet(client, createTestWallet());
    await client.call(logoutRoute.POST, {
      method: "POST",
      path: "/api/v1/auth/logout",
      headers: { "x-forwarded-for": "203.0.113.77" },
    });
    expect(token).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/);
    expect(nonce).toMatch(/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{22}$/);

    const db = await getDb();
    const found: { collection: string; path: string; value: string }[] = [];
    for (const collection of COLLECTIONS) {
      for (const doc of await db.collection(collection).find({}).toArray()) {
        for (const entry of strings(doc)) found.push({ collection, ...entry });
      }
    }
    expect(found.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);

    const containing = (needle: string) => found.filter((f) => f.value.includes(needle));
    expect(containing(token)).toEqual([]);
    expect(containing(signedIn.signature)).toEqual([]);
    expect(found.filter((f) => f.value === nonce)).toEqual([]);
    // The nonce appears only inside the signed message of a consumed challenge.
    for (const hit of containing(nonce)) {
      expect(hit).toMatchObject({ collection: "auth_challenges", path: "message" });
    }
    const consumed = await db.collection("auth_challenges").find({ consumedAt: null }).toArray();
    expect(consumed.map((c) => c.message)).not.toContain(signedIn.challenge.message);
    expect(found.filter((f) => f.value === "unknown" || f.value.includes("203.0.113.77"))).toEqual(
      [],
    );
    expect(found.filter((f) => /\S+@\S+\.\S+/.test(f.value))).toEqual([]);
    for (const limit of await db.collection("rate_limits").find({}).toArray()) {
      expect(limit.key).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{64}$/);
    }
  });
});
```

### Task 14: ESLint scope note, auth boundary cases, ADR-0006 and the docs

Architecture §2.3 and spec §9 item 5. No ESLint scope object changes: S7/S8/S9 already give `auth` (and `wallet`, `security`) the full module rules, and a second scope for the same files would replace those rules. The boundary test locks that in, so it passes on first run. ADR-0006 records A1–A5, the message format, the session model and the mainnet-binding risk. README gains the eight config rows and an Auth API section; `docs/DATA_MODEL.md` and `docs/OPEN_DECISIONS.md` are updated with evidence.

**Files:**
- Create: `docs/adr/0006-wallet-signature-auth.md`
- Modify: `tests/unit/lint/boundaries.test.ts`, `eslint.config.mjs`, `README.md`, `docs/DATA_MODEL.md`, `docs/OPEN_DECISIONS.md`
- Test: `tests/unit/lint/boundaries.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the existing lint fixtures in `tests/fixtures/lint/` (no new fixture) and every earlier task (for the docs' evidence links).
- Produces: documentation only, plus seven boundary cases: `env-outside-config.ts`, `mongodb-outside-database.ts` and `relative-escape.ts` as `src/modules/auth/internal/__lint__.ts`; `module-imports-app.ts` as `src/modules/auth/__lint__.ts`; `nested-internal.ts` as `src/modules/auth/internal/sub/__lint__.ts`; `console-call.ts` as `src/modules/security/internal/__lint__.ts`; `deep-import.ts` as `src/app/api/v1/__lint__/deep.ts`.

Replace the whole of `tests/unit/lint/boundaries.test.ts` (an existing file) with:

```ts
import { readFile } from "node:fs/promises";
import path from "node:path";
import { fileURLToPath } from "node:url";
import { ESLint, type Linter } from "eslint";
import { beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from "vitest";

const repoRoot = fileURLToPath(new URL("../../../", import.meta.url));
const CHECKED_RULES = new Set([
  "no-restricted-imports",
  "no-restricted-syntax",
  "no-restricted-properties",
  "no-console",
]);

const MSG = {
  deep: "Import modules only via their public entry '@/modules/<name>'.",
  relIntoModules: "Use '@/modules/<name>', not relative paths into src/modules.",
  escape: "Relative imports must stay inside the module; use '@/modules/<name>'.",
  app: "src/modules/** must not import from src/app/**.",
  mongodb: "Only src/modules/database/** may import 'mongodb'.",
  env: "Only src/modules/config/** may read process.env.",
  flat: "internal/ must be flat; no subdirectories.",
  console: "Unexpected console statement.",
};

const IMPORTS = "no-restricted-imports";
const SYNTAX = "no-restricted-syntax";
const PROPS = "no-restricted-properties";
const CONSOLE = "no-console";

/** One expected violation: source line, rule id, and the message the config must produce. */
type Expected = readonly [line: number, ruleId: string, message: string];

let eslint: ESLint;

beforeAll(() => {
  eslint = new ESLint({
    cwd: repoRoot,
    overrideConfigFile: path.join(repoRoot, "eslint.config.mjs"),
  });
});

async function boundaryMessages(
  fixture: string,
  virtualPath: string,
): Promise<Linter.LintMessage[]> {
  const code = await readFile(path.join(repoRoot, "tests/fixtures/lint", fixture), "utf8");
  const [result] = await eslint.lintText(code, { filePath: path.join(repoRoot, virtualPath) });
  if (result === undefined) throw new Error(`no lint result for ${fixture}`);
  expect(result.messages.filter((m) => m.fatal === true)).toEqual([]);
  return result.messages.filter((m) => m.ruleId !== null && CHECKED_RULES.has(m.ruleId));
}

describe("module boundary lint rules", { timeout: 60_000 }, () => {
  it.each<{ fixture: string; virtualPath: string; expected: Expected[] }>([
    // --- original scopes ---
    {
      fixture: "deep-import.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/app/__lint__/deep-import.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.deep]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "relative-escape.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.escape]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "module-imports-app.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/shared/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.app]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "mongodb-outside-database.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/payments/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.mongodb],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.mongodb],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "mongodb-outside-database.ts",
      virtualPath: "tests/unit/__lint__.test.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.mongodb],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.mongodb],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "env-outside-config.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.env],
        [3, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
        [5, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "env-outside-config.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/app/__lint__/env.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.env],
        [3, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
        [5, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "nested-internal.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/sub/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, SYNTAX, MSG.flat]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "allowed-database.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/database/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [],
    },
    {
      fixture: "allowed-config.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/config/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [],
    },
    { fixture: "allowed-config.ts", virtualPath: "src/instrumentation.ts", expected: [] },

    // --- fix round: files outside the named scopes still get the rules ---
    {
      fixture: "unscoped-multi.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/lib/x.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.deep],
        [2, IMPORTS, MSG.mongodb],
        [3, PROPS, MSG.env],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "mongodb-outside-database.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/lib/x.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.mongodb],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.mongodb],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "tsx-deep.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/database/internal/__lint__.tsx",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.deep]],
    },

    // --- fix round: dynamic import() and require() ---
    {
      fixture: "dynamic-imports.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, SYNTAX, MSG.deep],
        [2, SYNTAX, MSG.app],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "require-calls.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, SYNTAX, MSG.deep],
        [2, SYNTAX, MSG.mongodb],
        [3, SYNTAX, MSG.app],
      ],
    },

    // --- fix round: the "./" prefix no longer hides a relative escape ---
    {
      fixture: "dot-escape-root.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.escape]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "dot-escape-internal.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.escape]],
    },

    // --- fix round: remaining rules pinned once each ---
    {
      fixture: "relative-into-modules.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/app/__lint__/rel.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.relIntoModules]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "console-call.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/wallet/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, CONSOLE, MSG.console]],
    },
    {
      // src/instrumentation-node.ts is under S1: it must NOT read process.env.
      fixture: "allowed-config.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/instrumentation-node.ts",
      expected: [[1, PROPS, MSG.env]],
    },

    // --- PR 02a: the auth module and the v1 route tree get the same rules (no new scope) ---
    {
      fixture: "env-outside-config.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/auth/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.env],
        [3, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, PROPS, MSG.env],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
        [5, SYNTAX, MSG.env],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "mongodb-outside-database.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/auth/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [
        [1, IMPORTS, MSG.mongodb],
        [4, SYNTAX, MSG.mongodb],
      ],
    },
    {
      fixture: "module-imports-app.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/auth/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.app]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "relative-escape.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/auth/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.escape]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "nested-internal.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/auth/internal/sub/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, SYNTAX, MSG.flat]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "console-call.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/modules/security/internal/__lint__.ts",
      expected: [[1, CONSOLE, MSG.console]],
    },
    {
      fixture: "deep-import.ts",
      virtualPath: "src/app/api/v1/__lint__/deep.ts",
      expected: [[1, IMPORTS, MSG.deep]],
    },
  ])("$fixture linted as $virtualPath", async ({ fixture, virtualPath, expected }) => {
    const messages = await boundaryMessages(fixture, virtualPath);

    // Exact set of (line, ruleId) pairs, so a violation cannot move to another rule or line.
    const key = (line: number, ruleId: string | null): string => `${line}:${ruleId}`;
    const actualKeys = [...new Set(messages.map((m) => key(m.line, m.ruleId)))].sort();
    expect(actualKeys).toEqual(
      [...new Set(expected.map(([line, ruleId]) => key(line, ruleId)))].sort(),
    );

    // Every message on a (line, ruleId) is the configured one (ESLint prefixes its own text).
    for (const [line, ruleId, message] of expected) {
      const forKey = messages.filter((m) => m.line === line && m.ruleId === ruleId);
      expect(forKey.length, `${line}:${ruleId} reported`).toBeGreaterThan(0);
      for (const m of forKey) expect(m.message.endsWith(message), m.message).toBe(true);
    }
  });
});
```
